            При указании элемента requestedExecutionLevel будет отключена виртуализация файлов и реестра.

// ФАЙЛ: MainWindow.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using Photino.NET;
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ZapretGUI.Core;

namespace ZapretGUI
{
    public class MainWindow
    {
        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);

        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);

        [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
        private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hWnd, uint Msg, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);

        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern uint GetDpiForWindow(IntPtr hwnd);

        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern uint GetDpiForSystem();

        private const int SW_HIDE = 0;
        private const int SW_SHOWNORMAL = 1;
        private const int SW_SHOWMINIMIZED = 2;
        private const int SW_RESTORE = 9;

        private const uint WM_SETICON = 0x0080;
        private static readonly IntPtr ICON_SMALL = new(0);
        private static readonly IntPtr ICON_BIG = new(1);

        private const int NormalLogicalWidth = 1100;
        private const int NormalLogicalHeight = 760;
        private const int CompactLogicalWidth = 850;
        private const int CompactLogicalHeight = 520;

        public PhotinoWindow Window { get; }
        private TrayIconManager? _trayIconManager;
        private WebBridge? _webBridge;
        private readonly bool _isFirstRun;
        private bool _isForceExiting = false;
        private bool _isWindowCreated = false;
        private CancellationTokenSource? _resizeAnimCts;

        // Безопасное получение дескриптора окна только после физического создания HWND
        public IntPtr WindowHandle
        {
            get
            {
                if (!_isWindowCreated || Window == null)
                    return IntPtr.Zero;
                try
                {
                    return Window.WindowHandle;
                }
                catch
                {
                    return IntPtr.Zero;
                }
            }
        }

        public MainWindow()
        {
            string webViewDataPath = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), AppConstants.AppRegistryName, "WebView2Cache");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(webViewDataPath);
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("WEBVIEW2_USER_DATA_FOLDER", webViewDataPath);

            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("WEBVIEW2_DEFAULT_BACKGROUND_COLOR", "FF0A0A0A");
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("WEBVIEW2_ADDITIONAL_BROWSER_ARGUMENTS", "--disable-pinch --force-device-scale-factor=1");

            var configPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "config.json");
            _isFirstRun = !File.Exists(configPath);

            SettingsManager.Load();
            UIHelper.ApplyThemeColors();

            var (_, initIcoPath) = UIHelper.GetOrCreateAppIcon(false);
            var initialSize = GetScaledWindowSize(SettingsManager.Current.CompactMode);

            Window = new PhotinoWindow()
                .SetTitle("Zapret for ADHD")
                .SetIconFile(initIcoPath)
                .SetUseOsDefaultSize(false)
                .SetSize(initialSize)
                .Center()
                .SetChromeless(true)
                .SetResizable(false)
                .SetTopMost(SettingsManager.Current.AlwaysOnTop)
                .SetContextMenuEnabled(false)
                .SetDevToolsEnabled(false);

            _webBridge = new WebBridge(this, _isFirstRun);

            Window.RegisterWebMessageReceivedHandler((sender, message) =>
            {
                _webBridge.HandleMessage(message);
            });

            Window.RegisterWindowCreatedHandler((sender, args) =>
            {
                _isWindowCreated = true;

                try
                {
                    var exactSize = GetScaledWindowSize(SettingsManager.Current.CompactMode);
                    Window.SetSize(exactSize);
                    Window.Center();
                }
                catch { }

                _trayIconManager = new TrayIconManager(this);
                RefreshAppIcon();

                if (SettingsManager.Current.StartMinimized)
                {
                    HideToTray();
                }

                _ = InitializeAppAsync();
            });

            Window.RegisterWindowClosingHandler((sender, args) =>
            {
                if (!_isForceExiting && SettingsManager.Current.MinimizeOnClose)
                {
                    HideToTray();
                    ShowNotification(
                        "Программа работает в фоне",
                        "Zapret свернут в системный трей. Дважды кликните по иконке в трее, чтобы открыть окно.");
                    return true;
                }

                CleanupOnExit();
                return false;
            });
        }

        private double GetDpiScale()
        {
            try
            {
                IntPtr hwnd = WindowHandle;
                uint dpi = hwnd != IntPtr.Zero
                    ? GetDpiForWindow(hwnd)
                    : GetDpiForSystem();

                if (dpi >= 96)
                    return dpi / 96.0;
            }
            catch { }

            return 1.0;
        }

        private Size GetScaledWindowSize(bool isCompact)
        {
            double scale = GetDpiScale();
            int baseW = isCompact ? CompactLogicalWidth : NormalLogicalWidth;
            int baseH = isCompact ? CompactLogicalHeight : NormalLogicalHeight;

            return new Size(
                (int)Math.Round(baseW * scale),
                (int)Math.Round(baseH * scale));
        }

        public void Run()
        {
            var baseDir = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
            var devWebRootDir = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(baseDir, @"..\..\..\wwwroot"));
            var localWebRootDir = Path.Combine(baseDir, "wwwroot");

            string webRootDir;
            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(devWebRootDir, "index.html")))
            {
                webRootDir = devWebRootDir;
            }
            else if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(localWebRootDir, "index.html")))
            {
                webRootDir = localWebRootDir;
            }
            else
            {
                webRootDir = ExtractEmbeddedWebUI();
            }

            string htmlPath = BuildBundledHtmlIfNeeded(webRootDir);
            Window.Load(htmlPath);
            Window.WaitForClose();
        }

        private static string BuildBundledHtmlIfNeeded(string webRootDir)
        {
            var indexPath = Path.Combine(webRootDir, "index.html");
            if (!File.Exists(indexPath)) return indexPath;

            try
            {
                var html = File.ReadAllText(indexPath);
                if (!html.Contains("<!-- @include "))
                    return indexPath;

                var partialsDir = Path.Combine(webRootDir, "partials");
                if (Directory.Exists(partialsDir))
                {
                    foreach (var file in Directory.GetFiles(partialsDir, "*.html"))
                    {
                        var fileName = Path.GetFileName(file);
                        var token = $"<!-- @include partials/{fileName} -->";
                        if (html.Contains(token))
                        {
                            html = html.Replace(token, File.ReadAllText(file));
                        }
                    }
                }

                var bundledPath = Path.Combine(webRootDir, "index.bundled.html");
                File.WriteAllText(bundledPath, html, System.Text.Encoding.UTF8);
                return bundledPath;
            }
            catch
            {
                return indexPath;
            }
        }

        private async Task InitializeAppAsync()
        {
            var modManager = new ModManager();
            modManager.InitializeFolders();
            await modManager.SyncActiveBatModsAsync();
            await modManager.ApplyListModsAsync();

            BypassController.Current.NetMonitor.Start();

            if (!_isFirstRun)
                _ = CheckUpdatesOnStartupAsync();

            if (!_isFirstRun && SettingsManager.Current.AutoStartBypass && !BypassController.Current.IsRunning)
            {
                try
                {
                    await BypassController.Current.StartServicesAsync(
                        SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName,
                        SettingsManager.Current.ZapretEnabled,
                        SettingsManager.Current.TgProxyEnabled);
                    UpdateIndicators(BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning, BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning);
                }
                catch { }
            }
        }

        private async Task CheckUpdatesOnStartupAsync()
        {
            var stopServicesAction = () =>
            {
                if (BypassController.Current.IsRunning)
                {
                    BypassController.Current.StopServices();
                    UpdateIndicators(false, false);
                }
            };
            var progress = new Progress<string>(status =>
                _webBridge?.SendEvent("log", new
                {
                    message = $"[Updater] {status}",
                    time = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss"),
                    isProgress = status.Contains("Скачивание:") || status.Contains("Скачано:")
                }));
            await UpdateManager.RunUpdateWorkflowAsync(isManualCheck: false, stopServicesAction, progress);
        }

        public void SetTaskbarIcon(Icon icon)
        {
            IntPtr hwnd = WindowHandle;
            if (hwnd == IntPtr.Zero) return;
            try
            {
                SendMessage(hwnd, WM_SETICON, ICON_SMALL, icon.Handle);
                SendMessage(hwnd, WM_SETICON, ICON_BIG, icon.Handle);
            }
            catch { }
        }

        public void MinimizeWindow()
        {
            IntPtr hwnd = WindowHandle;
            if (hwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
                ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_SHOWMINIMIZED);
        }

        public void HideToTray()
        {
            IntPtr hwnd = WindowHandle;
            if (hwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
                ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_HIDE);
        }

        public void RestoreFromTray()
        {
            IntPtr hwnd = WindowHandle;
            if (hwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_RESTORE);
                ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
                SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
            }
        }

        public void CloseWindow()
        {
            if (SettingsManager.Current.MinimizeOnClose && !_isForceExiting)
            {
                HideToTray();
                ShowNotification(
                    "Программа работает в фоне",
                    "Zapret свернут в системный трей. Дважды кликните по иконке в трее, чтобы открыть окно.");
            }
            else
            {
                ForceExit();
            }
        }

        public void ForceExit()
        {
            _isForceExiting = true;
            CleanupOnExit();
            try { Window.Close(); } catch { }
            Environment.Exit(0);
        }

        private void CleanupOnExit()
        {
            _resizeAnimCts?.Cancel();
            BypassController.Current.NetMonitor.Stop();
            if (SettingsManager.Current.StopServicesOnExit)
                BypassController.Current.StopServices();

            _webBridge?.Dispose();
            _trayIconManager?.Dispose();
        }

        public void InvokeOnUI(Action action)
        {
            if (!_isWindowCreated || Window == null)
                return;

            try
            {
                Window.Invoke(action);
            }
            catch { }
        }

        public void UpdateIndicators(bool isZapretRunning, bool isProxyRunning)
        {
            var anyRunning = isZapretRunning || isProxyRunning;
            _trayIconManager?.UpdateDynamicIcon(anyRunning);
            _webBridge?.SendEvent("bypassState", new
            {
                isRunning = anyRunning,
                zapretRunning = isZapretRunning,
                proxyRunning = isProxyRunning
            });
        }

        public void RefreshAppIcon()
        {
            _trayIconManager?.UpdateDynamicIcon(BypassController.Current.IsRunning);
        }

        public void UpdateNetworkIndicator(bool isOnline)
        {
            _webBridge?.SendEvent("netStatus", new { isOnline });
        }

        public void ShowNotification(string title, string message, ToolTipIcon icon = ToolTipIcon.Info)
        {
            _trayIconManager?.ShowNotification(title, message, icon);
        }

        public bool IsBypassRunning() => BypassController.Current.IsRunning;

        public async void ToggleBypass()
        {
            if (BypassController.Current.IsRunning)
                BypassController.Current.StopServices();
            else
                await BypassController.Current.StartServicesAsync(
                    SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName,
                    SettingsManager.Current.ZapretEnabled,
                    SettingsManager.Current.TgProxyEnabled);

            UpdateIndicators(BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning, BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning);
        }

        public async Task RestartBypassAsync()
        {
            var profile = SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName;
            await BypassController.Current.RestartServicesAsync(profile);
            UpdateIndicators(BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning, BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning);
        }

        public async Task SelectProfileFromTrayAsync(string profileName)
        {
            SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName = profileName;
            SettingsManager.Save();

            _webBridge?.SendEvent("profileSelected", new { profile = profileName });

            if (BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning)
            {
                await BypassController.Current.RestartServicesAsync(profileName);
                UpdateIndicators(BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning, BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning);
            }
        }

        public async void AnimateWindowSize(bool isCompact)
        {
            if (!_isWindowCreated) return;

            _resizeAnimCts?.Cancel();
            _resizeAnimCts = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var token = _resizeAnimCts.Token;

            Size targetSize = default;
            Size startSize = default;
            Point startLoc = default;
            int targetX = 0, targetY = 0;

            InvokeOnUI(() =>
            {
                targetSize = GetScaledWindowSize(isCompact);
                startSize = Window.Size;
                startLoc = Window.Location;

                var screen = WindowHandle != IntPtr.Zero
                    ? Screen.FromHandle(WindowHandle).WorkingArea
                    : Screen.PrimaryScreen!.WorkingArea;

                targetX = startLoc.X - (targetSize.Width - startSize.Width) / 2;
                targetY = startLoc.Y - (targetSize.Height - startSize.Height) / 2;
                targetX = Math.Max(screen.Left, Math.Min(targetX, screen.Right - targetSize.Width));
                targetY = Math.Max(screen.Top, Math.Min(targetY, screen.Bottom - targetSize.Height));
            });

            if (Math.Abs(startSize.Width - targetSize.Width) < 2 &&
                Math.Abs(startSize.Height - targetSize.Height) < 2)
            {
                return;
            }

            const int steps = 14;
            for (int i = 1; i <= steps; i++)
            {
                if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;

                double t = (double)i / steps;
                double ease = 1.0 - Math.Pow(1.0 - t, 3.0);

                int curW = (int)Math.Round(startSize.Width + (targetSize.Width - startSize.Width) * ease);
                int curH = (int)Math.Round(startSize.Height + (targetSize.Height - startSize.Height) * ease);
                int curX = (int)Math.Round(startLoc.X + (targetX - startLoc.X) * ease);
                int curY = (int)Math.Round(startLoc.Y + (targetY - startLoc.Y) * ease);

                InvokeOnUI(() =>
                {
                    Window.SetSize(new Size(curW, curH));
                    Window.SetLocation(new Point(curX, curY));
                });

                await Task.Delay(14);
            }
        }

        private string ExtractEmbeddedWebUI()
        {
            var tempDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "ZapretGUI_UI_" + AppConstants.AppVersion);
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(tempDir);
                using var stream = System.Reflection.Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("wwwroot.zip");
                if (stream != null)
                {
                    using var archive = new ZipArchive(stream);
                    archive.ExtractToDirectory(tempDir, overwriteFiles: true);
                }
            }
            catch { }
            return tempDir;
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Program.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ZapretGUI.Core;

namespace ZapretGUI
{
    internal static class Program
    {
        [STAThread]
        static void Main()
        {
            Application.SetHighDpiMode(HighDpiMode.PerMonitorV2);
            Application.EnableVisualStyles();
            Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);

            SettingsManager.Load();

            var mainWindow = new MainWindow();
            mainWindow.Run();
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: ZapretGUI.csproj
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

<Project Sdk="Microsoft.NET.Sdk">

	<PropertyGroup>
		<OutputType>WinExe</OutputType>
		<TargetFramework>net8.0-windows</TargetFramework>
		<RuntimeIdentifier>win-x64</RuntimeIdentifier>
		<UseWPF>false</UseWPF>
		<UseWindowsForms>true</UseWindowsForms>
		<Nullable>enable</Nullable>
		<ImplicitUsings>enable</ImplicitUsings>

		<!-- Метаданные исполняемого файла -->
		<AssemblyName>ZapretGUI</AssemblyName>
		<Product>Zapret for ADHD</Product>
		<Version>4.1.0</Version>
		<FileVersion>4.1.0.0</FileVersion>

		<!-- Настройки сборки в ОДИН чистый .exe -->
		<PublishSingleFile>true</PublishSingleFile>
		<SelfContained>false</SelfContained>
		<IncludeNativeLibrariesForSelfExtract>true</IncludeNativeLibrariesForSelfExtract>
		<EnableCompressionInSingleFile>false</EnableCompressionInSingleFile>
		<PublishTrimmed>false</PublishTrimmed>

		<!-- Отключаем генерацию лишних файлов (.pdb, .xml) в релизе -->
		<DebugType Condition="'$(Configuration)' == 'Release'">none</DebugType>
		<DebugSymbols Condition="'$(Configuration)' == 'Release'">false</DebugSymbols>
		<GenerateDocumentationFile>false</GenerateDocumentationFile>

		<ApplicationManifest>app.manifest</ApplicationManifest>
	</PropertyGroup>

	<!-- Photino.NET: нативный легковесный WebView2 хост -->
	<ItemGroup>
		<PackageReference Include="Photino.NET" Version="3.2.3" />
	</ItemGroup>

	<!-- Звук успеха встраиваем как ресурс напрямую внутрь .exe -->
	<ItemGroup>
		<EmbeddedResource Include="Assets\Sounds\success.wav" Condition="Exists('Assets\Sounds\success.wav')" LogicalName="success.wav" />
		<EmbeddedResource Include="Assets\success.wav" Condition="Exists('Assets\success.wav') And !Exists('Assets\Sounds\success.wav')" LogicalName="success.wav" />
	</ItemGroup>

	<!-- АВТОМАТИЧЕСКАЯ АРХИВАЦИЯ ИНТЕРФЕЙСА ПЕРЕД СБОРКОЙ -->
	<!-- Сохраняем zip в корень проекта, чтобы избежать проблем с правами и путями в obj/Release -->
	<Target Name="ZipWebUI" BeforeTargets="BeforeBuild">
		<MakeDir Directories="$(IntermediateOutputPath)" />
		<ZipDirectory SourceDirectory="$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\wwwroot" DestinationFile="$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\wwwroot.zip" Overwrite="true" />
		<ItemGroup>
			<EmbeddedResource Include="$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\wwwroot.zip" LogicalName="wwwroot.zip" />
		</ItemGroup>
	</Target>

	<!-- Запрещаем копирование исходных папок и временного архива в папку Publish (они уже внутри .exe) -->
	<ItemGroup>
		<Content Remove="wwwroot\**\*.*" />
		<None Include="wwwroot\**\*.*">
			<CopyToOutputDirectory>Never</CopyToOutputDirectory>
			<CopyToPublishDirectory>Never</CopyToPublishDirectory>
		</None>
		<None Remove="Assets\**\*.*" />
		<None Include="wwwroot.zip">
			<CopyToOutputDirectory>Never</CopyToOutputDirectory>
			<CopyToPublishDirectory>Never</CopyToPublishDirectory>
		</None>
	</ItemGroup>

	<!-- Копируем в Publish директорию ТОЛЬКО ядро (ZapretFiles) -->
	<ItemGroup>
		<None Include="ZapretFiles\**\*.*" Condition="Exists('ZapretFiles')">
			<CopyToOutputDirectory>PreserveNewest</CopyToOutputDirectory>
			<CopyToPublishDirectory>PreserveNewest</CopyToPublishDirectory>
		</None>
	</ItemGroup>

	<!-- УДАЛЯЕМ ВРЕМЕННЫЙ АРХИВ ПОСЛЕ УСПЕШНОЙ СБОРКИ -->
	<Target Name="CleanZipWebUI" AfterTargets="Build">
		<Delete Files="$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\wwwroot.zip" Condition="Exists('$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\wwwroot.zip')" />
	</Target>

</Project>

// ФАЙЛ: Core\AppConstants.cs

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class AppConstants
    {
        // Основные
        public const string AppVersion = "v4.1";
        public const string CoreFilesDirectory = "ZapretFiles";
        public const string ModsDirectory = "Mods";
        public const string ZapretProcessName = "winws";
        public const string TgProxyProcessName = "TgWsProxy_windows";
        public const string GithubUserAgent = "ZapretForADHD-App";
        public const string AppRegistryName = "ZapretForADHD";

        // GitHub Репозитории
        public const string RepoOwner = "NIK-master";
        public const string RepoName = "ZapretGUI";
        public const string ZapretCoreRepoUrl = "https://api.github.com/repos/flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/releases/latest";
        public const string TgProxyCoreRepoUrl = "https://api.github.com/repos/flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/releases/latest";

        // Списки IPSet и Hosts
        public const string FlowsealIpSetUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/refs/heads/main/.service/ipset-service.txt";
        public const string FlowsealHostsUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/refs/heads/main/.service/hosts";
        public const string V3nillaHostsUrl = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/V3nilla/IPSets-For-Bypass-in-Russia/refs/heads/main/%D0%A0%D0%B0%D0%B7%D0%B1%D0%BB%D0%BE%D0%BA%D0%B8%D1%80%D0%BE%D0%B2%D0%BA%D0%B0%20%D0%BC%D0%BD%D0%BE%D0%B6%D0%B5%D1%81%D1%82%D0%B2%D0%B0%20%D1%81%D0%B5%D1%80%D0%B2%D0%B8%D1%81%D0%BE%D0%B2(%D0%BF%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BC%D0%B5%D1%80%20-%20ChatGPT)/hosts";

        // Мониторинг сети
        public const string DefaultPingUrl = "https://dynamodb.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com";
        public const string AwsPingHost = "ec2.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com";
        public const int AwsPingPort = 443;
    }

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\AudioHelper.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class AudioHelper
    {
        [DllImport("winmm.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
        private static extern bool PlaySound(string pszSound, IntPtr hmod, uint fdwSound);

        private const uint SND_FILENAME = 0x00020000;
        private const uint SND_ASYNC = 0x0001;
        private const uint SND_NODEFAULT = 0x0002;

        private static readonly string? _successFilePath;

        static AudioHelper()
        {
            try
            {
                var cacheDir = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "Assets", "Cache", "Sounds");
                if (Directory.Exists(cacheDir))
                {
                    foreach (var oldClick in Directory.GetFiles(cacheDir, "*click*.wav"))
                    {
                        try { File.Delete(oldClick); } catch { }
                    }
                }

                _successFilePath = EnsureSuccessFileCached("success.wav");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка инициализации AudioHelper: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        private static string? EnsureSuccessFileCached(string fileName)
        {
            var baseDir = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
            var cacheDir = Path.Combine(baseDir, "Assets", "Cache", "Sounds");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(cacheDir);
            var targetCachedPath = Path.Combine(cacheDir, fileName);

            if (File.Exists(targetCachedPath) && new FileInfo(targetCachedPath).Length > 100)
                return targetCachedPath;

            string[] diskCandidates =
            [
                Path.Combine(baseDir, "Assets", "Sounds", fileName),
                Path.Combine(baseDir, "Assets", fileName),
                Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(baseDir, @"..\..\..\Assets\Sounds", fileName)),
                Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(baseDir, @"..\..\..\Assets", fileName))
            ];

            foreach (var candidate in diskCandidates)
            {
                if (File.Exists(candidate) && new FileInfo(candidate).Length > 100)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        File.Copy(candidate, targetCachedPath, overwrite: true);
                        return targetCachedPath;
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                        return candidate;
                    }
                }
            }

            try
            {
                var asm = System.Reflection.Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
                foreach (var resName in asm.GetManifestResourceNames())
                {
                    if (resName.EndsWith(fileName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        using var stream = asm.GetManifestResourceStream(resName);
                        if (stream != null)
                        {
                            using var fs = new FileStream(targetCachedPath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite);
                            stream.CopyTo(fs);
                            if (fs.Length > 100)
                                return targetCachedPath;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            GenerateSuccessWavToFile(targetCachedPath);
            return targetCachedPath;
        }

        public static void PlayClick()
        {
        }

        public static void PlaySuccess()
        {
            if (!SettingsManager.Current.SoundEffectsEnabled) return;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_successFilePath) || !File.Exists(_successFilePath)) return;

            try
            {
                PlaySound(_successFilePath, IntPtr.Zero, SND_FILENAME | SND_ASYNC | SND_NODEFAULT);
            }
            catch { }
        }

        private static void GenerateSuccessWavToFile(string filePath)
        {
            const int sampleRate = 44100;
            const int durationMs = 350;
            var numSamples = (sampleRate * durationMs) / 1000;
            var dataSize = numSamples * 2;

            using var fs = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite);
            using var bw = new BinaryWriter(fs, Encoding.ASCII);

            bw.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("RIFF"));
            bw.Write(36 + dataSize);
            bw.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("WAVEfmt "));
            bw.Write(16);
            bw.Write((short)1);
            bw.Write((short)1);
            bw.Write(sampleRate);
            bw.Write(sampleRate * 2);
            bw.Write((short)2);
            bw.Write((short)16);
            bw.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("data"));
            bw.Write(dataSize);

            for (var i = 0; i < numSamples; i++)
            {
                var t = (double)i / sampleRate;
                var progress = (double)i / numSamples;

                var freq = progress < 0.45 ? 523.25 : 783.99;
                var localP = progress < 0.45 ? (progress / 0.45) : ((progress - 0.45) / 0.55);
                var env = Math.Sin(Math.Min(1.0, localP * 8.0) * (Math.PI / 2)) * Math.Exp(-localP * 4.5);
                var sample = (Math.Sin(2 * Math.PI * freq * t) + 0.35 * Math.Sin(2 * Math.PI * (freq * 2) * t)) * env * 0.35;

                var pcm = (short)Math.Clamp(sample * short.MaxValue, short.MinValue, short.MaxValue);
                bw.Write(pcm);
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\BypassController.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class BypassController
    {
        public static BypassController Current { get; } = new BypassController();

        public ZapretManager Zapret { get; } = new ZapretManager();
        public TgProxyManager TgProxy { get; } = new TgProxyManager();
        public NetworkMonitor NetMonitor { get; } = new NetworkMonitor();
        public ZapretScanner Scanner { get; } = new ZapretScanner();

        private readonly List<IBypassService> _services;

        public bool IsRunning => Zapret.IsRunning || TgProxy.IsRunning;

        public event Action<string>? OnLog;

        private BypassController()
        {
            _services = new List<IBypassService> { Zapret, TgProxy };

            foreach (var service in _services)
            {
                service.LogMessage += msg => OnLog?.Invoke(msg);
            }
            Scanner.LogMessage += msg => OnLog?.Invoke(msg);
        }

        public async Task StartServicesAsync(string profileName, bool useZapret, bool useTgProxy)
        {
            if (useZapret)
            {
                OnLog?.Invoke($"[Zapret] Подготовка профиля {profileName}...");
                await Task.Delay(400);
                OnLog?.Invoke($"[Zapret] Запуск службы...");
                Zapret.Start(profileName);
            }

            if (useTgProxy)
            {
                OnLog?.Invoke("[TgWsProxy] Настройка маршрутов...");
                await Task.Delay(300);
                OnLog?.Invoke("[TgWsProxy] Запуск прокси...");
                TgProxy.Start();
            }
        }

        public void StopServices()
        {
            foreach (var service in _services)
            {
                service.Stop();
            }
            OnLog?.Invoke("🛑 Все модули остановлены.");
        }

        public async Task RestartServicesAsync(string profileName)
        {
            StopServices();
            await Task.Delay(1000);

            if (SettingsManager.Current.ZapretEnabled)
                Zapret.Start(profileName);

            if (SettingsManager.Current.TgProxyEnabled)
                TgProxy.Start();

            OnLog?.Invoke("✅ Службы успешно перезапущены.");
        }

        public async Task<long> CheckPingAsync()
        {
            var hostToPing = AppConstants.AwsPingHost;
            var portToPing = AppConstants.AwsPingPort;

            var rawInput = SettingsManager.Current.PingUrl?.Trim();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawInput))
            {
                var normalized = rawInput.Contains("://") ? rawInput : $"https://{rawInput}";

                if (Uri.TryCreate(normalized, UriKind.Absolute, out Uri? uri) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(uri.Host))
                {
                    hostToPing = uri.Host;
                    portToPing = uri.Port > 0 ? uri.Port : 443;
                }
                else
                    hostToPing = rawInput;
            }

            return await NetworkHelper.TcpPingAsync(hostToPing, portToPing);
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\DiagnosticsEngine.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.NetworkInformation;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class DiagnosticsEngine
    {
        private static readonly (int dcId, string ip)[] TelegramDcs =
        [
            (1, "149.154.175.50"),
            (2, "149.154.167.51"),
            (3, "149.154.175.100"),
            (4, "149.154.167.91"),
            (5, "91.108.56.130"),
        ];

        private static readonly int[] TelegramPorts = [443, 80, 5222];
        private static readonly string[] PingHosts = ["1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8", "77.88.8.8"];
        private const string DiscordHost = "162.159.130.234";
        private static readonly int[] DiscordUdpPorts = Enumerable.Range(50000, 11).ToArray();

        private static readonly (string label, string ip)[] DiscordPingHosts =
        [
            ("Gateway", "162.159.130.234"),
            ("Media", "162.159.129.235"),
            ("API", "162.159.128.233"),
        ];

        private static readonly Dictionary<string, string[]> AppSignatures = new()
        {
            ["telegram"] = ["telegram", "tgram", "gram", "aygram"],
            ["discord"] = ["discord", "cord"],
            ["zapret"] = [AppConstants.ZapretProcessName, "zapret", "winws"],
            ["tgwsproxy"] = [AppConstants.TgProxyProcessName.ToLower(), "tg_ws_proxy", "flowseal"],
        };

        private static readonly HttpClient _httpClient = new HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(12) };

        static DiagnosticsEngine()
        {
            _httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.UserAgent.ParseAdd("Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36");
        }

        public static async Task<(bool ok, double latencyMs, string error)> TcpConnectAsync(string ip, int port, double timeoutSec = 3.0)
        {
            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            try
            {
                using var tcp = new TcpClient();
                using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(timeoutSec));
                await tcp.ConnectAsync(ip, port, cts.Token);
                return (true, sw.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, "");
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { return (false, 0, "timeout"); }
            catch (SocketException e) when (e.SocketErrorCode == SocketError.ConnectionRefused) { return (false, 0, "refused"); }
            catch (Exception e) { return (false, 0, e.Message); }
        }

        private static async Task<string> TlsHandshakeAsync(string ip, string sni, double timeoutSec = 4.0)
        {
            try
            {
                using var tcp = new TcpClient();
                using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(timeoutSec));
                await tcp.ConnectAsync(ip, 443, cts.Token);

                using var ssl = new SslStream(tcp.GetStream(), false, (_, _, _, _) => true);
                await ssl.AuthenticateAsClientAsync(new SslClientAuthenticationOptions
                {
                    TargetHost = sni,
                    RemoteCertificateValidationCallback = (_, _, _, _) => true,
                }, cts.Token);
                return "ok";
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { return "timeout"; }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                var msg = e.Message.ToLower();
                if (msg.Contains("connection reset") || msg.Contains("forcibly closed") || msg.Contains("eof"))
                    return "rst";
                return $"error:{e.Message}";
            }
        }

        private static async Task<List<string>> DnsResolveAsync(string domain, string? server = null)
        {
            if (server is null)
            {
                try
                {
                    var entry = await Dns.GetHostEntryAsync(domain);
                    return entry.AddressList.Select(a => a.ToString()).Distinct().ToList();
                }
                catch { return []; }
            }

            try
            {
                var txId = (ushort)Random.Shared.Next(0, 65536);
                var query = BuildDnsQuery(domain, txId);
                using var udp = new UdpClient();
                udp.Client.ReceiveTimeout = 3000;
                var ep = new IPEndPoint(IPAddress.Parse(server), 53);
                await udp.SendAsync(query, ep);
                var result = await udp.ReceiveAsync();
                return ParseDnsResponse(result.Buffer);
            }
            catch { return []; }
        }

        private static byte[] BuildDnsQuery(string domain, ushort txId)
        {
            var buf = new List<byte>
            {
                (byte)(txId >> 8), (byte)(txId & 0xff),
                0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00
            };
            foreach (var part in domain.Split('.'))
            {
                buf.Add((byte)part.Length);
                buf.AddRange(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(part));
            }
            buf.Add(0);
            buf.AddRange(new byte[] { 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01 });
            return buf.ToArray();
        }

        private static List<string> ParseDnsResponse(byte[] data)
        {
            var ips = new List<string>();
            try
            {
                var ancount = (data[6] << 8) | data[7];
                if (ancount == 0) 
                    return ips;

                var i = 12;
                while (i < data.Length && data[i] != 0) 
                    i += data[i] + 1;

                i += 5;
                for (var a = 0; a < ancount && i + 10 < data.Length; a++)
                {
                    i += 2;
                    var rtype = (data[i] << 8) | data[i + 1];
                    var rdlen = (data[i + 8] << 8) | data[i + 9];
                    i += 10;
                    if (rtype == 1 && rdlen == 4 && i + 4 <= data.Length)
                        ips.Add($"{data[i]}.{data[i + 1]}.{data[i + 2]}.{data[i + 3]}");
                    i += rdlen;
                }
            }
            catch { }
            return ips;
        }

        private static async Task<(bool ok, double latencyMs)> PingOnceAsync(string host)
        {
            try
            {
                using var ping = new Ping();
                var reply = await ping.SendPingAsync(host, 2000);
                if (reply.Status == IPStatus.Success)
                    return (true, reply.RoundtripTime);
            }
            catch { }
            return (false, 0);
        }

        private static async Task<bool> UdpProbeAsync(string host, int port, int timeoutMs = 2000)
        {
            try
            {
                using var udp = new UdpClient();
                await udp.SendAsync(new byte[4], new IPEndPoint(IPAddress.Parse(host), port));
                using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource(timeoutMs);
                try
                {
                    await udp.ReceiveAsync(cts.Token);
                    return true;
                }
                catch (OperationCanceledException)
                {
                    return false;
                }
                catch (SocketException e) when (e.SocketErrorCode == SocketError.ConnectionReset)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                catch { return false; }
            }
            catch { return false; }
        }

        public static AppStatus CheckAppStatus()
        {
            var status = new AppStatus();
            Process[]? procs = null;
            try
            {
                procs = Process.GetProcesses();
                var names = procs
                    .Select(p => { try { return p.ProcessName.ToLowerInvariant(); } catch { return ""; } })
                    .Where(n => n.Length > 0)
                    .ToList();

                string Match(string key) =>
                    names.FirstOrDefault(n => AppSignatures[key].Any(s => n.Contains(s))) ?? "";

                var tg = Match("telegram"); if (tg != "") { status.TelegramRunning = true; status.TelegramProcName = tg; }
                var dc = Match("discord"); if (dc != "") { status.DiscordRunning = true; status.DiscordProcName = dc; }
                var zp = Match("zapret"); if (zp != "") { status.ZapretRunning = true; status.ZapretProcName = zp; }
                var tw = Match("tgwsproxy"); if (tw != "") { status.TgWsProxyRunning = true; status.TgWsProxyProcName = tw; }
            }
            catch { }
            finally
            {
                if (procs != null)
                {
                    foreach (var p in procs)
                    {
                        try { p.Dispose(); } catch { }
                    }
                }
            }
            return status;
        }

        public static async Task<List<DcResult>> CheckTelegramDcsAsync(Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var done = 0;
            var total = TelegramDcs.Length * TelegramPorts.Length;
            var tasks = (from dc in TelegramDcs
                         from port in TelegramPorts
                         select Task.Run(async () =>
                         {
                             var (ok, lat, err) = await TcpConnectAsync(dc.ip, port, 4.0);
                             Interlocked.Increment(ref done);
                             progress?.Invoke((double)done / total);
                             return new DcResult
                             {
                                 DcId = dc.dcId,
                                 Ip = dc.ip,
                                 Port = port,
                                 Ok = ok,
                                 LatencyMs = ok ? lat : null,
                                 Error = err
                             };
                         })).ToList();

            var results = await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
            return [.. results.OrderBy(r => r.DcId).ThenBy(r => r.Port)];
        }

        public static async Task<List<PingResult>> CheckPingAsync(Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var results = new List<PingResult>();
            for (var i = 0; i < PingHosts.Length; i++)
            {
                var (ok, rtt) = await PingOnceAsync(PingHosts[i]);
                results.Add(new PingResult
                {
                    Host = PingHosts[i],
                    Ok = ok,
                    LatencyMs = ok ? rtt : null,
                    PacketLoss = ok ? 0 : 100
                });
                progress?.Invoke((double)(i + 1) / PingHosts.Length);
            }
            return results;
        }

        public static async Task<DnsResult> CheckDnsAsync(string domain = "telegram.org", Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var result = new DnsResult { Domain = domain };
            progress?.Invoke(0.1);
            result.SystemIps = await DnsResolveAsync(domain);
            progress?.Invoke(0.5);
            result.DohIps = await DnsResolveAsync(domain, "1.1.1.1");
            progress?.Invoke(0.9);
            if (result.SystemIps.Count > 0 && result.DohIps.Count > 0)
            {
                if (!result.SystemIps.Intersect(result.DohIps).Any())
                    result.Spoofed = true;
            }
            else if (result.SystemIps.Count == 0) 
                result.Spoofed = true;
            else if (result.DohIps.Count == 0) 
                result.Error = "1.1.1.1 недоступен";
            progress?.Invoke(1.0);
            return result;
        }

        public static async Task<DpiResult> CheckDpiAsync(Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var result = new DpiResult();
            const string targetIp = "149.154.167.51";
            progress?.Invoke(0.1);
            result.SniTelegram = await TlsHandshakeAsync(targetIp, "telegram.org", 5.0);
            progress?.Invoke(0.5);
            result.SniNeutral = await TlsHandshakeAsync(targetIp, "example.com", 5.0);
            progress?.Invoke(0.9);
            if (result.SniTelegram is "rst" or "timeout" && result.SniNeutral != "rst")
                result.DpiDetected = true;
            progress?.Invoke(1.0);
            return result;
        }

        public static async Task<MediaThrottleResult> CheckMediaThrottleAsync(Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var result = new MediaThrottleResult();
            string[] urls =
            [
                "https://cdn1.telegram.org/file/811140210/1/iQITMsUeOrg/0c6eeff1e0b33e29c0",
                "https://cdn4.telegram.org/file/811140210/1/iQITMsUeOrg/0c6eeff1e0b33e29c0",
                "https://web.telegram.org/a/runtime.js",
            ];
            progress?.Invoke(0.1);

            foreach (var url in urls)
            {
                try
                {
                    var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                    var data = await _httpClient.GetByteArrayAsync(url);
                    sw.Stop();
                    var sizeKb = data.Length / 1024.0;
                    if (sizeKb < 5) continue;
                    var elapsed = Math.Max(sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, 0.001);
                    result.SpeedKbps = sizeKb / elapsed * 8;
                    result.Throttled = result.SpeedKbps < MediaThrottleResult.SlowThreshold;
                    progress?.Invoke(1.0);
                    return result;
                }
                catch (Exception e) { result.Error = e.Message; }
            }
            progress?.Invoke(1.0);
            return result;
        }

        public static async Task<UdpResult> CheckDiscordUdpAsync(Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var result = new UdpResult();
            var tested = DiscordUdpPorts.Take(5).ToList();
            result.TestedPorts = tested;
            var done = 0;

            var tasks = tested.Select(async port =>
            {
                var open = await UdpProbeAsync(DiscordHost, port);
                Interlocked.Increment(ref done);
                progress?.Invoke((double)done / tested.Count);
                return (port, open);
            });

            var results = await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
            result.OpenPorts = results.Where(r => r.open).Select(r => r.port).ToList();
            result.Blocked = result.OpenPorts.Count == 0;
            return result;
        }

        public static async Task<List<DiscordPingResult>> CheckDiscordPingAsync(Action<double>? progress = null)
        {
            var results = new List<DiscordPingResult>();
            for (var i = 0; i < DiscordPingHosts.Length; i++)
            {
                var (label, ip) = DiscordPingHosts[i];
                var (ok, lat, err) = await TcpConnectAsync(ip, 443, 4.0);
                results.Add(new DiscordPingResult
                {
                    Label = label,
                    Ip = ip,
                    Ok = ok,
                    LatencyMs = ok ? lat : null,
                    Error = err
                });
                progress?.Invoke((double)(i + 1) / DiscordPingHosts.Length);
            }
            return results;
        }

        public static List<BlockType> ClassifyBlocks(DiagReport r)
        {
            var blocks = new HashSet<BlockType>();
            var dcOk = r.DcResults.Where(x => x.Ok).ToList();
            var pingOk = r.PingResults.Where(x => x.Ok).ToList();

            if (r.DnsResult?.Spoofed == true) 
                blocks.Add(BlockType.DnsSpoof);

            if (r.DpiResult?.DpiDetected == true) 
                blocks.Add(BlockType.SniBlock);

            if (dcOk.Count == 0 && pingOk.Count > 0 && !blocks.Contains(BlockType.SniBlock))
                blocks.Add(BlockType.IpBlock);

            if (dcOk.Count > 0 && pingOk.Count > 0)
            {
                var withLat = dcOk.Where(x => x.LatencyMs.HasValue).ToList();
                if (withLat.Count > 0)
                {
                    var avgDc = withLat.Average(x => x.LatencyMs!.Value);
                    var pingWithLat = pingOk.Where(x => x.LatencyMs.HasValue).ToList();
                    if (pingWithLat.Count > 0)
                    {
                        var avgPing = pingWithLat.Average(x => x.LatencyMs!.Value);
                        if ((avgDc > 150 && avgDc > avgPing * 3) || avgDc > 250)
                            blocks.Add(BlockType.Throttling);
                    }
                }
            }

            if (r.MediaResult?.Throttled == true) 
                blocks.Add(BlockType.MediaThrottle);

            return [.. blocks];
        }

        public static List<string> MakeRecommendations(DiagReport r)
        {
            var recs = new List<string>();
            var blocks = r.BlockTypes;
            var app = r.AppStatus ?? new AppStatus();
            var bypass = app.ZapretRunning;

            string BypassList()
            {
                var t = new List<string>();
                if (app.ZapretRunning) t.Add($"Zapret ({app.ZapretProcName})");
                return string.Join(", ", t);
            }

            if (blocks.Contains(BlockType.MediaThrottle))
            {
                var spd = r.MediaResult?.SpeedKbps > 0 ? $"{r.MediaResult.SpeedKbps:F0} kbps" : "не измерена";
                recs.Add($"⚠️  ТСПУ-ЗАМЕДЛЕНИЕ МЕДИА (РКН)\n    Измеренная скорость: {spd} (норма > 200 kbps)\n    Файлы и медиа грузятся медленно. Используйте Zapret или VPN.");
            }

            if (blocks.Contains(BlockType.SniBlock))
                recs.Add("🛡  SNI-БЛОКИРОВКА (DPI обрывает TLS по SNI)\n    Проверьте, что Zapret запущен с нужным профилем.");

            if (blocks.Contains(BlockType.IpBlock))
                recs.Add("📡  IP-БЛОКИРОВКА (все DC недоступны)\n    Требуется VPN или WARP.");

            if (blocks.Contains(BlockType.DnsSpoof))
                recs.Add("🔵  DNS-СПУФИНГ\n    Включите зашифрованный DNS в Windows (1.1.1.1).");

            if (r.DiscordPing != null && r.DiscordPing.Count > 0 && r.DiscordPing.All(p => !p.Ok))
                recs.Add("🔴  DISCORD API: Недоступен!\n    Текстовые чаты и авторизация не будут работать без обхода.");

            if (r.UdpResult?.Blocked == true)
                recs.Add(bypass
                    ? $"🎮  Discord UDP: заблокирован, НО {BypassList()} активен, Discord работает! ✓"
                    : "🎮  Discord UDP заблокирован. Звонки не работают без Zapret.");
            else
                recs.Add("🎮  Discord UDP: доступен ✓, голос работает без дополнительных настроек.");

            return recs;
        }

        public static async Task<DiagReport> RunFullDiagnosticsAsync(Action<double, string>? progress = null)
        {
            void Step(double r, string lbl) => progress?.Invoke(r, lbl);
            var report = new DiagReport();

            Step(0.00, "Определение процессов...");
            report.AppStatus = await Task.Run(CheckAppStatus);

            Step(0.05, "Проверка базового пинга...");
            report.PingResults = await CheckPingAsync(p => Step(0.05 + p * 0.05, "Пинг узлов..."));

            Step(0.10, "Проверка DC Telegram...");
            report.DcResults = await CheckTelegramDcsAsync(p => Step(0.10 + p * 0.20, "DC Telegram..."));

            Step(0.30, "DNS и DPI...");
            report.DnsResult = await CheckDnsAsync(progress: p => Step(0.30 + p * 0.10, "DNS..."));
            report.DpiResult = await CheckDpiAsync(p => Step(0.40 + p * 0.15, "DPI/SNI..."));

            Step(0.55, "Скорость медиа (ТСПУ)...");
            report.MediaResult = await CheckMediaThrottleAsync(p => Step(0.55 + p * 0.15, "Медиа..."));

            Step(0.70, "UDP порты Discord...");
            report.UdpResult = await CheckDiscordUdpAsync(p => Step(0.70 + p * 0.10, "UDP..."));

            Step(0.80, "Пинг серверов Discord...");
            report.DiscordPing = await CheckDiscordPingAsync(p => Step(0.80 + p * 0.10, "Пинг Discord..."));

            Step(0.90, "Анализ данных...");
            report.BlockTypes = ClassifyBlocks(report);
            report.Recommendations = MakeRecommendations(report);

            Step(1.0, "Готово ✓");
            return report;
        }
        public static async Task<SiteCheckResult> AnalyzeCustomSiteAsync(string rawInput)
        {
            var clean = (rawInput ?? "").Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
            if (clean.StartsWith("http://")) clean = clean.Substring(7);
            else if (clean.StartsWith("https://")) clean = clean.Substring(8);
            if (clean.StartsWith("www.")) clean = clean.Substring(4);

            var slashIdx = clean.IndexOf('/');
            if (slashIdx > 0) clean = clean.Substring(0, slashIdx);
            var colonIdx = clean.IndexOf(':');
            if (colonIdx > 0) clean = clean.Substring(0, colonIdx);

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clean) || !clean.Contains('.'))
                throw new Exception("Введите корректный домен или ссылку (например: youtube.com или rutracker.org).");

            var res = new SiteCheckResult { Domain = clean };

            // 1. Проверяем DNS (системный vs 1.1.1.1)
            var dns = await CheckDnsAsync(clean);
            res.DnsSpoofed = dns.Spoofed;
            res.CleanIp = dns.DohIps.FirstOrDefault() ?? dns.SystemIps.FirstOrDefault() ?? "";

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(res.CleanIp))
            {
                res.VerdictType = "offline";
                res.VerdictTitle = "Домен не найден в DNS";
                res.VerdictDetail = $"Не удалось разрешить IP-адрес для «{clean}» даже через независимый DNS 1.1.1.1. Проверьте правильность написания адреса.";
                return res;
            }

            // 2. Проверяем прямое TCP-соединение на 443 порт чистого IP
            var (tcpOk, latMs, _) = await TcpConnectAsync(res.CleanIp, 443, 3.5);
            res.TcpOk = tcpOk;
            res.LatencyMs = Math.Round(latMs);

            if (!tcpOk)
            {
                res.VerdictType = "ip_block";
                res.VerdictTitle = $"Жесткая блокировка по IP ({res.CleanIp})";
                res.VerdictDetail = "Провайдер или ТСПУ полностью отбрасывает пакеты к IP-адресу сервера (Blackhole / фильтрация маршрута). Обход заголовков Zapret здесь бессилен — поможет только туннелирование (VPN / Прокси / WARP).";
                return res;
            }

            // 3. Проверяем TLS Handshake с целевым SNI и нейтральным SNI
            res.TlsTargetSni = await TlsHandshakeAsync(res.CleanIp, clean, 4.0);
            res.TlsNeutralSni = await TlsHandshakeAsync(res.CleanIp, "example.com", 4.0);

            bool sniBlocked = (res.TlsTargetSni is "rst" or "timeout") && res.TlsNeutralSni != "rst";

            if (res.DnsSpoofed && sniBlocked)
            {
                res.VerdictType = "sni_block";
                res.VerdictTitle = "Комбинированная блокировка (DNS + TLS SNI)";
                res.VerdictDetail = $"Провайдер подменяет DNS и режет соединение по SNI «{clean}». Добавьте домен в список обхода Zapret и пропишите чистый IP ({res.CleanIp}) в Hosts.";
            }
            else if (res.DnsSpoofed)
            {
                res.VerdictType = "dns_spoof";
                res.VerdictTitle = $"Обнаружена подмена DNS (Чистый IP: {res.CleanIp})";
                res.VerdictDetail = $"Системный DNS возвращает неверный или заглушечный адрес, но сам сервер {res.CleanIp} открыт ({res.LatencyMs} мс). Пропишите чистый IP в системный файл Hosts в 1 клик!";
            }
            else if (sniBlocked || res.TlsTargetSni != "ok")
            {
                res.VerdictType = "sni_block";
                res.VerdictTitle = $"Сброс по TLS SNI (ТСПУ / DPI блокировка)";
                res.VerdictDetail = $"TCP-соединение до {res.CleanIp} проходит ({res.LatencyMs} мс), но оборудование провайдера разрывает TLS-рукопожатие при виде заголовка «{clean}». Добавьте этот домен в белый список обхода Zapret!";
            }
            else
            {
                res.VerdictType = "ok";
                res.VerdictTitle = $"Сайт «{clean}» полностью доступен ({res.LatencyMs} мс)";
                res.VerdictDetail = $"DNS чистый ({res.CleanIp}), TCP и защищенное TLS-рукопожатие проходят без вмешательства ТСПУ.";
            }

            return res;
        }
    }
}

// ФАЙЛ: Core\DiagnosticsModels.cs

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public enum BlockType { None, Throttling, IpBlock, SniBlock, DnsSpoof, MediaThrottle }

    public class DcResult
    {
        public int DcId { get; set; }
        public string Ip { get; set; } = "";
        public int Port { get; set; }
        public bool Ok { get; set; }
        public double? LatencyMs { get; set; }
        public bool IsLoading { get; set; } = false;
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";

        public string Title => $"DC {DcId} :{Port}";
        public string PingText => Ok && LatencyMs.HasValue ? $"{LatencyMs.Value:0} мс" : "Ошибка";
    }

    public class PingResult
    {
        public string Host { get; set; } = "";
        public bool Ok { get; set; }
        public double? LatencyMs { get; set; }
        public double PacketLoss { get; set; } = 100;
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class DnsResult
    {
        public string Domain { get; set; } = "";
        public List<string> SystemIps { get; set; } = new();
        public List<string> DohIps { get; set; } = new();
        public bool Spoofed { get; set; }
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class DpiResult
    {
        public string SniTelegram { get; set; } = "unknown";
        public string SniNeutral { get; set; } = "unknown";
        public bool DpiDetected { get; set; }
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class UdpResult
    {
        public List<int> TestedPorts { get; set; } = new();
        public List<int> OpenPorts { get; set; } = new();
        public bool Blocked { get; set; } = true;
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class MediaThrottleResult
    {
        public double SpeedKbps { get; set; }
        public bool Throttled { get; set; }
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";
        public const double SlowThreshold = 200.0;
    }

    public class AppStatus
    {
        public bool TelegramRunning { get; set; }
        public string TelegramProcName { get; set; } = "";
        public bool DiscordRunning { get; set; }
        public string DiscordProcName { get; set; } = "";
        public bool ZapretRunning { get; set; }
        public string ZapretProcName { get; set; } = "";
        public bool TgWsProxyRunning { get; set; }
        public string TgWsProxyProcName { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class DiscordPingResult
    {
        public string Label { get; set; } = "";
        public string Ip { get; set; } = "";
        public bool Ok { get; set; }
        public double? LatencyMs { get; set; }
        public string Error { get; set; } = "";

        public bool IsLoading { get; set; } = false;
        public string Title => Label;
        public string PingText => Ok && LatencyMs.HasValue ? $"{LatencyMs.Value:0} мс" : "Ошибка";
    }

    public class DiagReport
    {
        public List<DcResult> DcResults { get; set; } = new();
        public List<PingResult> PingResults { get; set; } = new();
        public DnsResult? DnsResult { get; set; }
        public DpiResult? DpiResult { get; set; }
        public UdpResult? UdpResult { get; set; }
        public MediaThrottleResult? MediaResult { get; set; }
        public AppStatus? AppStatus { get; set; }
        public List<BlockType> BlockTypes { get; set; } = new();
        public List<string> Recommendations { get; set; } = new();
        public List<DiscordPingResult> DiscordPing { get; set; } = new();
    }

    public class SiteCheckResult
    {
        public string Domain { get; set; } = "";
        public string CleanIp { get; set; } = "";
        public bool DnsSpoofed { get; set; }
        public bool TcpOk { get; set; }
        public double LatencyMs { get; set; }
        public string TlsTargetSni { get; set; } = "";
        public string TlsNeutralSni { get; set; } = "";
        public string VerdictType { get; set; } = "ok"; // ok, sni_block, dns_spoof, ip_block, offline
        public string VerdictTitle { get; set; } = "";
        public string VerdictDetail { get; set; } = "";
    }
}
