////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ModManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class ModManager
    {
        private static readonly Regex _sanitizeIdRegex = new(@"[^a-z0-9_\-]", RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions _jsonOptions = new()
        {
            PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true,
            WriteIndented = true
        };

        private readonly string _modsBasePath;
        private readonly string _strategiesPath;
        private readonly string _listsPath;
        private readonly string _zapretFilesPath;

        public ModManager()
        {
            _modsBasePath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.ModsDirectory);
            _strategiesPath = Path.Combine(_modsBasePath, "strategies");
            _listsPath = Path.Combine(_modsBasePath, "lists");
            _zapretFilesPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory);
        }

        public void InitializeFolders()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_modsBasePath);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_strategiesPath);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_listsPath);
        }

        public bool IsModIdTaken(string id)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(id)) return true;
            InitializeFolders();

            var normId = SanitizeModId(id);
            if (Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(_strategiesPath, normId)) ||
                Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(_listsPath, normId)))
            {
                return true;
            }

            foreach (var dir in Directory.GetDirectories(_strategiesPath).Concat(Directory.GetDirectories(_listsPath)))
            {
                var jsonPath = Path.Combine(dir, "mod.json");
                if (File.Exists(jsonPath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var meta = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModMetaData>(File.ReadAllText(jsonPath), _jsonOptions);
                        if (meta != null && string.Equals(meta.UniqueId, normId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            return true;
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }

            return false;
        }

        public static string SanitizeModId(string raw)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw))
                return "";

            var clean = raw.Trim().ToLowerInvariant().Replace(" ", "_");
            clean = _sanitizeIdRegex.Replace(clean, "");
            return clean.Trim('_');
        }

        public string GenerateUniqueModId(string baseName = "mod")
        {
            var prefix = SanitizeModId(baseName);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(prefix))
                prefix = "mod";

            if (prefix.Length > 18)
                prefix = prefix.Substring(0, 18).TrimEnd('_');

            while (true)
            {
                var shortHash = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 4);
                var candidate = $"{prefix}_{shortHash}";
                if (!IsModIdTaken(candidate))
                    return candidate;
            }
        }

        public async Task<ModMetaData> ImportModFromArchiveAsync(string archiveFilePath, string? fallbackName = null)
        {
            InitializeFolders();
            var tempDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "ZapretGUI_Mod_" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(tempDir);
                await Task.Run(() => ZipFile.ExtractToDirectory(archiveFilePath, tempDir));

                var jsonFiles = Directory.GetFiles(tempDir, "mod.json", SearchOption.AllDirectories);
                if (jsonFiles.Length == 0)
                    throw new Exception("В архиве не найден файл mod.json. Убедитесь, что это корректный мод.");

                var jsonPath = jsonFiles[0];
                var modRootPath = Path.GetDirectoryName(jsonPath)!;

                var meta = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModMetaData>(await File.ReadAllTextAsync(jsonPath), _jsonOptions)
                           ?? throw new Exception("Файл mod.json поврежден.");

                var baseCandidate = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(meta.UniqueId)
                    ? SanitizeModId(meta.UniqueId)
                    : SanitizeModId(meta.Name);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(baseCandidate))
                    baseCandidate = SanitizeModId(fallbackName ?? Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(archiveFilePath));

                var finalUniqueId = IsModIdTaken(baseCandidate)
                    ? GenerateUniqueModId(baseCandidate)
                    : baseCandidate;

                meta.UniqueId = finalUniqueId;
                await File.WriteAllTextAsync(jsonPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(meta, _jsonOptions));

                var folderType = meta.IsBatStrategy ? "strategies" : "lists";
                var destPath = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(_modsBasePath, folderType, finalUniqueId));
                var destRootWithSep = destPath + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
                Directory.CreateDirectory(destPath);

                await Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    foreach (var file in Directory.GetFiles(modRootPath, "*.*", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
                    {
                        var relativePath = Path.GetRelativePath(modRootPath, file);
                        var targetPath = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(destPath, relativePath));

                        // Защита от Zip-Slip
                        if (!targetPath.StartsWith(destRootWithSep, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            continue;

                        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(targetPath)!);
                        File.Copy(file, targetPath, true);
                    }
                });

                return meta;
            }
            finally
            {
                if (Directory.Exists(tempDir))
                    try { Directory.Delete(tempDir, true); } catch { }
            }
        }

        public async Task<List<UIModItem>> GetAvailableModsAsync(ModType type)
        {
            var mods = new List<UIModItem>();
            var targetFolder = type == ModType.BatStrategy ? _strategiesPath : _listsPath;

            if (!Directory.Exists(targetFolder))
                return mods;

            var activeList = type == ModType.BatStrategy
                ? SettingsManager.Current.ActiveBatMods
                : SettingsManager.Current.ActiveListMods;

            foreach (var dir in Directory.GetDirectories(targetFolder))
            {
                var modId = Path.GetFileName(dir);
                var jsonPath = Path.Combine(dir, "mod.json");

                if (File.Exists(jsonPath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var json = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(jsonPath);
                        var meta = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModMetaData>(json, _jsonOptions);

                        if (meta != null)
                        {
                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(meta.UniqueId))
                                meta.UniqueId = modId;

                            mods.Add(new UIModItem
                            {
                                Id = modId,
                                Type = type,
                                Meta = meta,
                                IsActive = activeList.Contains(modId)
                            });
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка чтения meta-данных мода {modId}: {ex.Message}");
                    }
                }
            }
            return mods;
        }

        public async Task ApplyListModsAsync()
        {
            var listsDir = Path.Combine(_zapretFilesPath, "lists");
            var targetFile = Path.Combine(listsDir, "list-general.txt");
            var backupBaseFile = Path.Combine(listsDir, "list-general.base.txt");

            if (!File.Exists(targetFile)) return;

            var allModDomains = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var activeModDomains = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            if (Directory.Exists(_listsPath))
            {
                foreach (var dir in Directory.GetDirectories(_listsPath))
                {
                    var modId = Path.GetFileName(dir);
                    var listPath = Path.Combine(dir, "list.txt");

                    if (File.Exists(listPath))
                    {
                        var lines = await File.ReadAllLinesAsync(listPath);
                        var domains = lines.Where(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l) && !l.TrimStart().StartsWith("#"))
                                           .Select(l => l.Trim());

                        foreach (var d in domains)
                        {
                            allModDomains.Add(d);
                            if (SettingsManager.Current.ActiveListMods.Contains(modId))
                                activeModDomains.Add(d);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!File.Exists(backupBaseFile))
            {
                var currentRaw = await File.ReadAllLinesAsync(targetFile);
                var baseLinesOnly = currentRaw.Where(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l))
                                              .Select(l => l.Trim())
                                              .Where(l => !allModDomains.Contains(l))
                                              .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                                              .ToList();
                await File.WriteAllLinesAsync(backupBaseFile, baseLinesOnly);
            }

            var baseLines = (await File.ReadAllLinesAsync(backupBaseFile))
                            .Where(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l))
                            .Select(l => l.Trim())
                            .ToList();

            baseLines.AddRange(activeModDomains);

            await File.WriteAllLinesAsync(targetFile, baseLines.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        }

        public async Task SyncActiveBatModsAsync()
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(_zapretFilesPath)) return;

            await Task.Run(() =>
            {
                foreach (var file in Directory.GetFiles(_zapretFilesPath, "mod_*.bat"))
                    try { File.Delete(file); } catch { }

                foreach (var modId in SettingsManager.Current.ActiveBatMods)
                {
                    var sourceBat = Path.Combine(_strategiesPath, modId, "strategy.bat");
                    if (File.Exists(sourceBat))
                    {
                        var destBat = Path.Combine(_zapretFilesPath, $"mod_{modId}.bat");
                        try { File.Copy(sourceBat, destBat, true); } catch { }
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        public (bool isValid, string errorMessage) ValidateBatMod(string modName, string batContent)
        {
            try
            {
                var lines = (batContent ?? "").Split(new[] { "\r\n", "\n" }, StringSplitOptions.None);
                ZapretManager.ParseAndValidateBatLines(lines, $"моде «{modName}»", _zapretFilesPath, checkReferencedFiles: true);
                return (true, "");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return (false, ex.Message);
            }
        }
        public async Task<(bool added, string modId)> AddCustomDomainToBypassAsync(string domain)
        {
            InitializeFolders();
            const string customModId = "user_custom_sites";
            var modDir = Path.Combine(_listsPath, customModId);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(modDir);

            var jsonPath = Path.Combine(modDir, "mod.json");
            if (!File.Exists(jsonPath))
            {
                var meta = new ModMetaData
                {
                    UniqueId = customModId,
                    Name = "Мои сайты (Быстрый фикс)",
                    Author = "Вы",
                    Version = "1.0",
                    Description = "Домены, добавленные через Умный анализатор блокировок в 1 клик.",
                    IsBatStrategy = false
                };
                await File.WriteAllTextAsync(jsonPath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(meta, _jsonOptions));
            }

            var listPath = Path.Combine(modDir, "list.txt");
            var existing = File.Exists(listPath)
                ? (await File.ReadAllLinesAsync(listPath)).Select(l => l.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()).Where(l => l.Length > 0).ToHashSet()
                : new HashSet<string>();

            string cleanDomain = domain.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
            bool isNew = existing.Add(cleanDomain);

            await File.WriteAllLinesAsync(listPath, existing.OrderBy(x => x));

            if (!SettingsManager.Current.ActiveListMods.Contains(customModId))
            {
                SettingsManager.Current.ActiveListMods.Add(customModId);
                SettingsManager.Save();
            }

            await ApplyListModsAsync();
            return (isNew, customModId);
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ModModels.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System.ComponentModel;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public enum ModType
    {
        BatStrategy,
        DomainList,
        Guide
    }

    public class ModMetaData
    {
        public string UniqueId { get; set; } = "";
        public string Name { get; set; } = "Неизвестный мод";
        public string Author { get; set; } = "Аноним";
        public string Version { get; set; } = "1.0";
        public string Description { get; set; } = "Описание отсутствует.";
        public bool IsBatStrategy { get; set; } = false;
    }

    public class UIModItem : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        public string Id { get; set; } = "";
        public ModType Type { get; set; }
        public ModMetaData Meta { get; set; } = new ModMetaData();

        private bool _isActive;
        public bool IsActive
        {
            get => _isActive;
            set
            {
                if (_isActive != value)
                {
                    _isActive = value;
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsActive));
                }
            }
        }

        protected void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName) =>
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\NetworkHelper.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class NetworkHelper
    {
        public static async Task<long> TcpPingAsync(string host, int port, int timeoutMs = 2000)
        {
            try
            {
                using var client = new TcpClient();
                using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource(timeoutMs);
                var stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();

                await client.ConnectAsync(host, port, cts.Token);
                stopwatch.Stop();

                return client.Connected ? stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds : -1;
            }
            catch
            {
                return -1;
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\NetworkMonitor.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Net.NetworkInformation;
using System.Timers;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class NetworkMonitor
    {
        private readonly System.Timers.Timer _networkTimer;
        private readonly Stopwatch _elapsedStopwatch = new Stopwatch();
        private long _lastBytesReceived = 0;
        private long _lastBytesSent = 0;
        private bool _wasNetworkAvailable = true;

        public event Action<double, double>? StatsUpdated;
        public event Action<bool>? StatusChanged;

        public NetworkMonitor()
        {
            _networkTimer = new System.Timers.Timer();
            UpdateIntervalFromSettings();
            _networkTimer.Elapsed += Timer_Tick;

            SettingsManager.SettingsSaved += UpdateIntervalFromSettings;
        }

        public void UpdateIntervalFromSettings()
        {
            int seconds = Math.Max(1, SettingsManager.Current.StatsUpdateInterval);
            _networkTimer.Interval = seconds * 1000;
        }

        public void Start()
        {
            UpdateIntervalFromSettings();
            _elapsedStopwatch.Restart();
            _networkTimer.Start();
        }

        public void Stop()
        {
            _networkTimer.Stop();
            _elapsedStopwatch.Stop();
        }

        private void Timer_Tick(object? sender, ElapsedEventArgs e)
        {
            var isAvailable = NetworkInterface.GetIsNetworkAvailable();

            if (isAvailable != _wasNetworkAvailable)
            {
                _wasNetworkAvailable = isAvailable;
                StatusChanged?.Invoke(isAvailable);
            }

            var currentReceived = 0L;
            var currentSent = 0L;

            var interfaces = NetworkInterface.GetAllNetworkInterfaces();
            foreach (var netInterface in interfaces)
            {
                if (netInterface.OperationalStatus == OperationalStatus.Up &&
                    netInterface.NetworkInterfaceType != NetworkInterfaceType.Loopback)
                {
                    var stats = netInterface.GetIPv4Statistics();
                    currentReceived += stats.BytesReceived;
                    currentSent += stats.BytesSent;
                }
            }

            var elapsedSeconds = Math.Max(0.1, _elapsedStopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds);
            _elapsedStopwatch.Restart();

            if (_lastBytesReceived != 0 && _lastBytesSent != 0)
            {
                var diffReceived = Math.Max(0L, currentReceived - _lastBytesReceived);
                var diffSent = Math.Max(0L, currentSent - _lastBytesSent);

                var mbpsReceived = (diffReceived * 8.0) / (1000000.0 * elapsedSeconds);
                var mbpsSent = (diffSent * 8.0) / (1000000.0 * elapsedSeconds);

                StatsUpdated?.Invoke(mbpsReceived, mbpsSent);
            }

            _lastBytesReceived = currentReceived;
            _lastBytesSent = currentSent;
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ProcessHelper.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class ProcessHelper
    {
        public static void KillProcessesByName(string processName)
        {
            var processes = Process.GetProcessesByName(processName);
            foreach (var process in processes)
            {
                try
                {
                    if (!process.HasExited)
                    {
                        process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                        process.WaitForExit(1500);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка при завершении процесса {processName}: {ex.Message}");
                }
                finally
                {
                    process.Dispose();
                }
            }
        }

        public static bool IsProcessRunning(string processName)
        {
            var processes = Process.GetProcessesByName(processName);
            bool running = processes.Length > 0;
            foreach (var p in processes)
            {
                p.Dispose();
            }
            return running;
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ServiceToolsManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class ServiceToolsManager
    {
        private static readonly HttpClient _http = new HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15) };

        private const string HostsMarkerStart = "# >>> ZAPRET FOR ADHD HOSTS START >>>";
        private const string HostsMarkerEnd = "# <<< ZAPRET FOR ADHD HOSTS END <<<";

        static ServiceToolsManager()
        {
            _http.DefaultRequestHeaders.UserAgent.ParseAdd(AppConstants.GithubUserAgent);
        }

        private static string ZapretBasePath => Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory);
        private static string ListsPath => Path.Combine(ZapretBasePath, "lists");
        private static string UtilsPath => Path.Combine(ZapretBasePath, "utils");

        public static bool ValidatePortRange(string? input, out string cleanRange)
        {
            cleanRange = "";
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input)) return false;

            string trimmed = input.Replace(" ", "");
            var parts = trimmed.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            if (parts.Length == 0) return false;

            foreach (var part in parts)
            {
                if (!Regex.IsMatch(part, @"^[1-9][0-9]*(?:-[1-9][0-9]*)?$"))
                    return false;

                var bounds = part.Split('-');
                if (!int.TryParse(bounds[0], out int start) || start < 1 || start > 65535)
                    return false;

                if (bounds.Length == 2)
                {
                    if (!int.TryParse(bounds[1], out int end) || end < 1 || end > 65535 || start > end)
                        return false;
                }
            }

            cleanRange = trimmed;
            return true;
        }

        public static (string gameFilter, string tcpFilter, string udpFilter) GetGameFilterSubstitutions()
        {
            string mode = (SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterMode ?? "disabled").ToLowerInvariant();
            string tcpRange = ValidatePortRange(SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterTcpRange, out var validTcp) ? validTcp : "1024-65535";
            string udpRange = ValidatePortRange(SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterUdpRange, out var validUdp) ? validUdp : "1024-65535";

            return mode switch
            {
                "all" => (tcpRange, tcpRange, udpRange),
                "tcp" => (tcpRange, tcpRange, "12"),
                "udp" => (udpRange, "12", udpRange),
                _ => ("12", "12", "12")
            };
        }

        public static string GetGameFilterDisplayStatus()
        {
            string mode = (SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterMode ?? "disabled").ToLowerInvariant();
            string tcp = SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterTcpRange;
            string udp = SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterUdpRange;

            return mode switch
            {
                "all" => $"TCP и UDP ({tcp})",
                "tcp" => $"Только TCP ({tcp})",
                "udp" => $"Только UDP ({udp})",
                _ => "Отключен"
            };
        }

        public static void SyncGameFilterFile()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!Directory.Exists(UtilsPath))
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(UtilsPath);

                string flagFile = Path.Combine(UtilsPath, "game_filter.enabled");
                string mode = (SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterMode ?? "disabled").ToLowerInvariant();

                if (mode == "disabled")
                {
                    if (File.Exists(flagFile))
                        File.Delete(flagFile);
                }
                else
                {
                    var lines = new[]
                    {
                        $"mode={mode}",
                        $"tcp={SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterTcpRange}",
                        $"udp={SettingsManager.Current.GameFilterUdpRange}"
                    };
                    File.WriteAllLines(flagFile, lines);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка синхронизации game_filter.enabled: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        public static (string mode, int count, string statusText) GetIpSetStatus()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!Directory.Exists(ListsPath)) Directory.CreateDirectory(ListsPath);
                string listFile = Path.Combine(ListsPath, "ipset-all.txt");

                if (!File.Exists(listFile))
                    return ("none", 0, "Файл не найден");

                var lines = File.ReadAllLines(listFile)
                                .Where(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l) && !l.TrimStart().StartsWith("#"))
                                .Select(l => l.Trim())
                                .ToList();

                if (lines.Count == 0)
                    return ("any", 0, "Весь трафик (Any • 0 IP)");

                if (lines.Count == 1 && lines[0] == "203.0.113.113/32")
                    return ("none", 0, "Отключен (Заглушка)");

                return ("loaded", lines.Count, $"Активен ({lines.Count} подсетей)");
            }
            catch
            {
                return ("none", 0, "Ошибка чтения");
            }
        }

        public static void SetIpSetMode(string targetMode)
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(ListsPath)) Directory.CreateDirectory(ListsPath);
            string listFile = Path.Combine(ListsPath, "ipset-all.txt");
            string backupFile = Path.Combine(ListsPath, "ipset-all.txt.backup");

            var (currentMode, _, _) = GetIpSetStatus();

            if (currentMode == "loaded" && File.Exists(listFile))
            {
                File.Copy(listFile, backupFile, overwrite: true);
            }

            if (targetMode == "none")
            {
                File.WriteAllText(listFile, "203.0.113.113/32\r\n");
            }
            else if (targetMode == "any")
            {
                File.WriteAllText(listFile, "");
            }
            else if (targetMode == "loaded")
            {
                if (File.Exists(backupFile))
                {
                    File.Copy(backupFile, listFile, overwrite: true);
                }
                else if (currentMode != "loaded")
                {
                    throw new FileNotFoundException("Резервная копия базы IPSet не найдена. Нажмите кнопку «Обновить IP», чтобы скачать свежий список.");
                }
            }
        }

        public static async Task<int> UpdateIpSetFromWebAsync()
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(ListsPath)) Directory.CreateDirectory(ListsPath);
            string listFile = Path.Combine(ListsPath, "ipset-all.txt");
            string backupFile = Path.Combine(ListsPath, "ipset-all.txt.backup");

            string content = await _http.GetStringAsync(AppConstants.FlowsealIpSetUrl);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(content))
                throw new Exception("Сервер вернул пустой список IPSet.");

            await File.WriteAllTextAsync(listFile, content, Encoding.UTF8);
            await File.WriteAllTextAsync(backupFile, content, Encoding.UTF8);

            int count = content.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
                               .Count(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l) && !l.TrimStart().StartsWith("#"));
            return count;
        }

        private static string SystemHostsPath => Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.System), @"drivers\etc\hosts");

        public static (bool isApplied, int lineCount) GetHostsStatus()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(SystemHostsPath)) return (false, 0);
                var text = File.ReadAllText(SystemHostsPath);

                int startIdx = text.IndexOf(HostsMarkerStart, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                int endIdx = text.IndexOf(HostsMarkerEnd, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                if (startIdx >= 0 && endIdx > startIdx)
                {
                    string block = text.Substring(startIdx + HostsMarkerStart.Length, endIdx - (startIdx + HostsMarkerStart.Length));
                    int count = block.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
                                     .Count(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l) && !l.TrimStart().StartsWith("#"));
                    return (true, count);
                }
                return (false, 0);
            }
            catch
            {
                return (false, 0);
            }
        }

        public static async Task<int> UpdateSystemHostsAsync(int sourceIndex)
        {
            var urls = new List<string>();
            if (sourceIndex == 0) 
                urls.Add(AppConstants.FlowsealHostsUrl);
            else if (sourceIndex == 1 || sourceIndex == 3) 
                urls.Add(AppConstants.V3nillaHostsUrl);
            else
            {
                urls.Add(AppConstants.FlowsealHostsUrl);
                urls.Add(AppConstants.V3nillaHostsUrl);
            }

            var includeAdBlockZeroIps = (sourceIndex == 3);

            var collectedLines = new List<string>();
            var seenEntries = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            foreach (var url in urls)
            {
                var raw = await _http.GetStringAsync($"{url}?t={Guid.NewGuid():N}");
                foreach (var line in raw.Split(new[] { "\r\n", "\n" }, StringSplitOptions.None))
                {
                    var trimmed = line.Trim();
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(trimmed)) continue;

                    if (!includeAdBlockZeroIps && trimmed.Contains("Блокировка рекламы, трекеров", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        break;

                    if (trimmed.StartsWith("#"))
                    {
                        collectedLines.Add(trimmed);
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (trimmed.Contains("localhost", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        continue;

                    if (!includeAdBlockZeroIps && trimmed.StartsWith("0.0.0.0"))
                        continue;

                    var parts = trimmed.Split(new[] { ' ', '\t' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                    if (parts.Length >= 2)
                    {
                        var normalizedEntry = $"{parts[0]} {parts[1]}";
                        if (seenEntries.Add(normalizedEntry))
                            collectedLines.Add(normalizedEntry);
                    }
                }
            }

            var activeDomainsCount = collectedLines.Count(l => !l.StartsWith("#"));
            if (activeDomainsCount == 0)
                throw new Exception("Не удалось получить ни одной записи из выбранного источника.");

            var backupPath = SystemHostsPath + ".zapret.bak";
            if (File.Exists(SystemHostsPath) && !File.Exists(backupPath))
                try { File.Copy(SystemHostsPath, backupPath, overwrite: false); } catch { }

            var existingContent = File.Exists(SystemHostsPath) ? await File.ReadAllTextAsync(SystemHostsPath) : "";
            var cleanBase = RemoveZapretBlock(existingContent).TrimEnd();

            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cleanBase))
            {
                sb.AppendLine(cleanBase);
                sb.AppendLine();
            }

            sb.AppendLine(HostsMarkerStart);
            sb.AppendLine($"# Обновлено: {DateTime.Now:dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm} (Активных записей: {activeDomainsCount})");
            foreach (var l in collectedLines)
                sb.AppendLine(l);
            sb.AppendLine(HostsMarkerEnd);

            await File.WriteAllTextAsync(SystemHostsPath, sb.ToString(), new UTF8Encoding(false));
            FlushDnsCache();

            return activeDomainsCount;
        }

        public static void ClearZapretHosts()
        {
            if (!File.Exists(SystemHostsPath)) return;

            var existingContent = File.ReadAllText(SystemHostsPath);
            var cleanBase = RemoveZapretBlock(existingContent).TrimEnd() + "\r\n";

            File.WriteAllText(SystemHostsPath, cleanBase, new UTF8Encoding(false));
            FlushDnsCache();
        }

        private static string RemoveZapretBlock(string content)
        {
            var startIdx = content.IndexOf(HostsMarkerStart, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var endIdx = content.IndexOf(HostsMarkerEnd, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            if (startIdx >= 0 && endIdx > startIdx)
            {
                var removeLength = (endIdx + HostsMarkerEnd.Length) - startIdx;
                return content.Remove(startIdx, removeLength);
            }
            return content;
        }

        private static void FlushDnsCache()
        {
            try
            {
                Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "ipconfig.exe",
                    Arguments = "/flushdns",
                    CreateNoWindow = true,
                    UseShellExecute = false
                })?.WaitForExit(3000);
            }
            catch { }
        }
        public static string UnloadWinDivertDriver()
        {
            BypassController.Current.StopServices();
            string[] driverNames = ["WinDivert", "WinDivert14", "WinDivert64", "Monkey"];
            int stopped = 0;

            foreach (var name in driverNames)
            {
                try
                {
                    using var p = Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "sc.exe",
                        Arguments = $"stop {name}",
                        CreateNoWindow = true,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true
                    });
                    p?.WaitForExit(2500);
                    if (p != null && p.ExitCode == 0)
                        stopped++;
                }
                catch { }
            }

            FlushDnsCache();
            return stopped > 0
                ? $"Драйвер WinDivert успешно остановлен и выгружен из ядра (служб остановлено: {stopped}), DNS-кэш очищен."
                : "Все процессы обхода остановлены, активных служб WinDivert в памяти не обнаружено. DNS-кэш очищен.";
        }
        public static async Task AddSingleHostEntryAsync(string domain, string ip)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(domain) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ip))
                throw new Exception("Некорректный домен или IP-адрес.");

            var existingContent = File.Exists(SystemHostsPath) ? await File.ReadAllTextAsync(SystemHostsPath) : "";
            var lines = existingContent.Split(new[] { "\r\n", "\n" }, StringSplitOptions.None).ToList();

            string rule = $"{ip.Trim()} {domain.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()}";
            if (!lines.Any(l => l.Trim().Equals(rule, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
            {
                lines.Add(rule);
                await File.WriteAllTextAsync(SystemHostsPath, string.Join("\r\n", lines).TrimEnd() + "\r\n", new UTF8Encoding(false));
                FlushDnsCache();
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class AppSettings
    {
        public bool ZapretEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool TgProxyEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public int SelectedProfileIndex { get; set; } = 0;
        public string SelectedProfileName { get; set; } = "general.bat";
        public bool StartMinimized { get; set; } = false;
        public bool MinimizeOnClose { get; set; } = true;
        public bool NotificationsEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool FocusMode { get; set; } = false;
        public bool CompactMode { get; set; } = false;
        public bool HardwareAcceleration { get; set; } = true;
        public string PingUrl { get; set; } = "https://dynamodb.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com";
        public bool AutoRestartServices { get; set; } = false;
        public int StatsUpdateInterval { get; set; } = 1;

        // Настройки v4.1
        public bool AutoStartBypass { get; set; } = false;
        public bool StopServicesOnExit { get; set; } = true;
        public bool SoundEffectsEnabled { get; set; } = true;
        public bool SkipSplashScreen { get; set; } = false;
        public bool AlwaysOnTop { get; set; } = false;
        public string AccentTheme { get; set; } = "green"; // green, blue, purple, amber, crimson
        public bool WatchdogPingAutoRestart { get; set; } = false;

        public string ZapretCoreVersion { get; set; } = "0.0.0";
        public string TgProxyCoreVersion { get; set; } = "0.0.0";

        public List<string> ActiveBatMods { get; set; } = new List<string>();
        public List<string> ActiveListMods { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        public string GameFilterMode { get; set; } = "disabled";
        public string GameFilterTcpRange { get; set; } = "1024-65535";
        public string GameFilterUdpRange { get; set; } = "1024-65535";
        public int HostsSourceIndex { get; set; } = 2;
        public bool FirstTimeScanPrompted { get; set; } = false;
    }

    public static class SettingsManager
    {
        private static readonly string _settingsPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "config.json");

        public static AppSettings Current { get; set; } = new AppSettings();

        public static event Action? SettingsSaved;

        public static void Load()
        {
            try
            {
                if (File.Exists(_settingsPath))
                {
                    var json = File.ReadAllText(_settingsPath);
                    var settings = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<AppSettings>(json);
                    if (settings != null)
                        Current = settings;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка загрузки настроек: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        public static void Save()
        {
            try
            {
                var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(Current, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true });
                File.WriteAllText(_settingsPath, json);

                SettingsSaved?.Invoke();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка сохранения настроек: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\StartupHelper.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class StartupHelper
    {
        private const string RegistryKeyPath = @"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run";
        private const string TaskName = AppConstants.AppRegistryName;

        public static bool IsAutoStartEnabled()
        {
            try
            {
                // 1. Проверяем наличие задачи в Планировщике Windows (основной способ для requireAdministrator)
                using var p = Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "schtasks.exe",
                    Arguments = $"/Query /TN \"{TaskName}\"",
                    CreateNoWindow = true,
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true
                });

                p?.WaitForExit(1500);
                if (p != null && p.ExitCode == 0)
                    return true;

                // 2. Фолбэк: проверяем реестр (на случай если запуск без админ-прав)
                using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryKeyPath, false);
                return key?.GetValue(AppConstants.AppRegistryName) != null;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка проверки автозапуска: {ex.Message}");
                return false;
            }
        }

        public static void SetAutoStart(bool enable)
        {
            try
            {
                var exePath = Environment.ProcessPath;
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(exePath))
                    exePath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "ZapretGUI.exe");

                // Всегда подчищаем старый ключ в Run, чтобы Windows не плодила дубликаты
                using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryKeyPath, true))
                {
                    key?.DeleteValue(AppConstants.AppRegistryName, false);
                }

                if (enable)
                {
                    // Создаем задачу при входе пользователя с наивысшими правами (/RL HIGHEST)
                    using var p = Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "schtasks.exe",
                        Arguments = $"/Create /F /RL HIGHEST /SC ONLOGON /TN \"{TaskName}\" /TR \"\\\"{exePath}\\\"\"",
                        CreateNoWindow = true,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true
                    });
                    p?.WaitForExit(2500);

                    // Если по какой-то причине служба планировщика отключена, пишем в реестр как резерв
                    if (p == null || p.ExitCode != 0)
                    {
                        using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryKeyPath, true);
                        key?.SetValue(AppConstants.AppRegistryName, $"\"{exePath}\"");
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    using var p = Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = "schtasks.exe",
                        Arguments = $"/Delete /F /TN \"{TaskName}\"",
                        CreateNoWindow = true,
                        UseShellExecute = false,
                        RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                        RedirectStandardError = true
                    });
                    p?.WaitForExit(2500);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка настройки автозапуска: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\TgProxyManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class TgProxyManager : IBypassService
    {
        private readonly string _proxyPath;
        private Process? _process;
        private DateTime _lastCheckTime = DateTime.MinValue;
        private bool _cachedRunning = false;

        public event Action<string>? LogMessage;

        public TgProxyManager()
        {
            _proxyPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory, $"{AppConstants.TgProxyProcessName}.exe");
        }

        public void Start(string profileName = "")
        {
            if (IsRunning)
                return;

            if (!File.Exists(_proxyPath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Файл прокси не найден: {_proxyPath}");

            var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
            {
                FileName = _proxyPath,
                UseShellExecute = false,
                CreateNoWindow = true,
                RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                RedirectStandardError = true
            };

            _process = new Process { StartInfo = startInfo };

            _process.OutputDataReceived += (s, e) => { if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Data)) LogMessage?.Invoke($"[TgWsProxy] {e.Data}"); };
            _process.ErrorDataReceived += (s, e) => { if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Data)) LogMessage?.Invoke($"[TgWsProxy ERR] {e.Data}"); };

            _process.Start();
            _process.BeginOutputReadLine();
            _process.BeginErrorReadLine();

            _cachedRunning = true;
            _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public void Stop()
        {
            try
            {
                if (_process != null)
                {
                    if (!_process.HasExited)
                        _process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                    _process.Dispose();
                    _process = null;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка остановки процесса TgProxy: {ex.Message}");
            }

            ProcessHelper.KillProcessesByName(AppConstants.TgProxyProcessName);
            _cachedRunning = false;
            _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public bool IsRunning
        {
            get
            {
                try
                {
                    if (_process != null && !_process.HasExited)
                        return true;
                }
                catch { }

                if ((DateTime.UtcNow - _lastCheckTime).TotalSeconds < 2.5)
                    return _cachedRunning;

                _cachedRunning = ProcessHelper.IsProcessRunning(AppConstants.TgProxyProcessName);
                _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
                return _cachedRunning;
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\TrayIconManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class TrayIconManager : IDisposable
    {
        private NotifyIcon? _notifyIcon;
        private CustomTrayPopupForm? _trayPopup;
        private readonly MainWindow _mainWindow;

        public TrayIconManager(MainWindow mainWindow)
        {
            _mainWindow = mainWindow;
            SetupTrayIcon();
        }

        private void SetupTrayIcon()
        {
            _trayPopup = new CustomTrayPopupForm(_mainWindow);

            _notifyIcon = new NotifyIcon
            {
                Text = $"Zapret for ADHD {AppConstants.AppVersion}",
                Visible = true
            };

            UpdateDynamicIcon(BypassController.Current.IsRunning);

            _notifyIcon.DoubleClick += (s, e) =>
            {
                _mainWindow.RestoreFromTray();
            };

            _notifyIcon.MouseClick += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.Button != MouseButtons.Left && e.Button != MouseButtons.Right)
                    return;

                _trayPopup.ShowAtCursor();
            };
        }

        public void UpdateDynamicIcon(bool isRunning)
        {
            try
            {
                var (trayIcon, icoFilePath) = UIHelper.GetOrCreateAppIcon(isRunning);

                if (_notifyIcon != null)
                {
                    _notifyIcon.Icon = trayIcon;
                    _notifyIcon.Text = isRunning
                        ? $"Zapret for ADHD ({SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName} — Активен)"
                        : $"Zapret for ADHD {AppConstants.AppVersion} (Остановлен)";
                }

                _mainWindow.InvokeOnUI(() =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        _mainWindow.Window.SetIconFile(icoFilePath);
                        _mainWindow.SetTaskbarIcon(trayIcon);
                    }
                    catch { }
                });
            }
            catch { }
        }

        public void ShowNotification(string title, string message, ToolTipIcon icon = ToolTipIcon.Info)
        {
            if (SettingsManager.Current.NotificationsEnabled && _notifyIcon != null && _notifyIcon.Visible)
                _notifyIcon.ShowBalloonTip(3000, title, message, icon);
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (_notifyIcon != null)
            {
                _notifyIcon.Visible = false;
                _notifyIcon.Dispose();
                _notifyIcon = null;
            }

            _trayPopup?.Dispose();
        }
    }

    internal sealed class CustomTrayPopupForm : Form
    {
        [DllImport("Gdi32.dll", EntryPoint = "CreateRoundRectRgn")]
        private static extern IntPtr CreateRoundRectRgn(int nLeftRect, int nTopRect, int nRightRect, int nBottomRect, int nWidthEllipse, int nHeightEllipse);

        [DllImport("Gdi32.dll")]
        private static extern bool DeleteObject(IntPtr hObject);

        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool GetCursorPos(out Point lpPoint);

        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);

        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern uint GetDpiForSystem();

        private const int BaseW = 308;
        private const int BaseH = 352;

        private readonly MainWindow _mainWindow;
        private double _rxMbps = 0;
        private double _txMbps = 0;
        private string _pingStr = "...";
        private Color _pingColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#4CAF50");
        private int _hoveredZone = -1;
        private float _scale = 1.0f;

        // Базовые координаты зон (умножаются на _scale при отрисовке и кликах)
        private Rectangle SRect(int x, int y, int w, int h) =>
            new((int)Math.Round(x * _scale), (int)Math.Round(y * _scale), (int)Math.Round(w * _scale), (int)Math.Round(h * _scale));

        private float S(float v) => v * _scale;
        private int SI(int v) => (int)Math.Round(v * _scale);

        public CustomTrayPopupForm(MainWindow mainWindow)
        {
            _mainWindow = mainWindow;
            AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.None;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.None;
            ShowInTaskbar = false;
            TopMost = true;
            DoubleBuffered = true;
            BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#121212");

            UpdateDpiMetrics();

            BypassController.Current.NetMonitor.StatsUpdated += OnStatsUpdated;
            Deactivate += (s, e) => Hide();
        }

        private void UpdateDpiMetrics()
        {
            try
            {
                uint dpi = DeviceDpi > 0 ? (uint)DeviceDpi : GetDpiForSystem();
                _scale = Math.Max(1.0f, dpi / 96.0f);
            }
            catch
            {
                _scale = 1.0f;
            }

            int w = SI(BaseW);
            int h = SI(BaseH);
            if (Width != w || Height != h)
            {
                Size = new Size(w, h);
                var oldRegion = Region;
                IntPtr hrgn = CreateRoundRectRgn(0, 0, w, h, SI(22), SI(22));
                try
                {
                    Region = Region.FromHrgn(hrgn);
                }
                finally
                {
                    DeleteObject(hrgn);
                    oldRegion?.Dispose();
                }
            }
        }

        private void OnStatsUpdated(double rx, double tx)
        {
            _rxMbps = rx;
            _txMbps = tx;
            if (Visible)
            {
                try { BeginInvoke(new Action(Invalidate)); } catch { }
            }
        }

        public void ShowAtCursor()
        {
            UpdateDpiMetrics();

            if (!GetCursorPos(out Point cursor))
                cursor = Cursor.Position;

            var screen = Screen.FromPoint(cursor).WorkingArea;

            int x = Math.Max(screen.Left + 8, Math.Min(cursor.X - Width / 2, screen.Right - Width - 8));
            int y = Math.Max(screen.Top + 8, Math.Min(cursor.Y - Height - 12, screen.Bottom - Height - 8));

            Location = new Point(x, y);
            if (!Visible)
                Show();

            SetDesktopLocation(x, y);
            SetForegroundWindow(Handle);
            Activate();
            Invalidate();
            _ = RefreshPingAsync();
        }

        private async Task RefreshPingAsync()
        {
            _pingStr = "...";
            _pingColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#888888");
            Invalidate();

            var ms = await BypassController.Current.CheckPingAsync();
            if (ms >= 0)
            {
                _pingStr = $"{ms} мс";
                _pingColor = ms < 80 ? UIHelper.GetAccentColor(true) : ms < 150 ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#FF8C00") : UIHelper.GetErrorColor();
            }
            else
            {
                _pingStr = "недоступен";
                _pingColor = UIHelper.GetErrorColor();
            }
            if (Visible) Invalidate();
        }

        protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseMove(e);
            int zone = GetZoneAt(e.Location);
            if (zone != _hoveredZone)
            {
                _hoveredZone = zone;
                Cursor = zone >= 0 ? Cursors.Hand : Cursors.Default;
                Invalidate();
            }
        }

        protected override void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseLeave(e);
            _hoveredZone = -1;
            Cursor = Cursors.Default;
            Invalidate();
        }

        protected override async void OnMouseClick(MouseEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnMouseClick(e);
            if (e.Button != MouseButtons.Left) return;

            switch (GetZoneAt(e.Location))
            {
                case 1:
                    await CycleProfileAsync(-1);
                    break;
                case 2:
                    await CycleProfileAsync(1);
                    break;
                case 3:
                    _mainWindow.ToggleBypass();
                    await Task.Delay(450);
                    Invalidate();
                    break;
                case 4:
                    await RefreshPingAsync();
                    break;
                case 5:
                    await _mainWindow.RestartBypassAsync();
                    Invalidate();
                    break;
                case 6:
                    Hide();
                    _mainWindow.RestoreFromTray();
                    break;
                case 7:
                    Hide();
                    _mainWindow.ForceExit();
                    break;
            }
        }

        private async Task CycleProfileAsync(int dir)
        {
            var profiles = BypassController.Current.Zapret.GetAvailableProfiles();
            if (profiles.Count == 0) return;

            var current = SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName ?? "general.bat";
            var idx = profiles.FindIndex(p => string.Equals(p, current, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            var nextIdx = idx < 0 ? 0 : (idx + dir + profiles.Count) % profiles.Count;

            await _mainWindow.SelectProfileFromTrayAsync(profiles[nextIdx]);
            Invalidate();
        }

        private Rectangle RectPrevProf => SRect(154, 60, 24, 26);
        private Rectangle RectNextProf => SRect(270, 60, 24, 26);
        private Rectangle RectPowerBtn => SRect(14, 96, 280, 42);
        private Rectangle RectPingBtn => SRect(264, 196, 24, 24);
        private Rectangle RectRestartBtn => SRect(14, 236, 280, 34);
        private Rectangle RectOpenBtn => SRect(14, 272, 280, 34);
        private Rectangle RectExitBtn => SRect(14, 308, 280, 34);

        private int GetZoneAt(Point pt)
        {
            if (RectPrevProf.Contains(pt)) return 1;
            if (RectNextProf.Contains(pt)) return 2;
            if (RectPowerBtn.Contains(pt)) return 3;
            if (RectPingBtn.Contains(pt)) return 4;
            if (RectRestartBtn.Contains(pt)) return 5;
            if (RectOpenBtn.Contains(pt)) return 6;
            if (RectExitBtn.Contains(pt)) return 7;
            return -1;
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            var g = e.Graphics;
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            g.TextRenderingHint = System.Drawing.Text.TextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;

            bool zapretRun = BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning;
            bool proxyRun = BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning;
            bool anyRun = zapretRun || proxyRun;

            var accent = UIHelper.GetAccentColor(false);
            var accentLight = UIHelper.GetAccentColor(true);
            var accentDarkBg = UIHelper.GetAccentDarkBgColor();
            var danger = UIHelper.GetErrorColor();

            var sfCenter = new StringFormat { Alignment = StringAlignment.Center, LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center };
            var sfLeftCenter = new StringFormat { Alignment = StringAlignment.Near, LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center };
            var sfRightCenter = new StringFormat { Alignment = StringAlignment.Far, LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center };

            // Внешняя рамка
            using (var borderPen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#2A2A2A"), S(1.5f)))
            using (var borderPath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(new Rectangle(1, 1, Width - 3, Height - 3), SI(11)))
            {
                g.DrawPath(borderPen, borderPath);
            }

            // 1. Шапка: иконка, заголовок, подзаголовок и бейдж версии
            var logoRect = SRect(14, 14, 32, 32);
            using (var logoBg = new SolidBrush(accentDarkBg))
            using (var logoPen = new Pen(accent, S(1f)))
            using (var logoPath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(logoRect, SI(8)))
            {
                g.FillPath(logoBg, logoPath);
                g.DrawPath(logoPen, logoPath);
            }
            using (var fLogo = new Font("Segoe UI", S(15f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var bLogo = new SolidBrush(accentLight))
            {
                g.DrawString("Z", fLogo, bLogo, logoRect, sfCenter);
            }

            using (var fTitle = new Font("Segoe UI", S(13.5f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var fSub = new Font("Segoe UI", S(11f), FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var bSub = new SolidBrush(anyRun ? accentLight : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#888888")))
            {
                g.DrawString("Zapret for ADHD", fTitle, Brushes.White, S(54), S(13));
                string subTxt = (zapretRun && proxyRun) ? "Zapret + TgWsProxy активны"
                              : zapretRun ? "Обход Zapret активен"
                              : proxyRun ? "Прокси Telegram активен"
                              : "Службы остановлены";
                g.DrawString(subTxt, fSub, bSub, S(54), S(30));
            }

            var verRect = SRect(250, 18, 44, 22);
            using (var verBg = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1A1A1A")))
            using (var verPen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#2A2A2A")))
            using (var verPath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(verRect, SI(5)))
            using (var fVer = new Font("Consolas", S(10.5f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var bVer = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#888888")))
            {
                g.FillPath(verBg, verPath);
                g.DrawPath(verPen, verPath);
                g.DrawString(AppConstants.AppVersion, fVer, bVer, verRect, sfCenter);
            }

            // 2. Пилюли статуса + селектор профиля
            DrawStatusPill(g, SRect(14, 60, 66, 26), "Zapret", zapretRun ? accentLight : danger);
            DrawStatusPill(g, SRect(84, 60, 64, 26), "Proxy", proxyRun ? accentLight : danger);

            var profBox = SRect(154, 60, 140, 26);
            using (var profBg = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#181818")))
            using (var profPen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#262626")))
            using (var profPath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(profBox, SI(6)))
            {
                g.FillPath(profBg, profPath);
                g.DrawPath(profPen, profPath);
            }
            using (var fArrow = new Font("Segoe UI", S(13f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var fProf = new Font("Consolas", S(10.5f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            {
                g.DrawString("‹", fArrow, _hoveredZone == 1 ? Brushes.White : Brushes.Gray, RectPrevProf, sfCenter);
                g.DrawString("›", fArrow, _hoveredZone == 2 ? Brushes.White : Brushes.Gray, RectNextProf, sfCenter);

                string profName = SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName ?? "general.bat";
                if (profName.Length > 13) profName = profName.Substring(0, 12) + "…";
                var textRect = SRect(176, 60, 96, 26);
                g.DrawString(profName, fProf, Brushes.White, textRect, sfCenter);
            }

            // 3. Главная кнопка запуска / остановки
            var pwrBgColor = anyRun ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#2A1215") : accentDarkBg;
            var pwrBorderColor = anyRun ? danger : accent;
            using (var pwrBg = new SolidBrush(pwrBgColor))
            using (var pwrPen = new Pen(pwrBorderColor, S(1.2f)))
            using (var pwrPath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(RectPowerBtn, SI(9)))
            {
                g.FillPath(pwrBg, pwrPath);
                g.DrawPath(pwrPen, pwrPath);
            }
            using (var fPwr = new Font("Segoe UI", S(13f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            {
                g.DrawString(anyRun ? "■   Остановить обход" : "▶   Включить обход", fPwr, Brushes.White, RectPowerBtn, sfCenter);
            }

            // 4. Бенто-карточка телеметрии и пинга
            var teleRect = SRect(14, 148, 280, 80);
            using (var teleBg = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#171717")))
            using (var telePen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#242424")))
            using (var telePath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(teleRect, SI(9)))
            {
                g.FillPath(teleBg, telePath);
                g.DrawPath(telePen, telePath);
            }

            using (var fLbl = new Font("Segoe UI", S(10f), FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var fVal = new Font("Consolas", S(12f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel))
            using (var bGray = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#888888")))
            using (var sepPen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#242424")))
            {
                // Входящий (левая колонка)
                g.DrawString("↓ Входящий", fLbl, bGray, S(24), S(154));
                g.DrawString($"{_rxMbps:0.0} Мбит/с", fVal, Brushes.White, S(24), S(170));

                // Вертикальный разделитель
                g.DrawLine(sepPen, S(154), S(155), S(154), S(185));

                // Исходящий (правая колонка)
                g.DrawString("↑ Исходящий", fLbl, bGray, SRect(160, 154, 124, 14), sfRightCenter);
                g.DrawString($"{_txMbps:0.0} Мбит/с", fVal, Brushes.White, SRect(160, 170, 124, 16), sfRightCenter);

                // Горизонтальный разделитель
                g.DrawLine(sepPen, S(24), S(191), S(284), S(191));

                // Строка пинга
                using var fPingLbl = new Font("Segoe UI", S(11.5f), FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
                g.DrawString("Пинг (EU):", fPingLbl, bGray, SRect(24, 196, 110, 24), sfLeftCenter);

                using var bPing = new SolidBrush(_pingColor);
                g.DrawString(_pingStr, fVal, bPing, SRect(110, 196, 146, 24), sfRightCenter);

                using var btnPath = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(RectPingBtn, SI(5));
                using var btnBg = new SolidBrush(_hoveredZone == 4 ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#2E2E2E") : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#222222"));
                g.FillPath(btnBg, btnPath);
                g.DrawString("↻", fVal, Brushes.LightGray, RectPingBtn, sfCenter);
            }

            // 5. Нижние кнопки быстрых действий
            DrawMenuRow(g, RectRestartBtn, "↻   Перезапустить службы", _hoveredZone == 5, false);
            DrawMenuRow(g, RectOpenBtn, "❐   Открыть панель управления", _hoveredZone == 6, false);
            DrawMenuRow(g, RectExitBtn, "✕   Полностью закрыть", _hoveredZone == 7, true);
        }

        private void DrawStatusPill(Graphics g, Rectangle rect, string text, Color dotColor)
        {
            using var bg = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#181818"));
            using var pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#262626"));
            using var path = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(rect, SI(6));
            g.FillPath(bg, path);
            g.DrawPath(pen, path);

            int dotSize = SI(6);
            using var dotBrush = new SolidBrush(dotColor);
            g.FillEllipse(dotBrush, rect.X + SI(8), rect.Y + (rect.Height - dotSize) / 2, dotSize, dotSize);

            using var font = new Font("Segoe UI", S(10.5f), FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
            using var textBrush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#CCCCCC"));
            var textRect = new Rectangle(rect.X + SI(18), rect.Y, rect.Width - SI(20), rect.Height);
            var sf = new StringFormat { Alignment = StringAlignment.Near, LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center };
            g.DrawString(text, font, textBrush, textRect, sf);
        }

        private void DrawMenuRow(Graphics g, Rectangle rect, string text, bool hovered, bool isDanger)
        {
            if (hovered)
            {
                var bgCol = isDanger ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#2A1215") : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1E1E1E");
                var brdCol = isDanger ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#5C242A") : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#2E2E2E");
                using var bg = new SolidBrush(bgCol);
                using var pen = new Pen(brdCol);
                using var path = UIHelper.CreateRoundedRectPath(rect, SI(7));
                g.FillPath(bg, path);
                g.DrawPath(pen, path);
            }

            using var font = new Font("Segoe UI", S(12f), isDanger ? FontStyle.Bold : FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
            using var brush = new SolidBrush(isDanger ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#F44336") : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#E0E0E0"));
            var textRect = new Rectangle(rect.X + SI(10), rect.Y, rect.Width - SI(16), rect.Height);
            var sf = new StringFormat { Alignment = StringAlignment.Near, LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center };
            g.DrawString(text, font, brush, textRect, sf);
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\UIHelper.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Drawing.Imaging;
using System.Drawing.Text;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public static class UIHelper
    {
        [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
        public static extern bool DestroyIcon(IntPtr handle);

        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, (Icon trayIcon, string icoFilePath)> _iconCache = new();
        private static readonly string[] AllThemes = ["green", "blue", "purple", "amber", "pink"];

        public static Action<string, string, bool>? WebAlertHandler { get; set; }
        public static Func<string, string, string, bool, Task<bool>>? WebConfirmHandler { get; set; }

        public static string GetAccentHex(bool light = false, string? themeOverride = null)
        {
            var t = (themeOverride ?? SettingsManager.Current.AccentTheme ?? "green").ToLowerInvariant();
            return t switch
            {
                "blue" => light ? "#60A5FA" : "#0078D7",
                "purple" => light ? "#A78BFA" : "#7C3AED",
                "amber" => light ? "#FBBF24" : "#D97706",
                "pink" or "cyan" or "crimson" => light ? "#F472B6" : "#DB2777",
                _ => light ? "#4CAF50" : "#107C10"
            };
        }

        public static string GetAccentDarkBgHex(string? themeOverride = null)
        {
            var t = (themeOverride ?? SettingsManager.Current.AccentTheme ?? "green").ToLowerInvariant();
            return t switch
            {
                "blue" => "#102033",
                "purple" => "#1E1433",
                "amber" => "#2B1D0E",
                "pink" or "cyan" or "crimson" => "#2D1222",
                _ => "#142616"
            };
        }

        public static Color GetAccentColor(bool light = false)
            => ColorTranslator.FromHtml(GetAccentHex(light));

        public static Color GetAccentDarkBgColor()
            => ColorTranslator.FromHtml(GetAccentDarkBgHex());

        public static Color GetErrorColor()
            => ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#D13438");

        public static void ApplyThemeColors()
        {
            // При старте или смене темы гарантируем наличие обеих иконок (ON и OFF)
            WarmupAllIcons();
        }

        public static void WarmupAllIcons()
        {
            try
            {
                var currentTheme = (SettingsManager.Current.AccentTheme ?? "green").ToLowerInvariant();
                if (currentTheme is "crimson" or "cyan") currentTheme = "pink";

                // Сначала мгновенно готовим обе версии (ON и OFF) для текущей темы
                GetOrCreateAppIcon(false, currentTheme);
                GetOrCreateAppIcon(true, currentTheme);

                // Остальные темы догенерируем в фоне, если их ещё нет на диске
                Task.Run(() =>
                {
                    foreach (var theme in AllThemes)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            GetOrCreateAppIcon(false, theme);
                            GetOrCreateAppIcon(true, theme);
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                });
            }
            catch { }
        }

        public static (Icon trayIcon, string icoFilePath) GetOrCreateAppIcon(bool isRunning, string? themeOverride = null)
        {
            var theme = (themeOverride ?? SettingsManager.Current.AccentTheme ?? "green").ToLowerInvariant();
            if (theme is "crimson" or "cyan") theme = "pink";

            var cacheKey = $"{theme}_{(isRunning ? "on" : "off")}";

            if (_iconCache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var cached) && File.Exists(cached.icoFilePath))
                return cached;

            var cacheDir = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "Assets", "Cache", "Icons");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(cacheDir);
            var icoPath = Path.Combine(cacheDir, $"icon_{cacheKey}.ico");

            if (!File.Exists(icoPath) || new FileInfo(icoPath).Length < 100)
            {
                using var bmp = RenderIconBitmap(isRunning, theme);
                SaveBitmapAsTrueIco(bmp, icoPath);
            }

            var managedTrayIcon = new Icon(icoPath, 64, 64);
            var result = (managedTrayIcon, icoPath);
            _iconCache[cacheKey] = result;
            return result;
        }

        // Записывает настоящий бинарный .ICO файл с PNG-слоем внутри (понимается Windows Taskbar, Alt+Tab и Photino)
        private static void SaveBitmapAsTrueIco(Bitmap bmp, string icoPath)
        {
            using var pngMs = new MemoryStream();
            bmp.Save(pngMs, ImageFormat.Png);
            byte[] pngBytes = pngMs.ToArray();

            using var fs = new FileStream(icoPath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite);
            using var bw = new BinaryWriter(fs);

            // ICONDIR header (6 байт)
            bw.Write((short)0);   // Reserved
            bw.Write((short)1);   // Type: 1 = ICO
            bw.Write((short)1);   // Count: 1 image

            // ICONDIRENTRY (16 байт)
            bw.Write((byte)bmp.Width);
            bw.Write((byte)bmp.Height);
            bw.Write((byte)0);    // Palette
            bw.Write((byte)0);    // Reserved
            bw.Write((short)1);   // Color planes
            bw.Write((short)32);  // Bits per pixel
            bw.Write(pngBytes.Length); // Size of image data
            bw.Write(22);         // Offset of image data (6 + 16 = 22)

            // PNG payload
            bw.Write(pngBytes);
        }

        private static Bitmap RenderIconBitmap(bool isRunning, string theme)
        {
            const int size = 64;
            var bmp = new Bitmap(size, size);
            using var g = Graphics.FromImage(bmp);
            g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            g.TextRenderingHint = TextRenderingHint.AntiAliasGridFit;
            g.Clear(Color.Transparent);

            var accentColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml(GetAccentHex(light: true, theme));
            var borderColor = isRunning ? accentColor : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#3A3A3A");
            var bgTop = isRunning ? ColorTranslator.FromHtml(GetAccentDarkBgHex(theme)) : ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#18181B");
            var bgBottom = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#0A0A0C");

            using var path = CreateRoundedRectPath(new Rectangle(4, 4, size - 8, size - 8), 14);
            using (var bgBrush = new LinearGradientBrush(new Rectangle(0, 0, size, size), bgTop, bgBottom, LinearGradientMode.Vertical))
            {
                g.FillPath(bgBrush, path);
            }

            using (var pen = new Pen(borderColor, 3.2f))
            {
                g.DrawPath(pen, path);
            }

            using var font = new Font("Segoe UI", 28f, FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Pixel);
            var textBrush = isRunning ? new SolidBrush(accentColor) : new SolidBrush(Color.White);
            using (textBrush)
            {
                var sf = new StringFormat
                {
                    Alignment = StringAlignment.Center,
                    LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center
                };
                g.DrawString("Z", font, textBrush, new RectangleF(0, 1, size, size), sf);
            }

            var dotColor = isRunning ? accentColor : GetErrorColor();
            using var dotBgBrush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#0A0A0C"));
            using var dotBrush = new SolidBrush(dotColor);
            g.FillEllipse(dotBgBrush, size - 20, size - 20, 16, 16);
            g.FillEllipse(dotBrush, size - 17, size - 17, 10, 10);

            return bmp;
        }

        public static GraphicsPath CreateRoundedRectPath(Rectangle bounds, int radius)
        {
            int d = radius * 2;
            var path = new GraphicsPath();
            path.AddArc(bounds.X, bounds.Y, d, d, 180, 90);
            path.AddArc(bounds.Right - d, bounds.Y, d, d, 270, 90);
            path.AddArc(bounds.Right - d, bounds.Bottom - d, d, d, 0, 90);
            path.AddArc(bounds.X, bounds.Bottom - d, d, d, 90, 90);
            path.CloseFigure();
            return path;
        }

        public static void ShowAlert(string title, string message, bool isError = false)
        {
            WebAlertHandler?.Invoke(title, message, isError);
        }

        public static Task<bool> ShowConfirmAsync(string title, string message, string confirmText = "Да", bool isDanger = false)
        {
            if (WebConfirmHandler != null)
                return WebConfirmHandler.Invoke(title, message, confirmText, isDanger);

            return Task.FromResult(false);
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\UpdateManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class AppUpdateResult
    {
        public bool UpdateAvailable { get; set; }
        public string Version { get; set; } = "";
        public string DownloadUrl { get; set; } = "";
        public string ReleaseUrl { get; set; } = "";
    }

    public class CoreUpdateResult
    {
        public bool UpdateAvailable { get; set; }
        public string Version { get; set; } = "";
        public string CoreName { get; set; } = "";
        public string CurrentVersion { get; set; } = "";
        public string DownloadUrl { get; set; } = "";
        public string TargetFileName { get; set; } = "";
        public bool IsZip { get; set; }
    }

    public static class UpdateManager
    {
        public const string CurrentVersion = AppConstants.AppVersion;

        private static readonly HttpClient _httpClient = new HttpClient();

        static UpdateManager()
        {
            _httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("User-Agent", AppConstants.GithubUserAgent);
        }

        private static string CleanVersion(string? version)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(version)) return "0.0.0";
            return version.Trim().TrimStart('v', 'V');
        }

        private static bool IsNewerVersion(string? remoteVersion, string? localVersion)
        {
            string cleanRemote = CleanVersion(remoteVersion);
            string cleanLocal = CleanVersion(localVersion);
