            if (s.TryGetProperty("notificationsEnabled", out var ne)) cur.NotificationsEnabled = ne.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("focusMode", out var fm)) cur.FocusMode = fm.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("autoStartBypass", out var asb)) cur.AutoStartBypass = asb.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("stopServicesOnExit", out var ssoe)) cur.StopServicesOnExit = ssoe.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("soundEffectsEnabled", out var see)) cur.SoundEffectsEnabled = see.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("skipSplashScreen", out var sss)) cur.SkipSplashScreen = sss.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("watchdogPingAutoRestart", out var wpar)) cur.WatchdogPingAutoRestart = wpar.GetBoolean();

            if (s.TryGetProperty("alwaysOnTop", out var aot))
            {
                bool top = aot.GetBoolean();
                cur.AlwaysOnTop = top;
                _mainWindow.InvokeOnUI(() => _mainWindow.Window.SetTopMost(top));
            }

            bool themeChanged = false;
            if (s.TryGetProperty("accentTheme", out var at))
            {
                var themeStr = at.GetString() ?? "green";
                if (cur.AccentTheme != themeStr)
                {
                    cur.AccentTheme = themeStr;
                    themeChanged = true;
                }
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("compactMode", out var cm))
            {
                bool newCompact = cm.GetBoolean();
                if (cur.CompactMode != newCompact)
                {
                    cur.CompactMode = newCompact;
                    _mainWindow.AnimateWindowSize(newCompact);
                }
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("hardwareAcceleration", out var ha))
            {
                cur.HardwareAcceleration = ha.GetBoolean();
            }

            if (themeChanged)
            {
                UIHelper.ApplyThemeColors();
                _mainWindow.RefreshAppIcon();
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("pingUrl", out var pu))
            {
                var urlStr = pu.GetString();
                cur.PingUrl = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(urlStr) ? AppConstants.DefaultPingUrl : urlStr.Trim();
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("statsUpdateInterval", out var sui))
                cur.StatsUpdateInterval = Math.Max(1, sui.GetInt32());

            if (s.TryGetProperty("autoRestartServices", out var ars))
                cur.AutoRestartServices = ars.GetBoolean();

            if (s.TryGetProperty("hostsSourceIndex", out var hsi))
                cur.HostsSourceIndex = Math.Clamp(hsi.GetInt32(), 0, 3);

            SettingsManager.Save();
        }

        private void HandleExportConfigBackup()
        {
            _mainWindow.InvokeOnUI(() =>
            {
                using var sfd = new SaveFileDialog
                {
                    Filter = "Конфигурация Zapret (*.json)|*.json",
                    Title = "Экспорт резервной копии настроек",
                    FileName = $"ZapretForADHD_Config_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd}.json"
                };

                if (sfd.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(SettingsManager.Current, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true });
                        File.WriteAllText(sfd.FileName, json);
                        SendEvent("alert", new { title = "Бэкап сохранен", message = "Ваша конфигурация успешно экспортирована в файл!", isError = false });
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        SendEvent("alert", new { title = "Ошибка экспорта", message = ex.Message, isError = true });
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        private async Task HandleImportConfigBackupAsync()
        {
            string? selectedFile = null;
            _mainWindow.InvokeOnUI(() =>
            {
                using var ofd = new OpenFileDialog
                {
                    Filter = "Конфигурация Zapret (*.json)|*.json",
                    Title = "Импорт резервной копии настроек"
                };
                if (ofd.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
                    selectedFile = ofd.FileName;
            });

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedFile)) return;

            try
            {
                var json = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(selectedFile);
                var loaded = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<AppSettings>(json);
                if (loaded == null)
                    throw new Exception("Не удалось распознать структуру файла настроек.");

                SettingsManager.Current = loaded;
                SettingsManager.Save();
                UIHelper.ApplyThemeColors();

                _mainWindow.InvokeOnUI(() =>
                {
                    _mainWindow.Window.SetTopMost(SettingsManager.Current.AlwaysOnTop);
                    _mainWindow.AnimateWindowSize(SettingsManager.Current.CompactMode);
                    _mainWindow.RefreshAppIcon();
                });

                await SendInitialStateAsync();
                AudioHelper.PlaySuccess();
                SendEvent("alert", new { title = "Настройки восстановлены", message = "Конфигурация успешно импортирована и применена!", isError = false });
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                SendEvent("alert", new { title = "Ошибка импорта", message = $"Не удалось загрузить файл настроек:\n{ex.Message}", isError = true });
            }
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            _watcherDebounceCts?.Cancel();
            _watcherDebounceCts?.Dispose();
            _modsWatcher?.Dispose();
            _pendingConfirmTcs?.TrySetResult(false);
        }

        private void BroadcastBypassState()
        {
            _mainWindow.UpdateIndicators(
                BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning,
                BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning);
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ZapretManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class ZapretManager : IBypassService
    {
        private static readonly Regex _winwsCmdRegex = new($@"{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}(?:\.exe)?[""']?\s*(.*)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _standardBatCmdRegex = new(@"^(@?echo|chcp|cd|pushd|popd|set|title|cls|color|mode|goto|call|if|for|pause|timeout|:)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _tokenSplitRegex = new(@"[\""].+?[\""]|[^ ]+", RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _fileParamRegex = new(@"^--(hostlist|hostlist-exclude|ipset|ipset-exclude|dpi-desync-fake-tls|dpi-desync-fake-quic|dpi-desync-fake-unknown-udp|dpi-desync-split-seqovl-pattern)=(.+)$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _unexpandedVarRegex = new(@"%[a-zA-Z0-9_]+%", RegexOptions.Compiled);

        private readonly string _basePath;
        private Process? _process;
        private DateTime _lastCheckTime = DateTime.MinValue;
        private bool _cachedRunning = false;

        public event Action<string>? LogMessage;

        public ZapretManager()
        {
            _basePath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory);
        }

        public List<string> GetAvailableProfiles()
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(_basePath))
                return new List<string> { "general.bat" };

            var list = Directory.GetFiles(_basePath, "*.bat")
                .Select(Path.GetFileName)
                .Where(name => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) &&
                              (name.StartsWith("general", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                               name.StartsWith("mod_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                .Cast<string>()
                .OrderBy(n => n)
                .ToList();

            return list.Count > 0 ? list : new List<string> { "general.bat" };
        }

        private string GetSourceDisplayName(string profileName)
        {
            if (profileName.StartsWith("mod_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                var modId = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(profileName).Substring(4);
                var modJsonPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.ModsDirectory, "strategies", modId, "mod.json");
                if (File.Exists(modJsonPath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var meta = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModMetaData>(File.ReadAllText(modJsonPath), new JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true });
                        if (meta != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(meta.Name))
                            return $"моде «{meta.Name}» ({profileName})";
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
                return $"моде «{modId}» ({profileName})";
            }
            return $"профиле «{profileName}»";
        }

        public static string ParseAndValidateBatLines(string[] lines, string sourceName, string zapretBasePath, bool checkReferencedFiles = true)
        {
            if (lines == null || lines.Length == 0 || lines.All(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace))
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName}: Файл стратегии пуст.");

            var arguments = "";
            var isReadingArgs = false;
            var winwsFound = false;
            var winwsStartLine = 0;

            for (var i = 0; i < lines.Length; i++)
            {
                var lineNum = i + 1;
                var rawLine = lines[i];
                var trimmed = rawLine.Trim();

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(trimmed) ||
                    trimmed.StartsWith("::") ||
                    trimmed.StartsWith("rem ", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    trimmed.Equals("rem", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!isReadingArgs)
                {
                    if (trimmed.Contains(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        isReadingArgs = true;
                        winwsFound = true;
                        winwsStartLine = lineNum;

                        var match = _winwsCmdRegex.Match(trimmed);
                        trimmed = match.Success ? match.Groups[1].Value.Trim() : "";
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (!_standardBatCmdRegex.IsMatch(trimmed))
                            throw new Exception($"Синтаксическая ошибка в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nНеизвестная команда «{trimmed}».\nОжидался вызов {AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe или стандартная инструкция Batch.");
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                if (isReadingArgs)
                {
                    var hasContinuation = trimmed.EndsWith("^");
                    var cleanLine = hasContinuation ? trimmed.Substring(0, trimmed.Length - 1).TrimEnd() : trimmed;
                    var quoteCount = cleanLine.Count(c => c == '\"');

                    if (quoteCount % 2 != 0)
                        throw new Exception($"Ошибка синтаксиса в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nНе закрыта кавычка (\") в параметрах:\n«{rawLine.Trim()}»");

                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleanLine))
                    {
                        var tokens = _tokenSplitRegex.Matches(cleanLine)
                                          .Select(m => m.Value.Trim())
                                          .ToList();

                        foreach (var token in tokens)
                        {
                            if (!token.StartsWith("--") && !token.StartsWith("-") && !token.Equals("--new", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                throw new Exception($"Ошибка параметра в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nАргумент «{token}» не подходит для Zapret (winws).\nВсе параметры должны начинаться с «--» (например, --filter-tcp=80 или --dpi-desync=fake).\nСтрока: «{rawLine.Trim()}»");

                            if (token.StartsWith("---") || token == "--" || token == "-")
                                throw new Exception($"Ошибка синтаксиса в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nНекорректный формат флага «{token}».\nСтрока: «{rawLine.Trim()}»");

                            if (checkReferencedFiles && Directory.Exists(zapretBasePath))
                            {
                                var fileMatch = _fileParamRegex.Match(token);
                                if (fileMatch.Success)
                                {
                                    var paramName = fileMatch.Groups[1].Value;
                                    var rawFilePath = fileMatch.Groups[2].Value.Trim('\"', '\'');

                                    if (!rawFilePath.StartsWith("0x", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && !rawFilePath.StartsWith("!"))
                                    {
                                        var resolvedPath = rawFilePath
                                            .Replace("%BIN%", @"bin\")
                                            .Replace("%LISTS%", @"lists\")
                                            .Replace("%~dp0", "");

                                        var fullCheckPath = Path.IsPathRooted(resolvedPath)
                                            ? resolvedPath
                                            : Path.Combine(zapretBasePath, resolvedPath);

                                        if (!File.Exists(fullCheckPath))
                                            throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nВ параметре «--{paramName}» указан несуществующий файл «{rawFilePath}».\nСтрока: «{rawLine.Trim()}»");
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        arguments += " " + cleanLine;
                    }

                    if (!hasContinuation)
                        break;
                }
            }

            if (!winwsFound)
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка структуры в {sourceName}:\nВ батнике не найден вызов ядра «{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe». Этот скрипт не подходит для Zapret.");

            var (gfAll, gfTcp, gfUdp) = ServiceToolsManager.GetGameFilterSubstitutions();
            ServiceToolsManager.SyncGameFilterFile();

            arguments = arguments.Replace("%BIN%", @"bin\")
                                 .Replace("%LISTS%", @"lists\")
                                 .Replace("%~dp0", "")
                                 .Replace("%GameFilterTCP%", gfTcp)
                                 .Replace("%GameFilterUDP%", gfUdp)
                                 .Replace("%GameFilter%", gfAll)
                                 .Trim();

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(arguments))
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName} [Строка {winwsStartLine}]:\nКоманда «{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}» вызвана без параметров фильтрации.");

            var unexpandedVar = _unexpandedVarRegex.Match(arguments);
            if (unexpandedVar.Success)
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName}:\nОбнаружена неизвестная переменная «{unexpandedVar.Value}», которая не поддерживается в прямом запуске Zapret.");

            return arguments;
        }

        public void Start(string profileName)
        {
            Stop();

            var batFilePath = Path.Combine(_basePath, profileName);
            var sourceName = GetSourceDisplayName(profileName);

            if (!File.Exists(batFilePath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Конфигурационный файл не найден в {sourceName} по пути: {batFilePath}");

            var lines = File.ReadAllLines(batFilePath);
            var arguments = ParseAndValidateBatLines(lines, sourceName, _basePath, checkReferencedFiles: true);

            var exePath = Path.Combine(_basePath, $"{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe");
            var binExePath = Path.Combine(_basePath, "bin", $"{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe");

            if (!File.Exists(exePath) && File.Exists(binExePath))
                exePath = binExePath;

            if (!File.Exists(exePath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Не найден исполняемый файл ядра по пути: {exePath}. Запустите проверку обновлений в настройках.");

            var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
            {
                FileName = exePath,
                Arguments = arguments,
                UseShellExecute = false,
                CreateNoWindow = true,
                RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                RedirectStandardError = true,
                WorkingDirectory = _basePath
            };

            _process = new Process { StartInfo = startInfo };

            var errorOutputs = new ConcurrentQueue<string>();

            _process.OutputDataReceived += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Data))
                    return;

                var line = e.Data;
                var noise = new[]
                {
                    "Loading hostlist", "loading plain text list", "Loaded",
                    "user defined desync profile", "github version", "windivert initialized"
                };

                if (noise.Any(n => line.Contains(n, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                    return;

                LogMessage?.Invoke($"[Zapret] {line}");
            };

            _process.ErrorDataReceived += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Data))
                {
                    errorOutputs.Enqueue(e.Data);
                    LogMessage?.Invoke($"[Zapret ERR] {e.Data}");
                }
            };

            _process.Start();
            _process.BeginOutputReadLine();
            _process.BeginErrorReadLine();

            if (_process.WaitForExit(350))
            {
                var errDetails = !errorOutputs.IsEmpty
                    ? string.Join(" | ", errorOutputs)
                    : $"Код завершения: {_process.ExitCode}";

                _cachedRunning = false;
                throw new Exception($"Ядро Zapret отвергло конфигурацию в {sourceName}!\nПричина от движка winws: {errDetails}");
            }

            _cachedRunning = true;
            _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public void Stop()
        {
            try
            {
                if (_process != null)
                {
                    if (!_process.HasExited)
                        _process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                    _process.Dispose();
                    _process = null;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка остановки процесса Zapret: {ex.Message}");
            }

            ProcessHelper.KillProcessesByName(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
            _cachedRunning = false;
            _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public bool IsRunning
        {
            get
            {
                try
                {
                    if (_process != null && !_process.HasExited)
                        return true;
                }
                catch { }

                if ((DateTime.UtcNow - _lastCheckTime).TotalSeconds < 2.5)
                    return _cachedRunning;

                _cachedRunning = ProcessHelper.IsProcessRunning(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
                _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
                return _cachedRunning;
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ZapretScanner.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Timers;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class ZapretScanner
    {
        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);
        private const int SW_HIDE = 0;

        private Process? _scanProcess;
        private CancellationTokenSource? _scanCts;
        private readonly List<string> _topConfigs = new List<string>();

        public event Action<string>? LogMessage;
        public event Action<string, int, int>? ConfigTested; // fileName, okCount, totalCount
        public event Action<List<string>>? ScanCompleted;
        public event Action<Exception>? ScanFailed;

        public bool IsScanning => _scanProcess != null && !_scanProcess.HasExited;

        public async Task StartScanAsync()
        {
            if (IsScanning) return;

            _topConfigs.Clear();
            _scanCts?.Dispose();
            _scanCts = new CancellationTokenSource();

            using var hideTimer = new System.Timers.Timer(120) { AutoReset = false };
            hideTimer.Elapsed += (s, ev) =>
            {
                if (_scanCts == null || _scanCts.IsCancellationRequested)
                    return;

                var procs = Process.GetProcessesByName(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
                foreach (var p in procs)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (p.MainWindowHandle != IntPtr.Zero)
                            ShowWindow(p.MainWindowHandle, SW_HIDE);
                    }
                    catch { }
                    finally
                    {
                        p.Dispose();
                    }
                }

                try { if (!_scanCts.IsCancellationRequested) hideTimer.Start(); } catch { }
            };
            hideTimer.Start();

            try
            {
                var scriptPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory, "utils", "test zapret.ps1");

                if (!File.Exists(scriptPath))
                    throw new FileNotFoundException("Скрипт тестирования не найден по пути: " + scriptPath);

                var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "powershell.exe",
                    Arguments = $"-NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"{scriptPath}\"",
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardInput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true,
                    StandardOutputEncoding = System.Text.Encoding.UTF8
                };

                _scanProcess = new Process { StartInfo = startInfo };
                _scanProcess.Start();

                await _scanProcess.StandardInput.WriteLineAsync("1");
                await _scanProcess.StandardInput.WriteLineAsync("1");
                _scanProcess.StandardInput.Close();

                await Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    while (!_scanProcess.StandardOutput.EndOfStream)
                    {
                        if (_scanCts.Token.IsCancellationRequested) break;

                        var line = await _scanProcess.StandardOutput.ReadLineAsync();
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))
                            continue;

                        line = Regex.Replace(line, @"\x1B\[[0-9;]*[a-zA-Z]", "");

                        string? simpleLog = SimplifyLogLine(line);
                        if (simpleLog != null)
                            LogMessage?.Invoke($"[Auto] {simpleLog}");
                    }

                    if (!_scanProcess.HasExited)
                        _scanProcess.WaitForExit();

                }, _scanCts.Token);

                if (_scanCts.Token.IsCancellationRequested)
                    LogMessage?.Invoke("🛑 Сканирование было прервано пользователем.");
                else
                    ScanCompleted?.Invoke(new List<string>(_topConfigs));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ScanFailed?.Invoke(ex);
            }
            finally
            {
                hideTimer.Stop();
                try
                {
                    _scanProcess?.Dispose();
                    _scanProcess = null;
                }
                catch { }
            }
        }

        public void CancelScan()
        {
            _scanCts?.Cancel();
            try
            {
                if (_scanProcess != null && !_scanProcess.HasExited)
                {
                    _scanProcess.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                    _scanProcess.Dispose();
                    _scanProcess = null;
                }
            }
            catch { }

            ProcessHelper.KillProcessesByName(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
        }

        private string? SimplifyLogLine(string line)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))
                return null;

            var progressMatch = Regex.Match(line, @"\[(\d+/\d+)\]\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\(\)\s]+\.bat)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (progressMatch.Success)
            {
                var progress = progressMatch.Groups[1].Value;
                var configName = progressMatch.Groups[2].Value.Trim();
                return $"[{progress}] 🔍 Анализ профиля: {configName}...";
            }

            if (line.Contains("=== ANALYTICS ==="))
                return "📊 Сводка результатов тестирования:";

            var statsMatch = Regex.Match(line, @"([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\(\)\s]+\.bat)\s*:\s*(?:HTTP\s*)?OK:\s*(\d+).*?(?:ERR|FAIL):\s*(\d+)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (statsMatch.Success)
            {
                var batName = statsMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();
                var okStr = statsMatch.Groups[2].Value;
                var errStr = statsMatch.Groups[3].Value;

                int.TryParse(okStr, out int ok);
                int.TryParse(errStr, out int err);
                ConfigTested?.Invoke(batName, ok, ok + err);

                if (err == 0 && ok > 0)
                {
                    if (!_topConfigs.Contains(batName)) _topConfigs.Add(batName);
                    return $"      ✨ {batName} -> Работает идеально (Успешно: {okStr})";
                }
                else
                    return $"      ⚠️ {batName} -> Есть блокировки (Успешно: {okStr}, Ошибок: {errStr})";
            }

            if (line.Contains("Best config:"))
            {
                var bestMatch = Regex.Match(line, @"Best config:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\(\)\s]+\.bat)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
                if (bestMatch.Success)
                {
                    var best = bestMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();
                    if (_topConfigs.Contains(best)) _topConfigs.Remove(best);
                    _topConfigs.Insert(0, best);
                }
                return null;
            }

            return null;
        }
    }
}

// =========================================================================
// 1. ГЛОБАЛЬНОЕ СОСТОЯНИЕ И МОСТ PHOTINO / WEBVIEW2
// =========================================================================
let appState = {
    isRunning: false,
    zapretRunning: false,
    proxyRunning: false,
    isScanning: false,
    firstTimeScanPrompted: false,
    selectedProfile: 'general.bat',
    currentPing: -1,
    configs: [],
    gameFilter: { mode: 'disabled', tcp: '1024-65535', udp: '1024-65535' },
    modsSubTab: 'bat',
    batMods: [],
    listMods: [],
    editingMod: { id: '', isBat: true, wordWrap: true, fontSize: 13 },
    settings: {
        focusMode: false,
        compactMode: false,
        accentTheme: 'green',
        statsUpdateInterval: 1,
        hostsSourceIndex: 2,
        ipSetMode: 'loaded',
        watchdogPingAutoRestart: false
    }
};

function sendCmd(action, data = {}) {
    if (!action || action === 'playClick') return;

    const payload = JSON.stringify({ action, ...data });

    if (window.external && typeof window.external.sendMessage === 'function') {
        window.external.sendMessage(payload);
    } else if (window.chrome && window.chrome.webview) {
        window.chrome.webview.postMessage(payload);
    }
}

function handleBackendEvent(type, data) {
    switch (type) {
        case 'initData':
            document.querySelectorAll('.app-ver-text').forEach(el => el.innerText = data.version);
            document.getElementById('chk-zapret').checked = data.settings.zapretEnabled;
            document.getElementById('chk-proxy').checked = data.settings.tgProxyEnabled;

            appState.firstTimeScanPrompted = data.settings.firstTimeScanPrompted;
            appState.selectedProfile = data.settings.selectedProfileName || 'general.bat';
            appState.gameFilter = {
                mode: data.settings.gameFilterMode || 'disabled',
                tcp: data.settings.gameFilterTcpRange || '1024-65535',
                udp: data.settings.gameFilterUdpRange || '1024-65535'
            };

            if (typeof initSettingsFromBackend === 'function') {
                initSettingsFromBackend(data);
            }
            document.body.classList.toggle('compact-mode', !!data.settings.compactMode);

            if (data.settings.skipSplashScreen && !data.isFirstRun) {
                skipSplash();
            }

            appState.batMods = data.batMods || [];
            appState.listMods = data.listMods || [];

            initConfigsList(data.profiles || ['general.bat']);
            if (typeof renderModsView === 'function') renderModsView();
            if (typeof prepareDiagSkeletons === 'function') prepareDiagSkeletons();
            updateGameFilterBadge(data.gameFilterStatus, appState.gameFilter.mode !== 'disabled');
            updateRunningUI(data.zapretRunning, data.proxyRunning);

            if (data.zapretRunning || data.proxyRunning)
                appendLog(new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), 'Интерфейс загружен. Найдены активные процессы в фоне.');
            else
                appendLog(new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), 'Интерфейс загружен. Ожидание команд...');

            if (data.isFirstRun) {
                openWizardModal();
            }
            break;

        case 'wizardLog':
            appendWizardLog(data.time, data.message, data.isProgress);
            break;

        case 'wizardCompleted':
            wizardFinished = true;
            wizHyperSpeed = false;
            wizCurrentPct = 100;
            document.getElementById('wiz-finish-status').innerText = 'Установка успешно завершена!';
            document.getElementById('wiz-finish-status').className = 'text-base font-bold text-brand';
            setWizardProgress(100);
            const btnNext = document.getElementById('wiz-btn-next');
            btnNext.disabled = false;
            btnNext.style.opacity = '1';
            btnNext.innerText = 'Запустить панель →';
            break;

        case 'askConfirm':
            isBackendConfirm = true;
            showConfirm(data.title, data.message, data.confirmText || 'Да', null, data.isDanger);
            break;

        case 'routingStatusUpdated':
            if (typeof updateRoutingStatusUI === 'function') updateRoutingStatusUI(data);
            document.getElementById('btn-update-ipset').innerHTML = '<span class="mdl-icon text-xs text-brand">&#xE149;</span><span>Обновить базу</span>';
            document.getElementById('btn-update-ipset').disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('btn-update-hosts').innerHTML = '<span class="mdl-icon text-xs">&#xE945;</span><span>Обновить</span>';
            document.getElementById('btn-update-hosts').disabled = false;
            break;

        case 'updatesCheckFinished':
            document.getElementById('btn-check-updates').innerHTML = '<span class="mdl-icon text-xs">&#xE149;</span><span>Проверить обновления</span>';
            document.getElementById('btn-check-updates').disabled = false;
            break;

        case 'modsUpdated':
            appState.batMods = data.batMods || [];
            appState.listMods = data.listMods || [];
            if (data.selectedProfile) {
                appState.selectedProfile = data.selectedProfile;
            }
            initConfigsList(data.profiles || ['general.bat']);
            if (typeof renderModsView === 'function') renderModsView();
            break;

        case 'modIdGenerated':
            document.getElementById('new-mod-id').value = data.id;
            if (typeof updateModIdValidationUI === 'function') updateModIdValidationUI(data.id, false, false);
            break;

        case 'modIdChecked':
            if (typeof updateModIdValidationUI === 'function') updateModIdValidationUI(data.cleanId, data.isTaken, data.isEmpty);
            break;

        case 'modCreated':
            if (typeof closeCreateModModal === 'function') closeCreateModModal();
            break;

        case 'modContentLoaded':
            if (typeof openEditorWithData === 'function') openEditorWithData(data);
            break;

        case 'batValidationResult':
            if (data.isValid) {
                if (typeof setEditorStatus === 'function') setEditorStatus('✓ Синтаксис корректен!', 'text-brand');
            } else {
                if (typeof setEditorStatus === 'function') setEditorStatus('⚠ Ошибка в скрипте', 'text-[#F44336]');
                showAlert('Проверка синтаксиса Zapret', data.errorMessage, true);
            }
            break;

        case 'modSaveSyntaxWarning':
            showConfirm(
                'Предупреждение синтаксиса Zapret',
                `В скрипте обнаружена проблема:\n\n${data.errorMessage}\n\nВсё равно сохранить файл?`,
                'Сохранить всё равно',
                () => saveEditorChanges(true)
            );
            break;

        case 'modContentSaved':
            if (typeof closeEditorModal === 'function') closeEditorModal();
            break;

        case 'promptRestartZapret':
            showConfirm(
                'Перезапустить Zapret?',
                `${data.reason}\n\nФайл list-general.txt был обновлен в прямом эфире, но для применения новых доменов в уже работающем ядре winws.exe требуется перезапуск. Перезапустить сейчас?`,
                'Перезапустить',
                () => sendCmd('restartBypass')
            );
            break;

        case 'diagProgress':
            document.getElementById('diag-status-txt').innerText = data.status;
            document.getElementById('diag-progress-bar').style.width = `${data.progress}%`;
            if (typeof updateDiagStepPills === 'function') {
                updateDiagStepPills(data.progress, data.status);
            }
            break;

        case 'diagResult':
            if (typeof renderDiagnosticsReport === 'function') renderDiagnosticsReport(data);
            if (typeof updateRadarNodesFromReport === 'function') updateRadarNodesFromReport(data);
            if (typeof syncGlobe3DWithDiagReport === 'function') syncGlobe3DWithDiagReport(data);
            break;

        case 'log':
            appendLog(data.time, data.message, data.isProgress);
            break;

        case 'netStats':
            document.getElementById('stat-rx').innerText = data.download.toFixed(1);
            document.getElementById('stat-tx').innerText = data.upload.toFixed(1);
            updateChart(data.download, data.upload);
            updateRunningUI(data.zapretRunning, data.proxyRunning);
            break;

        case 'netStatus':
            document.getElementById('dot-net').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${data.isOnline ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
            break;

        case 'pingResult':
            document.getElementById('ping-refresh-icon').classList.remove('spin-anim');
            appState.currentPing = data.ping;
            updatePingDisplay(data.ping);
            document.getElementById('dot-net').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${data.ping >= 0 ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
            appState.configs.forEach(c => c.pingMs = data.ping > 0 ? data.ping : 999999);
            renderConfigCards();

            if (appState.isRunning && appState.settings.watchdogPingAutoRestart) {
                if (data.ping < 0) {
                    watchdogFailCount++;
                    if (watchdogFailCount >= 3) {
                        watchdogFailCount = 0;
                        appendLog(new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), '🐕 [Ping-Watchdog] Обнаружена потеря пинга. Автоматический перезапуск служб...');
                        sendCmd('restartBypass');
                    }
                } else {
                    watchdogFailCount = 0;
                }
            }
            break;

        case 'bypassState':
            updateRunningUI(data.zapretRunning, data.proxyRunning);
            break;

        case 'configTested':
            const item = appState.configs.find(c => c.fileName.toLowerCase() === data.fileName.toLowerCase());
            if (item) {
                item.testsOk = data.ok;
                item.testsTotal = data.total;
                if (appState.currentPing > 0) item.pingMs = appState.currentPing;
                renderConfigCards();
            }
            break;

        case 'scanState':
            appState.isScanning = data.isScanning;
            updateScanButtonUI();
            break;

        case 'scanCompleted':
            appState.isScanning = false;
            updateScanButtonUI();
            if (data.topConfigs && data.topConfigs.length > 0) {
                appState.configs.forEach(c => c.topRank = 999999);
                data.topConfigs.forEach((name, idx) => {
                    const cfg = appState.configs.find(c => c.fileName.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());
                    if (cfg) cfg.topRank = idx;
                });
                selectProfile(data.bestConfig);
                appendLog(new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), `🏆 Сканирование завершено! Выбрана лучшая стратегия: ${data.bestConfig}`);
            } else {
                appendLog(new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), '⚠ Сканирование завершено, но ни одна стратегия не сработала.');
            }
            break;

        case 'scanFailed':
            appState.isScanning = false;
            updateScanButtonUI();
            appendLog(new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), `❌ Ошибка сканера: ${data.error}`);
            break;

        case 'gameFilterSaved':
            appState.gameFilter = { mode: data.mode, tcp: data.tcp, udp: data.udp };
            updateGameFilterBadge(data.statusText, data.mode !== 'disabled');
            closeGameFilterModal();

            if (appState.zapretRunning) {
                showConfirm(
                    'Перезапустить Zapret?',
                    'Настройки Игрового фильтра сохранены. Чтобы новые диапазоны портов вступили в силу в работающем обходе, требуется перезапуск. Перезапустить сейчас?',
                    'Перезапустить',
                    () => sendCmd('saveGameFilter', { ...appState.gameFilter, restartIfRunning: true })
                );
            }
            break;

        case 'errorGlitch':
            if (!appState.settings.focusMode) {
                const mc = document.getElementById('main-container');
                mc.classList.remove('glitch-anim');
                void mc.offsetWidth;
                mc.classList.add('glitch-anim');
            }
            break;

        case 'alert':
            showAlert(data.title, data.message, data.isError);
            break;

        case 'profileSelected':
            appState.selectedProfile = data.profile;
            document.getElementById('txt-main-profile').innerText = data.profile;
            document.getElementById('modal-active-profile').innerText = data.profile;
            renderConfigCards();
            if (typeof renderModsView === 'function') renderModsView();
            break;

        case 'toggleCommandPalette':
            toggleCommandPaletteModal();
            break;

        case 'siteAnalyzed':
            if (typeof renderSiteAnalyzeResult === 'function') renderSiteAnalyzeResult(data);
            break;

        case 'siteAnalyzeError':
            if (typeof renderSiteAnalyzeError === 'function') renderSiteAnalyzeError(data.error);
            break;
    }
}

// =========================================================================
// 2. НАВИГАЦИЯ ПО ВКЛАДКАМ И СЛАЙДЯЩИЕ КАПСУЛЫ
// =========================================================================
function toggleSidebar() {
    document.getElementById('sidebar')?.classList.toggle('rail-mode');
}

function switchTab(tabId, btnEl) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-view').forEach(sec => sec.classList.remove('active'));
    document.getElementById('tab-' + tabId)?.classList.add('active');

    const navBtns = document.querySelectorAll('#sidebar-nav .nav-btn');
    navBtns.forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('active', 'font-semibold', 'text-white');
        b.classList.add('font-medium', 'text-[#CCCCCC]');
        const ico = b.querySelector('.mdl-icon');
        if (ico) {
            ico.classList.remove('text-brand', 'text-[#4CAF50]');
            ico.classList.add('text-[#888888]');
        }
    });

    const targetBtn = btnEl || Array.from(navBtns).find(b => b.getAttribute('onclick')?.includes(`'${tabId}'`));
    if (targetBtn) {
        targetBtn.classList.add('active', 'font-semibold', 'text-white');
        targetBtn.classList.remove('font-medium', 'text-[#CCCCCC]');
        const ico = targetBtn.querySelector('.mdl-icon');
        if (ico) {
            ico.classList.remove('text-[#888888]');
            ico.classList.add('text-brand');
        }

        const pill = document.getElementById('sidebar-active-pill');
        if (pill) {
            pill.style.transform = `translateY(${targetBtn.offsetTop}px)`;
        }
    }

    if (tabId === 'home') {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => drawChart());
    } else if (tabId === 'mods') {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => updateModsSlidingPill());
    } else if (tabId === 'settings') {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            const activeSetBtn = document.querySelector('.set-cat-btn.active');
            if (activeSetBtn) updateSettingsSlidingPill(activeSetBtn);
            if (typeof initGems3DShowcase === 'function') initGems3DShowcase();
            if (typeof resizeGems3DIfNeeded === 'function') resizeGems3DIfNeeded();
        });
    }
}

function updateModsSlidingPill() {
    const pill = document.getElementById('mods-seg-pill');
    const activeBtn = document.querySelector('#mods-seg-track .mods-seg-btn.active') ||
        document.getElementById('mod-tab-' + (appState.modsSubTab || 'bat'));
    if (!pill || !activeBtn) return;

    pill.style.width = `${activeBtn.offsetWidth}px`;
    pill.style.transform = `translateX(${activeBtn.offsetLeft}px)`;
}

function updateSettingsSlidingPill(targetBtn) {
    const pill = document.getElementById('settings-seg-pill');
    const btn = targetBtn || document.querySelector('#settings-seg-track .set-cat-btn.active');
    if (!pill || !btn) return;

    pill.style.width = `${btn.offsetWidth}px`;
    pill.style.transform = `translateX(${btn.offsetLeft}px)`;
}

// =========================================================================
// 3. ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ПРИ ЗАГРУЗКЕ DOM
// =========================================================================
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    runSplashSequence().catch(() => skipSplash());
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (!splashSkipped) skipSplash();
    }, 6500);

    if (window.external && typeof window.external.receiveMessage === 'function') {
        window.external.receiveMessage(rawMsg => {
            try {
                const msg = typeof rawMsg === 'string' ? JSON.parse(rawMsg) : rawMsg;
                if (msg && msg.type) {
                    handleBackendEvent(msg.type, msg.data);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Ошибка Photino message:', e);
            }
        });
    } else if (window.chrome && window.chrome.webview) {
        window.chrome.webview.addEventListener('message', e => {
            try {
                const msg = typeof e.data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(e.data) : e.data;
                if (msg && msg.type) {
                    handleBackendEvent(msg.type, msg.data);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
            }
        });
    }

    if (typeof window.runDiagnostics === 'function') {
        const origRunDiag = window.runDiagnostics;
        window.runDiagnostics = function () {
            if (typeof buildDefaultRadarNodes === 'function') buildDefaultRadarNodes();
            origRunDiag();
        };
    }

    try {
        if (typeof initReactor3D === 'function') initReactor3D();
        if (typeof initGems3DShowcase === 'function') initGems3DShowcase();
        if (typeof refreshCardsTiltEffect === 'function') refreshCardsTiltEffect();
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('3D init error:', e);
    }

    try {
        if (typeof initDiagnosticsRadarView === 'function') initDiagnosticsRadarView();
        if (typeof injectGlobe3DViewButton === 'function') injectGlobe3DViewButton();
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Radar/Globe init error:', e);
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        updateModsSlidingPill();
        updateSettingsSlidingPill();
        drawChart();
    });

    ['titlebar', 'wizard-titlebar'].forEach(id => {
        const bar = document.getElementById(id);
        if (bar) {
            bar.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
                if (e.button === 0 && !e.target.closest('button') && !e.target.closest('input')) {
                    sendCmd('windowDrag');
                }
            });
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.code === 'KeyK' || e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K' || e.key === 'л' || e.key === 'Л')) {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            toggleCommandPaletteModal();
        }
    }, true);

    window.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
        if (e.ctrlKey) {
            e.preventDefault();
        }
    }, { passive: false });

    const dropOverlay = document.getElementById('dropzone-overlay');
    let dragCounter = 0;

    window.addEventListener('dragenter', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        dragCounter++;
        if (dropOverlay) {
            dropOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
            dropOverlay.classList.add('flex');
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('dragleave', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        dragCounter--;
        if (dragCounter <= 0) {
            dragCounter = 0;
            if (dropOverlay) {
                dropOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
                dropOverlay.classList.remove('flex');
            }
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('dragover', e => e.preventDefault());

    window.addEventListener('drop', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        dragCounter = 0;
        if (dropOverlay) {
            dropOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
            dropOverlay.classList.remove('flex');
        }

        const file = e.dataTransfer?.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const base64 = String(reader.result).split(',')[1] || '';
            sendCmd('importDroppedFile', { fileName: file.name, base64 });
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    });


    drawChart();
    sendCmd('init');
});

// =========================================================================
// 4. СОХРАНЕНИЕ НАСТРОЕК, МИНИМАЛИСТИЧНЫЙ РЕЖИМ И ПОИСК ПО НАСТРОЙКАМ
// =========================================================================
let settingsSaveToastTimer = null;

function onSettingsChanged() {
    const compact = document.getElementById('set-compact-mode')?.checked ?? false;
    const focus = document.getElementById('set-focus-mode')?.checked ?? false;
    const watchdog = document.getElementById('set-watchdog-ping')?.checked ?? false;

    appState.settings.compactMode = compact;
    appState.settings.focusMode = focus;
    appState.settings.watchdogPingAutoRestart = watchdog;

    document.body.classList.toggle('compact-mode', compact);
    if (typeof renderModsView === 'function') renderModsView();
    if (typeof syncReactor3DThemeAndState === 'function') syncReactor3DThemeAndState();
    if (typeof syncGems3DWithActiveTheme === 'function') syncGems3DWithActiveTheme(appState.settings.accentTheme);

    const payload = {
        autoStart: document.getElementById('set-autostart')?.checked ?? false,
        autoStartBypass: document.getElementById('set-autostart-bypass')?.checked ?? false,
        startMinimized: document.getElementById('set-start-minimized')?.checked ?? false,
        minimizeOnClose: document.getElementById('set-minimize-close')?.checked ?? true,
        stopServicesOnExit: document.getElementById('set-stop-on-exit')?.checked ?? true,
        notificationsEnabled: document.getElementById('set-notifications')?.checked ?? true,
        alwaysOnTop: document.getElementById('set-always-on-top')?.checked ?? false,
        soundEffectsEnabled: document.getElementById('set-sound-effects')?.checked ?? true,
        skipSplashScreen: document.getElementById('set-skip-splash')?.checked ?? false,
        compactMode: compact,
        focusMode: focus,
        hardwareAcceleration: document.getElementById('set-hw-accel')?.checked ?? true,
        pingUrl: document.getElementById('set-ping-url')?.value?.trim() || 'https://dynamodb.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com',
        statsUpdateInterval: appState.settings.statsUpdateInterval || 1,
        autoRestartServices: document.getElementById('set-autorestart')?.checked ?? false,
        watchdogPingAutoRestart: watchdog,
        accentTheme: appState.settings.accentTheme || 'green',
        hostsSourceIndex: appState.settings.hostsSourceIndex ?? 2
    };

    sendCmd('saveSettings', { settings: payload });

    const toast = document.getElementById('settings-save-toast');
    if (toast) {
        toast.style.opacity = '1';
        clearTimeout(settingsSaveToastTimer);
        settingsSaveToastTimer = setTimeout(() => { toast.style.opacity = '0'; }, 1600);
    }

    setTimeout(() => {
        updateModsSlidingPill();
        updateSettingsSlidingPill();
        drawChart();
    }, 80);
}

function filterSettingsView() {
    const query = (document.getElementById('settings-search-input')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const activeCatBtn = document.querySelector('#settings-seg-track .set-cat-btn.active');
    const currentCat = window.currentSettingsCat || (activeCatBtn?.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/'([^']+)'/)?.[1] || 'all');

    const groups = document.querySelectorAll('#tab-settings [data-set-group]');
    groups.forEach(group => {
        const groupCat = group.getAttribute('data-set-group');
        const catMatches = (currentCat === 'all' || currentCat === groupCat);

        if (!catMatches) {
            group.classList.add('hidden');
            return;
        }

        if (!query) {
            group.classList.remove('hidden');
            group.querySelectorAll('[data-search]').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('hidden'));
            group.querySelectorAll('.glass-card').forEach(card => card.classList.remove('hidden'));
            const routingGrid = group.querySelector('#settings-routing-grid');
            if (routingGrid) routingGrid.classList.remove('hidden');
            return;
        }

        const innerCards = group.classList.contains('glass-card') ? [group] : Array.from(group.querySelectorAll('.glass-card'));
        let anyVisibleInGroup = false;

        innerCards.forEach(card => {
            const searchableItems = card.querySelectorAll('[data-search]');
            let visibleInCard = 0;

            searchableItems.forEach(item => {
                const hay = (item.getAttribute('data-search') + ' ' + item.innerText).toLowerCase();
                const match = hay.includes(query);
                item.classList.toggle('hidden', !match);
                if (match) visibleInCard++;
            });

            const routingGrid = card.querySelector('#settings-routing-grid');
            if (routingGrid) {
                const visRouting = routingGrid.querySelectorAll('[data-search]:not(.hidden)').length;
                routingGrid.classList.toggle('hidden', visRouting === 0);
            }

            card.classList.toggle('hidden', visibleInCard === 0);
            if (visibleInCard > 0) anyVisibleInGroup = true;
        });

        group.classList.toggle('hidden', !anyVisibleInGroup);
    });
}
