let tgClicks = 0, lastTgClick = 0;
let dsClicks = 0, lastDsClick = 0;
let lastDiagReport = null;

const DC_LOCATIONS = {
    1: 'Майами (US)',
    2: 'Амстердам (NL)',
    3: 'Майами (US)',
    4: 'Амстердам (NL)',
    5: 'Сингапур (SG)'
};

function quickFillSiteCheck(domain) {
    const input = document.getElementById('site-check-input');
    if (input) {
        input.value = domain;
        runCustomSiteCheck();
    }
}

function runCustomSiteCheck() {
    const input = document.getElementById('site-check-input');
    const raw = (input?.value || '').trim();
    if (!raw) return;

    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-site-check');
    const icon = document.getElementById('site-check-icon');
    const txt = document.getElementById('site-check-btn-txt');
    const box = document.getElementById('site-check-result-box');

    if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    if (icon) icon.classList.add('spin-anim');
    if (txt) txt.innerText = 'Анализ...';

    if (box) {
        box.className = 'rounded-lg border border-[#262626] bg-[#141414] p-3 text-xs text-[#999999] flex items-center gap-2';
        box.innerHTML = `<span class="mdl-icon text-xs spin-anim text-brand">&#xE149;</span><span>Проверяем DNS (1.1.1.1), TCP :443 и TLS SNI для <b>${raw}</b>...</span>`;
    }

    sendCmd('analyzeSite', { url: raw });
}

function renderSiteAnalyzeResult(res) {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-site-check');
    const icon = document.getElementById('site-check-icon');
    const txt = document.getElementById('site-check-btn-txt');
    const box = document.getElementById('site-check-result-box');

    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
    if (icon) icon.classList.remove('spin-anim');
    if (txt) txt.innerText = 'Проверить сайт';
    if (!box) return;

    let borderCls = 'border-[#107C10]/60 bg-[#0F200F]/60';
    let badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#107C10]/30 text-brand font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">ДОСТУПЕН ✓</span>`;
    let actionsHtml = '';

    const safeDom = (res.domain || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");
    const safeIp = (res.cleanIp || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'");

    if (res.verdictType === 'sni_block') {
        borderCls = 'border-[#F59E0B]/60 bg-[#2E2210]/40';
        badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#F59E0B]/20 text-[#FBBF24] font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">TLS SNI DPI БЛОК</span>`;
        actionsHtml = `
            <button onclick="sendCmd('quickFixAddDomain', { domain: '${safeDom}' })" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg btn-brand text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow shrink-0">
                <span class="mdl-icon text-xs">&#xE710;</span>
                <span>Добавить в белый список обхода (1 клик)</span>
            </button>
        `;
        if (res.dnsSpoofed && res.cleanIp) {
            actionsHtml += `
                <button onclick="sendCmd('quickFixAddHost', { domain: '${safeDom}', ip: '${safeIp}' })" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#222222] hover:bg-[#2C2C2C] border border-[#3E3E3E] text-xs font-semibold text-white cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-xs text-brand">&#xE8B7;</span>
                    <span>Прописать IP в Hosts</span>
                </button>
            `;
        }
    } else if (res.verdictType === 'dns_spoof') {
        borderCls = 'border-[#3B82F6]/60 bg-[#1E293B]/50';
        badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#3B82F6]/20 text-[#60A5FA] font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">ПОДМЕНА DNS</span>`;
        actionsHtml = `
            <button onclick="sendCmd('quickFixAddHost', { domain: '${safeDom}', ip: '${safeIp}' })" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg btn-brand text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow shrink-0">
                <span class="mdl-icon text-xs">&#xE8B7;</span>
                <span>Прописать чистый IP (${res.cleanIp}) в Hosts</span>
            </button>
            <button onclick="sendCmd('quickFixAddDomain', { domain: '${safeDom}' })" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#222222] hover:bg-[#2C2C2C] border border-[#3E3E3E] text-xs font-semibold text-white cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                <span class="mdl-icon text-xs text-brand">&#xE710;</span>
                <span>+ В список Zapret</span>
            </button>
        `;
    } else if (res.verdictType === 'ip_block' || res.verdictType === 'offline') {
        borderCls = 'border-[#D13438]/60 bg-[#2A1215]/50';
        badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#D13438]/25 text-[#F87171] font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">БЛОК ПО IP / ОФФЛАЙН</span>`;
    } else {
        actionsHtml = `
            <button onclick="sendCmd('quickFixAddDomain', { domain: '${safeDom}' })" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#1E1E1E] hover:bg-[#282828] border border-[#333333] text-xs font-semibold text-[#CCCCCC] hover:text-white cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                <span class="mdl-icon text-xs text-brand">&#xE710;</span>
                <span>Принудительно добавить в список обхода</span>
            </button>
        `;
    }

    box.className = `rounded-xl border ${borderCls} p-3.5 flex flex-col gap-2.5 transition-all`;
    box.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="text-sm font-bold text-white">${res.verdictTitle}</span>
                ${badgeHtml}
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] font-mono text-[#AAAAAA]">
                IP: <b class="text-white">${res.cleanIp || '—'}</b> • TLS: <b class="text-white">${(res.tlsTargetSni || '—').toUpperCase()}</b>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="text-xs text-[#CCCCCC] leading-relaxed">${res.verdictDetail}</div>
        ${actionsHtml ? `<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">${actionsHtml}</div>` : ''}
    `;
}

function renderSiteAnalyzeError(errMsg) {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-site-check');
    const icon = document.getElementById('site-check-icon');
    const txt = document.getElementById('site-check-btn-txt');
    const box = document.getElementById('site-check-result-box');

    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
    if (icon) icon.classList.remove('spin-anim');
    if (txt) txt.innerText = 'Проверить сайт';
    if (!box) return;

    box.className = 'rounded-lg border border-[#D13438]/60 bg-[#2A1215]/40 p-3 text-xs text-[#F87171]';
    box.innerText = `❌ ${errMsg}`;
}

function prepareDiagSkeletons(shimmer = false) {
    const dcs = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
    const ports = [443, 80, 5222];
    const dcGrid = document.getElementById('diag-dc-grid');

    dcGrid.innerHTML = dcs.map(dc => `
        <div class="dc-cluster-card ${shimmer ? 'shimmer-box' : ''}">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">DC ${dc}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#777777]">${DC_LOCATIONS[dc]}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="space-y-1">
                ${ports.map(p => `
                    <div class="port-badge-row">
                        <span class="text-[#888888]">:${p}</span>
                        <span class="text-[#555555] font-bold">${shimmer ? '...' : '—'}</span>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
            </div>
        </div>
    `).join('');

    const dsNodes = [
        { label: 'Gateway', desc: 'Авторизация и сокеты' },
        { label: 'Media', desc: 'Картинки и стримы' },
        { label: 'API', desc: 'Сообщения и чаты' }
    ];

    document.getElementById('diag-discord-grid').innerHTML = dsNodes.map(n => `
        <div class="dc-cluster-card ${shimmer ? 'shimmer-box' : ''}">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <div class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${n.label}</div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-[#777777]">${n.desc}</div>
                </div>
                <span class="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-[#555555]">${shimmer ? '...' : '—'}</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    `).join('');
}

function updateDiagStepPills(progressPercent, statusText) {
    const lbl = document.getElementById('diag-step-label');
    const pct = document.getElementById('diag-step-percent');
    if (lbl) lbl.innerText = statusText || 'Проверка...';
    if (pct) pct.innerText = `${progressPercent}%`;

    const thresholds = [0, 10, 30, 40, 55, 70];
    thresholds.forEach((t, idx) => {
        const el = document.getElementById('dstep-' + idx);
        if (!el) return;
        const nextT = thresholds[idx + 1] ?? 100;
        if (progressPercent >= nextT) {
            el.className = 'diag-step-pill done';
        } else if (progressPercent >= t) {
            el.className = 'diag-step-pill active';
        } else {
            el.className = 'diag-step-pill';
        }
    });
}

function runDiagnostics() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-run-diag');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.style.opacity = '0.5';
    document.getElementById('diag-btn-text').innerText = 'Сканирование...';

    const wrap = document.getElementById('diag-progress-wrap');
    wrap.classList.remove('hidden');
    wrap.classList.add('flex');
    document.getElementById('diag-progress-bar').style.width = '5%';
    updateDiagStepPills(5, 'Запуск диагностики...');

    prepareDiagSkeletons(true);
    sendCmd('runDiagnostics');
}

function getPingColorHex(latency, ok) {
    const isCb = appState.settings.colorblindMode;
    const okColor = isCb ? '#60A5FA' : '#4CAF50';
    const warnColor = isCb ? '#FFB900' : '#F59E0B';
    const errColor = isCb ? '#FF8C00' : '#EF4444';

    if (!ok || latency == null) return errColor;
    return latency < 120 ? okColor : warnColor;
}

function computeHumanVerdict(r) {
    const blocks = r.blockTypes || [];
    const app = r.appStatus || {};
    const dcResults = r.dcResults || [];
    const pingResults = r.pingResults || [];
    const dcOk = dcResults.filter(x => x.ok).length;
    const dcTot = dcResults.length;
    const pingOk = pingResults.some(p => p.ok);
    const bypass = !!app.zapretRunning;

    const isCb = appState.settings.colorblindMode;
    const successColor = isCb ? '#0078D7' : '#107C10';
    const errorColor = isCb ? '#FF8C00' : '#D13438';
    const warningColor = isCb ? '#FFB900' : '#F59E0B';

    const hasSniBlock = blocks.includes(3);
    const hasIpBlock = blocks.includes(2);

    if (app.tgWsProxyRunning)
        return { title: 'TgWsProxy активен', detail: 'Трафик Telegram идет через локальный прокси.', color: successColor };
    if (!pingOk && dcOk === 0)
        return { title: 'Нет соединения', detail: 'Ни один внешний узел не отвечает.', color: errorColor };
    if (hasSniBlock)
        return bypass
            ? { title: 'Работает (Zapret активен)', detail: 'Обнаружен DPI, но обход успешно активен.', color: successColor }
            : { title: 'Блокировка по TLS SNI (DPI)', detail: 'Провайдер режет соединение. Включите Zapret.', color: errorColor };
    if (hasIpBlock)
        return bypass
            ? { title: 'Работает через обход', detail: 'Прямой доступ закрыт, но обходчик активен.', color: successColor }
            : { title: 'IP-адреса заблокированы', detail: 'Дата-центры недоступны напрямую.', color: errorColor };
    if (dcOk >= Math.max(Math.floor(dcTot / 2), 1))
        return { title: 'Telegram доступен', detail: `Отвечает ${dcOk} из ${dcTot} проверенных портов DC.`, color: successColor };

    return { title: 'Нестабильная связь', detail: 'Часть дата-центров не отвечает на запросы.', color: warningColor };
}

function computeDiscordVerdict(r) {
    const app = r.appStatus || {};
    const bypass = !!app.zapretRunning;
    const dsPing = r.discordPing || [];

    const isCb = appState.settings.colorblindMode;
    const successColor = isCb ? '#0078D7' : '#107C10';
    const errorColor = isCb ? '#FF8C00' : '#D13438';
    const warningColor = isCb ? '#FFB900' : '#F59E0B';

    if (dsPing.length > 0 && dsPing.every(p => !p.ok))
        return bypass
            ? { title: 'Сбои соединения Discord', detail: 'Zapret запущен, но узлы не отвечают. Смените .bat профиль.', color: warningColor }
            : { title: 'Discord заблокирован', detail: 'Gateway и API не отвечают. Включите Zapret.', color: errorColor };

    if (r.udpResult && r.udpResult.blocked)
        return bypass
            ? { title: 'Discord работает (с обходом)', detail: 'UDP-голос маршрутизируется через активный Zapret.', color: successColor }
            : { title: 'Проблемы с голосом (UDP)', detail: 'Чаты доступны, но голосовые порты блокируются.', color: warningColor };

    return { title: 'Discord работает отлично', detail: 'Все магистральные серверы и голосовые порты открыты.', color: successColor };
}

function setProbeBoxStyle(iconId, valId, text, state) {
    const iconEl = document.getElementById(iconId);
    const valEl = document.getElementById(valId);
    if (!iconEl || !valEl) return;

    valEl.innerText = text;
    if (state === 'ok') {
        iconEl.className = 'w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#107C10]/20 border border-[#107C10] flex items-center justify-center text-brand shrink-0';
        valEl.className = 'text-xs font-mono font-bold text-brand truncate';
    } else if (state === 'warn') {
        iconEl.className = 'w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#F59E0B]/15 border border-[#F59E0B]/60 flex items-center justify-center text-[#F59E0B] shrink-0';
        valEl.className = 'text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#F59E0B] truncate';
    } else {
        iconEl.className = 'w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#D13438]/20 border border-[#D13438] flex items-center justify-center text-[#F44336] shrink-0';
        valEl.className = 'text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#F44336] truncate';
    }
}

function renderDiagnosticsReport(report) {
    lastDiagReport = report;

    document.getElementById('diag-status-txt').innerText = `Последняя проверка: ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()} • Все тесты выполнены.`;
    const wrap = document.getElementById('diag-progress-wrap');
    wrap.classList.add('hidden');
    wrap.classList.remove('flex');

    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-run-diag');
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.style.opacity = '1';
    document.getElementById('diag-btn-text').innerText = 'Повторить проверку';

    const copyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-copy-diag');
    if (copyBtn) {
        copyBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        copyBtn.classList.add('inline-flex');
    }

    const tgV = computeHumanVerdict(report);
    const tgCard = document.getElementById('diag-tg-card');
    tgCard.style.borderColor = tgV.color;
    tgCard.style.background = '';
    document.getElementById('diag-tg-title').innerText = tgV.title;
    document.getElementById('diag-tg-title').style.color = 'white';
    document.getElementById('diag-tg-sub').innerText = tgV.detail;

    const dsV = computeDiscordVerdict(report);
    const dsCard = document.getElementById('diag-ds-card');
    dsCard.style.borderColor = dsV.color;
    dsCard.style.background = '';
    document.getElementById('diag-ds-title').innerText = dsV.title;
    document.getElementById('diag-ds-title').style.color = 'white';
    document.getElementById('diag-ds-sub').innerText = dsV.detail;

    const st = report.appStatus || {};
    document.getElementById('proc-dot-tg').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${st.telegramRunning ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
    document.getElementById('proc-dot-ds').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${st.discordRunning ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
    document.getElementById('proc-dot-zp').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${st.zapretRunning ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
    document.getElementById('proc-dot-tw').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${st.tgWsProxyRunning ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;

    if (report.dnsResult) {
        if (report.dnsResult.spoofed) {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-dns', 'probe-val-dns', 'Подмена (Spoof)', 'err');
        } else {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-dns', 'probe-val-dns', 'Чистый (OK)', 'ok');
        }
    }

    if (report.dpiResult) {
        if (report.dpiResult.dpiDetected) {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-dpi', 'probe-val-dpi', `Блок (${report.dpiResult.sniTelegram.toUpperCase()})`, st.zapretRunning ? 'warn' : 'err');
        } else {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-dpi', 'probe-val-dpi', 'TLS Проходит', 'ok');
        }
    }

    if (report.mediaResult) {
        const spd = report.mediaResult.speedKbps || 0;
        if (report.mediaResult.throttled) {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-media', 'probe-val-media', `${spd.toFixed(0)} кбит/с (ТСПУ)`, 'warn');
        } else if (spd > 0) {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-media', 'probe-val-media', `${spd.toFixed(0)} кбит/с (ОК)`, 'ok');
        } else {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-media', 'probe-val-media', 'Нет ответа CDN', 'err');
        }
    }

    if (report.udpResult) {
        const openCnt = (report.udpResult.openPorts || []).length;
        const totCnt = (report.udpResult.testedPorts || []).length || 5;
        if (report.udpResult.blocked) {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-udp', 'probe-val-udp', st.zapretRunning ? 'Через Zapret' : 'Закрыт (0/5)', st.zapretRunning ? 'ok' : 'warn');
        } else {
            setProbeBoxStyle('probe-icon-udp', 'probe-val-udp', `Открыт (${openCnt}/${totCnt})`, 'ok');
        }
    }

    const dcList = report.dcResults || [];
    const dcOkCount = dcList.filter(x => x.ok).length;
    document.getElementById('diag-dc-summary').innerText = `Доступно портов: ${dcOkCount} из ${dcList.length}`;

    const dcs = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
    document.getElementById('diag-dc-grid').innerHTML = dcs.map(dcId => {
        const items = dcList.filter(x => x.dcId === dcId);
        const okItems = items.filter(x => x.ok && x.latencyMs != null);
        const avgPing = okItems.length > 0
            ? Math.round(okItems.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v.latencyMs, 0) / okItems.length)
            : null;

        const borderStatus = okItems.length === items.length
            ? 'border-[#107C10]/60'
            : (okItems.length > 0 ? 'border-[#F59E0B]/60' : 'border-[#D13438]/60');

        return `
        <div class="dc-cluster-card ${borderStatus}">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">DC ${dcId}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#888888]">${avgPing != null ? avgPing + ' мс' : 'Недоступен'}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-[#666666] -mt-1">${DC_LOCATIONS[dcId]}</div>
            <div class="space-y-1 mt-0.5">
                ${items.map(portItem => {
            const c = getPingColorHex(portItem.latencyMs, portItem.ok);
            const valTxt = portItem.ok && portItem.latencyMs != null ? `${Math.round(portItem.latencyMs)} мс` : 'ERR';
            return `
                    <div class="port-badge-row">
                        <span class="text-[#888888]">:${portItem.port}</span>
                        <span class="font-bold" style="color:${c}">${valTxt}</span>
                    </div>`;
        }).join('')}
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const dsList = report.discordPing || [];
    const dsOkCount = dsList.filter(x => x.ok).length;
    document.getElementById('diag-ds-summary').innerText = `Доступно узлов: ${dsOkCount} из ${dsList.length}`;

    document.getElementById('diag-discord-grid').innerHTML = dsList.map(ds => {
        const c = getPingColorHex(ds.latencyMs, ds.ok);
        return `
        <div class="dc-cluster-card">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <div class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${ds.title}</div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#777777] mt-0.5">${ds.ip || 'TCP :443'}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="text-right">
                    <div class="text-sm font-mono font-bold" style="color:${c}">${ds.pingText}</div>
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-[#666666]">${ds.ok ? 'Онлайн' : 'Таймаут'}</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const totalChecks = dcList.length + dsList.length + 2;
    const passedChecks = dcOkCount + dsOkCount + (report.dnsResult && !report.dnsResult.spoofed ? 1 : 0) + (report.mediaResult && !report.mediaResult.throttled ? 1 : 0);
    const score = totalChecks > 0 ? Math.round((passedChecks / totalChecks) * 100) : 0;

    const hBadge = document.getElementById('diag-health-badge');
    const hDot = document.getElementById('diag-health-dot');
    const hTxt = document.getElementById('diag-health-txt');
    if (hBadge && hDot && hTxt) {
        hTxt.innerText = `Здоровье сети: ${score}%`;
        if (score >= 75) {
            hBadge.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#0F240F] border border-[#107C10] font-mono text-xs text-brand font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5';
            hDot.className = 'w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full dot-online';
        } else if (score >= 40) {
            hBadge.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#2E2210] border border-[#F59E0B] font-mono text-xs text-[#F59E0B] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5';
            hDot.className = 'w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#F59E0B]';
        } else {
            hBadge.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#2A1215] border border-[#D13438] font-mono text-xs text-[#F44336] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5';
            hDot.className = 'w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full dot-offline';
        }
    }

    const recs = report.recommendations || [];
    document.getElementById('diag-recs-list').innerHTML = recs.length
        ? recs.map(rawRec => formatCleanRecommendation(rawRec)).join('')
        : `<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-[#0F200F] border border-[#107C10] text-brand font-semibold">
               <span class="mdl-icon text-sm">&#xE73E;</span>
               <span>Все проверенные узлы и протоколы работают стабильно, дополнительных действий не требуется!</span>
           </div>`;
}

function formatCleanRecommendation(rawText) {
    const clean = rawText.replace(/^[⚠️🛡📡🔵🔴🎮✓\s]+/u, '').trim().replace(/✓/g, '');
    const isWarn = rawText.includes('⚠️') || rawText.includes('🔴') || rawText.includes('БЛОКИРОВКА') || rawText.includes('Недоступен');
    const isOk = rawText.includes('доступен') || rawText.includes('активен, Discord работает');

    const borderCls = isOk
        ? 'border-[#107C10]/60 bg-[#0F200F]/60'
        : (isWarn ? 'border-[#D13438]/60 bg-[#2A1215]/40' : 'border-[#2A2A2A] bg-[#161616]');
    const iconColor = isOk ? 'text-brand' : (isWarn ? 'text-[#F44336]' : 'text-[#60A5FA]');
    const glyph = isOk ? '&#xE73E;' : (isWarn ? '&#xE7BA;' : '&#xE946;');

    let actionBtnHtml = '';
    if (rawText.includes('SNI-БЛОКИРОВКА') || rawText.includes('DISCORD API: Недоступен') || rawText.includes('Звонки не работают без Zapret')) {
        actionBtnHtml = !appState.isRunning
            ? `<button onclick="diagQuickAction('startBypass')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg btn-brand text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow">
                   <span class="mdl-icon text-[10px]">&#xE7E8;</span><span>Включить обход</span>
               </button>`
            : `<button onclick="diagQuickAction('scanConfigs')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#222222] hover:bg-[#2C2C2C] border border-[#3A3A3A] text-white text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition">
                   <span class="mdl-icon text-[10px] text-brand">&#xE721;</span><span>Подобрать .bat</span>
               </button>`;
    } else if (rawText.includes('ТСПУ-ЗАМЕДЛЕНИЕ МЕДИА')) {
        actionBtnHtml = !appState.proxyRunning
            ? `<button onclick="diagQuickAction('enableProxy')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg btn-brand text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow">
                   <span class="mdl-icon text-[10px]">&#xE709;</span><span>Запустить с TgWsProxy</span>
               </button>`
            : `<button onclick="diagQuickAction('scanConfigs')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#222222] hover:bg-[#2C2C2C] border border-[#3A3A3A] text-white text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition">
                   <span class="mdl-icon text-[10px] text-brand">&#xE721;</span><span>Сменить профиль</span>
               </button>`;
    } else if (rawText.includes('DNS-СПУФИНГ')) {
        actionBtnHtml = `<button onclick="diagQuickAction('applyHosts')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#222222] hover:bg-[#2C2C2C] border border-[#107C10] text-brand text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition">
                             <span class="mdl-icon text-[10px]">&#xE945;</span><span>Применить обходной Hosts</span>
                         </button>`;
    }

    return `
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border ${borderCls}">
        <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
            <span class="mdl-icon text-sm ${iconColor} mt-0.5 shrink-0">${glyph}</span>
            <span class="whitespace-pre-line leading-relaxed text-[#E0E0E0]">${clean}</span>
        </div>
        ${actionBtnHtml}
    </div>`;
}

function diagQuickAction(act) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const navBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn');

    if (act === 'startBypass') {
        switchTab('home', navBtns[0]);
        if (!appState.isRunning) onMainToggleClick();
    } else if (act === 'scanConfigs') {
        switchTab('home', navBtns[0]);
        openConfigModal();
    } else if (act === 'enableProxy') {
        document.getElementById('chk-proxy').checked = true;
        onModuleToggleChange();
        switchTab('home', navBtns[0]);
        if (!appState.isRunning) onMainToggleClick();
        else sendCmd('restartBypass');
    } else if (act === 'applyHosts') {
        switchTab('settings', navBtns[3]);
        updateSystemHosts();
    }
}

async function copyDiagnosticsReport() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    if (!lastDiagReport) return;

    const dcOk = (lastDiagReport.dcResults || []).filter(x => x.ok).length;
    const dcTot = (lastDiagReport.dcResults || []).length;
    const dsOk = (lastDiagReport.discordPing || []).filter(x => x.ok).length;
    const dsTot = (lastDiagReport.discordPing || []).length;
    const spd = lastDiagReport.mediaResult?.speedKbps?.toFixed(0) || '0';

    const lines = [
        `=== ОТЧЁТ ДИАГНОСТИКИ ZAPRET FOR ADHD ===`,
        `Время: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
        `Telegram DC: ${dcOk}/${dcTot} портов доступно`,
        `Discord Nodes: ${dsOk}/${dsTot} узлов доступно`,
        `DNS Spoofing: ${lastDiagReport.dnsResult?.spoofed ? 'Обнаружен' : 'Чисто'}`,
        `DPI / TLS SNI: ${lastDiagReport.dpiResult?.dpiDetected ? 'Блокируется (' + lastDiagReport.dpiResult.sniTelegram + ')' : 'ОК'}`,
        `Скорость медиа CDN: ${spd} kbps`,
        `Активный профиль: ${appState.selectedProfile} (Zapret: ${appState.zapretRunning ? 'ВКЛ' : 'ВЫКЛ'})`
    ];

    try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(lines.join('\r\n'));
        const txt = document.getElementById('txt-copy-diag');
        if (txt) {
            txt.innerText = 'Скопировано!';
            setTimeout(() => txt.innerText = 'Отчёт', 1800);
        }
    } catch { }
}

function onTgEmojiClick() {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastTgClick > 1500) tgClicks = 0;
    tgClicks++;
    lastTgClick = now;

    if (tgClicks >= 5) {
        tgClicks = 0;
        sendCmd('playSuccess');
        const title = document.getElementById('diag-tg-title');
        const sub = document.getElementById('diag-tg-sub');
        const card = document.getElementById('diag-tg-card');
        const emoji = document.getElementById('diag-tg-emoji');

        title.innerText = 'Telegram (Режим Дурова)';
        title.style.color = '#00E5FF';
        sub.innerText = 'Свободу интернету! Трафик летит напрямую.';
        card.style.borderColor = '#00E5FF';
        card.style.background = '#001A22';

        emoji.classList.remove('easter-fly');
        void emoji.offsetWidth;
        emoji.classList.add('easter-fly');
    }
}

function onDiscordEmojiClick() {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastDsClick > 1500) dsClicks = 0;
    dsClicks++;
    lastDsClick = now;

    if (dsClicks >= 5) {
        dsClicks = 0;
        sendCmd('playSuccess');
        const title = document.getElementById('diag-ds-title');
        const sub = document.getElementById('diag-ds-sub');
        const card = document.getElementById('diag-ds-card');
        const emoji = document.getElementById('diag-ds-emoji');

        title.innerText = 'Discord (Режим турбо-деда)';
        title.style.color = '#FF00FF';
        sub.innerText = 'Никакие блокировки больше не страшны!';
        card.style.borderColor = '#FF00FF';
        card.style.background = '#1A001A';

        emoji.classList.remove('easter-spin');
        void emoji.offsetWidth;
        emoji.classList.add('easter-spin');
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: wwwroot\js\gems3d.js
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

// =========================================================================
// МОДУЛЬ: 3D WEBGL КРИСТАЛЛЫ ТЕМ 2.0 (КОНТРАСТНЫЕ ГРАНИ БЕЗ ЗАСВЕТА И КОЛЕЦ)
// Файл: wwwroot/js/gems3d.js
// =========================================================================

let gems3DInstances = [];
let gems3DLoopStarted = false;

const GEMS_3D_CONFIG = [
    { id: 'green', title: 'Изумруд', eng: 'EMERALD', color: 0x16A34A, specular: 0x86EFAC, edge: 0x4ADE80, shape: 'emerald' },
    { id: 'blue', title: 'Сапфир', eng: 'SAPPHIRE', color: 0x2563EB, specular: 0x93C5FD, edge: 0x60A5FA, shape: 'sapphire' },
    { id: 'purple', title: 'Аметист', eng: 'AMETHYST', color: 0x7C3AED, specular: 0xDDD6FE, edge: 0xC084FC, shape: 'amethyst' },
    { id: 'amber', title: 'Топаз', eng: 'TOPAZ', color: 0xD97706, specular: 0xFDE68A, edge: 0xFBBF24, shape: 'topaz' },
    { id: 'pink', title: 'Морганит', eng: 'MORGANITE', color: 0xDB2777, specular: 0xFBCFE8, edge: 0xF472B6, shape: 'morganite' }
];

// Ювелирные 3D-формы с хорошо читаемыми крупными гранями
function buildUniqueGemGeometry(shapeType) {
    switch (shapeType) {
        case 'emerald': {
            // Изумрудный восьмигранный кристалл
            const g = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.36, 0.36, 0.88, 6, 1);
            g.scale(1.05, 1.0, 0.78);
            return g;
        }
        case 'sapphire': {
            // Классический двухвершинный кристалл (Октаэдр)
            const g = new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.64, 0);
            g.scale(0.82, 1.25, 0.82);
            return g;
        }
        case 'amethyst': {
            // 12-гранный ювелирный додекаэдр (четкие пятиугольные грани)
            const g = new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(0.58, 0);
            g.scale(0.96, 1.12, 0.96);
            return g;
        }
        case 'topaz': {
            // Бриллиантовая огранка (широкая корона сверху, острый павильон снизу)
            const g = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.58, 0.94, 6);
            g.rotateX(Math.PI);
            return g;
        }
        case 'morganite':
        default: {
            // Двадцатигранный кристалл (Икосаэдр)
            const g = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.58, 0);
            g.scale(0.95, 1.10, 0.95);
            return g;
        }
    }
}

function createGemInstanceForWrap(wrap) {
    if (!wrap || wrap._gems3dMounted) return;

    const oldCanvas = wrap.querySelector('canvas');
    if (!oldCanvas) return;

    wrap._gems3dMounted = true;

    const w = Math.max(wrap.clientWidth, 440);
    const h = Math.max(wrap.clientHeight, 108);

    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(30, w / h, 0.1, 100);
    camera.position.set(0, 0.45, 6.2);
    camera.lookAt(0, -0.06, 0);

    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: true, antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
    renderer.setSize(w, h);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.domElement.className = 'gem-field-canvas';
    renderer.domElement.style.width = '100%';
    renderer.domElement.style.height = '108px';
    renderer.domElement.style.display = 'block';

    oldCanvas.style.display = 'none';
    wrap.insertBefore(renderer.domElement, oldCanvas);

    const labelsContainer = document.createElement('div');
    labelsContainer.className = 'absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none overflow-hidden';
    labelsContainer.style.zIndex = '4';
    wrap.appendChild(labelsContainer);

    // Сбалансированный контрастный свет БЕЗ пересвета:
    // слабый Ambient (чтобы теневые грани были глубокими и темными) + боковой ключевой свет
    scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.26));

    const keyLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.95);
    keyLight.position.set(3.5, 5.0, 4.5);
    scene.add(keyLight);

    const backRimLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x94A3B8, 0.45);
    backRimLight.position.set(-4.0, -2.5, -2.0);
    scene.add(backRimLight);

    const crystalGroups = [];
    const step = (Math.PI * 2) / GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length;

    GEMS_3D_CONFIG.forEach((gem, idx) => {
        const group = new THREE.Group();
        const geom = buildUniqueGemGeometry(gem.shape);

        // 1. Ювелирный граненый материал (MeshPhongMaterial + flatShading дает идеальный контраст граней)
        const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
            color: gem.color,
            specular: gem.specular,
            shininess: 85,
            flatShading: true,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.95
        });
        const bodyMesh = new THREE.Mesh(geom, bodyMat);
        group.add(bodyMesh);

        // 2. Четкая обводка ТОЛЬКО внешних ребер огранки (EdgesGeometry вместо диагональной сетки)
        const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geom, 12);
        const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
            color: gem.edge,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.65
        });
        const edgesMesh = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
        edgesMesh.scale.set(1.006, 1.006, 1.006);
        group.add(edgesMesh);

        const labelEl = document.createElement('div');
        labelEl.innerText = gem.title;
        labelsContainer.appendChild(labelEl);

        scene.add(group);
        crystalGroups.push({
            data: gem,
            index: idx,
            baseAngle: idx * step,
            group,
            bodyMesh,
            edgesMesh,
            bodyMat,
            edgesMat,
            labelEl
        });
    });

    const instance = {
        wrapEl: wrap,
        scene,
        camera,
        renderer,
        crystalGroups,
        currentAngle: 0,
        targetAngle: 0,
        isDragging: false,
        dragStartX: 0,
        dragMoved: 0
    };

    const glCanvas = renderer.domElement;
    glCanvas.style.touchAction = 'none';

    glCanvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
        instance.isDragging = true;
        instance.dragStartX = e.clientX;
        instance.dragMoved = 0;
        try { glCanvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch { }
    });

    glCanvas.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
        if (!instance.isDragging) return;
        const dx = e.clientX - instance.dragStartX;
        instance.dragStartX = e.clientX;
        instance.dragMoved += Math.abs(dx);
        instance.targetAngle += dx * 0.011;
    });

    glCanvas.addEventListener('pointerup', e => {
        if (!instance.isDragging) return;
        instance.isDragging = false;
        try { glCanvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch { }

        if (instance.dragMoved < 6) {
            const rect = glCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
            const relX = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
            if (relX < 0.38) triggerGemStep(-1);
            else if (relX > 0.62) triggerGemStep(1);
        } else {
            const stepAngle = (Math.PI * 2) / GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length;
            const rawIdx = Math.round(-instance.targetAngle / stepAngle);
            const normIdx = ((rawIdx % GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length) + GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length) % GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length;
            const chosen = GEMS_3D_CONFIG[normIdx];
            if (chosen) applyGemSelectionEverywhere(chosen.id);
        }
    });

    glCanvas.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        triggerGemStep(e.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1);
    }, { passive: false });

    gems3DInstances.push(instance);
}

function triggerGemStep(dir) {
    const curTheme = (appState?.settings?.accentTheme || 'green');
    const norm = (curTheme === 'crimson' || curTheme === 'cyan') ? 'pink' : curTheme;
    const idx = Math.max(0, GEMS_3D_CONFIG.findIndex(g => g.id === norm));
    const nextIdx = (idx + dir + GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length) % GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length;
    applyGemSelectionEverywhere(GEMS_3D_CONFIG[nextIdx].id);
}

function applyGemSelectionEverywhere(themeId) {
    if (typeof window.selectAccentTheme === 'function') {
        window.selectAccentTheme(themeId);
    }
    if (typeof window.selectWizTheme === 'function') {
        try { window.selectWizTheme(themeId); } catch { }
    }
    syncGems3DWithActiveTheme(themeId, false);
}

function initGems3DShowcase() {
    if (typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;
    if (appState?.settings?.hardwareAcceleration === false) return;

    const wraps = document.querySelectorAll('.gem-field-wrap');
    wraps.forEach(w => createGemInstanceForWrap(w));

    ['selectAccentTheme', 'selectWizTheme'].forEach(fnName => {
        if (typeof window[fnName] === 'function' && !window['_' + fnName + '_hooked']) {
            const origFn = window[fnName];
            window['_' + fnName + '_hooked'] = true;
            window[fnName] = function (themeName, skipSave = false) {
                origFn(themeName, skipSave);
                syncGems3DWithActiveTheme(themeName, false);
                if (typeof syncReactor3DThemeAndState === 'function') syncReactor3DThemeAndState();
            };
        }
    });

    syncGems3DWithActiveTheme(appState?.settings?.accentTheme || 'green', true);

    if (!gems3DLoopStarted) {
        gems3DLoopStarted = true;
        animateGems3D();
    }
}

function resizeGems3DIfNeeded() {
    gems3DInstances.forEach(inst => {
        const w = inst.wrapEl.clientWidth;
        const h = inst.wrapEl.clientHeight || 108;
        if (w > 60 && h > 40) {
            const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
            if (Math.abs(inst.renderer.domElement.width / dpr - w) > 2) {
                inst.renderer.setSize(w, h);
                inst.camera.aspect = w / h;
                inst.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            }
        }
    });
}

function syncGems3DWithActiveTheme(themeId, instant = false) {
    const norm = (themeId === 'crimson' || themeId === 'cyan') ? 'pink' : (themeId || 'green');
    const idx = GEMS_3D_CONFIG.findIndex(g => g.id === norm);
    if (idx < 0) return;

    const step = (Math.PI * 2) / GEMS_3D_CONFIG.length;
    const desired = -idx * step;

    gems3DInstances.forEach(inst => {
        let diff = (desired - inst.targetAngle) % (Math.PI * 2);
        if (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
        if (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;

        inst.targetAngle += diff;
        if (instant) inst.currentAngle = inst.targetAngle;
    });
}

function animateGems3D() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animateGems3D);

    const radiusX = 2.30;
    const radiusZ = 1.25;
    const now = performance.now();

    gems3DInstances.forEach(inst => {
        const w = inst.wrapEl.clientWidth;
        const h = inst.wrapEl.clientHeight || 108;
        if (w < 40) return;

        const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
        if (Math.abs(inst.renderer.domElement.width / dpr - w) > 2) {
            inst.renderer.setSize(w, h);
            inst.camera.aspect = w / h;
            inst.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        }

        inst.currentAngle += (inst.targetAngle - inst.currentAngle) * 0.12;

        inst.crystalGroups.forEach(item => {
            const a = item.baseAngle + inst.currentAngle;
            const x = Math.sin(a) * radiusX;
            const z = Math.cos(a) * radiusZ;
            const depthNorm = (z + radiusZ) / (radiusZ * 2);
            const isFront = depthNorm > 0.92;

            const bobY = Math.sin(now * 0.0025 + item.index) * 0.04 + 0.14;
            item.group.position.set(x, bobY, z);

            const s = 0.45 + depthNorm * 0.52;
            item.group.scale.set(s, s, s);

            // Плавное вращение камня с легким наклоном, чтобы каждая грань переливалась светом
            item.bodyMesh.rotation.y += 0.012 + depthNorm * 0.012;
            item.bodyMesh.rotation.x = 0.22 + Math.sin(now * 0.0014 + item.index) * 0.12;
            item.edgesMesh.rotation.copy(item.bodyMesh.rotation);

            item.bodyMat.opacity = 0.32 + depthNorm * 0.66;
            item.edgesMat.opacity = isFront ? 0.85 : 0.22;

            const labelPos3D = new THREE.Vector3(x, -0.46, z);
            labelPos3D.project(inst.camera);
            const screenX = (labelPos3D.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * w;
            const screenY = (-(labelPos3D.y * 0.5) + 0.5) * h;

            item.labelEl.style.left = `${screenX}px`;
            item.labelEl.style.top = `${Math.min(h - 13, screenY)}px`;
            item.labelEl.style.opacity = String(0.38 + depthNorm * 0.62);
            item.labelEl.style.transform = `translate(-50%, -50%) scale(${0.82 + depthNorm * 0.22})`;

            if (isFront) {
                item.labelEl.className = 'absolute px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-[#141418] border border-[var(--accent-light)] text-white font-mono text-[11px] font-bold shadow whitespace-nowrap';
            } else {
                item.labelEl.className = 'absolute px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-[#0E0E12]/80 border border-[#26262E] text-[#888888] font-mono text-[10px] whitespace-nowrap';
            }
        });

        inst.renderer.render(inst.scene, inst.camera);
    });
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: wwwroot\js\globe3d.js
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

// =========================================================================
// МОДУЛЬ: 3D ГОЛОГРАФИЧЕСКИЙ ГЛОБУС МАРШРУТИЗАЦИИ В ДИАГНОСТИКЕ (THREE.JS)
// Файл: wwwroot/js/globe3d.js
// =========================================================================

let diagGlobe = {
    initialized: false,
    scene: null,
    camera: null,
    renderer: null,
    globeGroup: null,
    arcsGroup: null,
    pulses: [],
    isDragging: false,
    prevX: 0,
    prevY: 0,
    velX: 0.0025,
    velY: 0
};
function latLonToVec3(lat, lon, radius) {
    const phi = (90 - lat) * (Math.PI / 180);
    const theta = (lon + 180) * (Math.PI / 180);
    return new THREE.Vector3(
        -radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta),
        radius * Math.cos(phi),
        radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta)
    );
}

const GLOBE_NODES = [
    { id: 'user', name: 'Ваш ПК (РФ)',     lat: 55.75, lon: 37.61, isOrigin: true },
    { id: 'dc2',  name: 'Амстердам (DC2/4)', lat: 52.37, lon: 4.89,  ok: true },
    { id: 'dc1',  name: 'Майами (DC1/3)',   lat: 25.76, lon: -80.19, ok: true },
    { id: 'dc5',  name: 'Сингапур (DC5)',   lat: 1.35,  lon: 103.82, ok: true },
    { id: 'ds',   name: 'Франкфурт (Discord)', lat: 50.11, lon: 8.68, ok: true }
];

function injectGlobe3DViewButton() {
    const btnRadar = document.getElementById('btn-diag-view-radar');
    if (!btnRadar || document.getElementById('btn-diag-view-globe')) return;

    const track = btnRadar.parentElement;
    const btnGlobe = document.createElement('button');
    btnGlobe.id = 'btn-diag-view-globe';
    btnGlobe.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#888888] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
    btnGlobe.innerHTML = `<span class="mdl-icon text-[10px] text-[#60A5FA]">&#xE774;</span><span>3D Глобус</span>`;
    btnGlobe.onclick = () => switchDiagViewWithGlobe('globe');
    track.appendChild(btnGlobe);

    // Вставляем контейнер 3D-глобуса внутрь той же карточки, где лежат #diag-cards-view-wrap и #diag-radar-wrap
    const radarWrap = document.getElementById('diag-radar-wrap');
    if (radarWrap && !document.getElementById('diag-globe3d-wrap')) {
        const globeWrap = document.createElement('div');
        globeWrap.id = 'diag-globe3d-wrap';
        globeWrap.className = 'hidden flex-col items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden pt-1';
        globeWrap.innerHTML = `
            <div class="w-full flex items-center justify-between mb-2 px-1 z-10">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full dot-online"></span>
                    <span class="text-xs font-bold text-[#E0E0E0] uppercase tracking-wider">Межконтинентальная 3D-карта маршрутов (Telegram DC 1–5 & Discord)</span>
                </div>
                <span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-[#888888]">Зажмите ЛКМ для вращения 3D-сферы</span>
            </div>

            <div class="relative w-full h-[290px] rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-b from-[#07090E] to-[#0D131F] border border-[#222938] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                <div id="diag-globe3d-canvas-host" class="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing"></div>

                <!-- Плашка-легенда узлов в углу 3D-экрана -->
                <div class="absolute left-3 top-3 bg-[#0B0F17]/85 border border-[#243044] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-[10.5px] font-mono text-[#CCCCCC] space-y-1 pointer-events-none backdrop-blur-sm">
                    <div class="text-[10px] text-[#777777] uppercase font-bold mb-1">Активные магистрали:</div>
                    <div id="globe-node-list" class="space-y-1"></div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="w-full flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-[#999999] mt-2.5 px-1">
                <span>📍 Точка исхода: Ваш маршрутизатор</span>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <span><b class="text-[#60A5FA]">●</b> Ваш ПК</span>
                    <span><b class="text-brand">―●</b> Канал открыт (OK)</span>
                    <span><b class="text-[#EF4444]">―✕</b> Блокировка / Таймаут</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
        radarWrap.parentNode.insertBefore(globeWrap, radarWrap.nextSibling);
    }

    // Расширяем штатный переключатель setDiagViewMode из radar.js, чтобы все 3 кнопки работали синхронно
    if (typeof window.setDiagViewMode === 'function' && !window._globeHooked) {
        const origSetMode = window.setDiagViewMode;
        window._globeHooked = true;
        window.setDiagViewMode = function (mode) {
            const globeWrap = document.getElementById('diag-globe3d-wrap');
            const gBtn = document.getElementById('btn-diag-view-globe');
            if (globeWrap) {
                globeWrap.classList.add('hidden');
                globeWrap.classList.remove('flex');
            }
            if (gBtn) {
                gBtn.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#888888] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
            }
            origSetMode(mode);
        };
    }
}

function switchDiagViewWithGlobe(mode) {
    if (mode !== 'globe') {
        if (typeof setDiagViewMode === 'function') setDiagViewMode(mode);
        return;
    }

    sendCmd('playClick');

    // Скрываем ТОЛЬКО внутренние блоки (#diag-cards-view-wrap и #diag-radar-wrap), НЕ пряча родительский .glass-card!
    const cardsWrap = document.getElementById('diag-cards-view-wrap');
    const radarWrap = document.getElementById('diag-radar-wrap');
    const globeWrap = document.getElementById('diag-globe3d-wrap');

    if (cardsWrap) cardsWrap.classList.add('hidden');
    if (radarWrap) {
        radarWrap.classList.add('hidden');
        radarWrap.classList.remove('flex');
    }
    if (globeWrap) {
        globeWrap.classList.remove('hidden');
        globeWrap.classList.add('flex');
    }

    ['btn-diag-view-cards', 'btn-diag-view-radar'].forEach(id => {
        const b = document.getElementById(id);
        if (b) b.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#888888] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
    });

    const gBtn = document.getElementById('btn-diag-view-globe');
    if (gBtn) {
        gBtn.className = 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-[#262626] text-white text-[11px] font-semibold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        initDiagGlobe3D();
        rebuildGlobe3DArcs();
    });
}

function initDiagGlobe3D() {
    if (diagGlobe.initialized) return;
    if (typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;

    const host = document.getElementById('diag-globe3d-canvas-host');
    if (!host) return;

    const w = host.clientWidth || 720;
    const h = host.clientHeight || 290;

    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(38, w / h, 0.1, 100);
    camera.position.z = 5.2;

    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: true, antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
    renderer.setSize(w, h);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    host.innerHTML = '';
    host.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

    const globeGroup = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(globeGroup);

    // 1. Темное ядро Земли
    const coreGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.58, 36, 36);
    const coreMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x0A0F18, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95 });
    globeGroup.add(new THREE.Mesh(coreGeo, coreMat));

    // 2. Голографическая сетка параллелей и меридианов
    const wireGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.60, 28, 20);
    const wireMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x1E3A5F, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45 });
    globeGroup.add(new THREE.Mesh(wireGeo, wireMat));

    // 3. Точечная матрица поверхности планеты (эффект голограммы материков)
    const dotsCount = 360;
    const dotPositions = new Float32Array(dotsCount * 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < dotsCount; i++) {
        const phi = Math.acos(-1 + (2 * i) / dotsCount);
        const theta = Math.sqrt(dotsCount * Math.PI) * phi;
        const r = 1.61;
        dotPositions[i * 3] = r * Math.cos(theta) * Math.sin(phi);
        dotPositions[i * 3 + 1] = r * Math.sin(theta) * Math.sin(phi);
        dotPositions[i * 3 + 2] = r * Math.cos(phi);
    }
    const dotsGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    dotsGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(dotPositions, 3));
    const dotsMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x38BDF8, size: 0.028, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 });
    globeGroup.add(new THREE.Points(dotsGeo, dotsMat));

    // 4. Внешнее орбитальное кольцо атмосферы
    const ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(1.76, 1.78, 64);
    const ringMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x3B82F6, transparent: true, opacity: 0.22, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    scene.add(new THREE.Mesh(ringGeo, ringMat));

    const arcsGroup = new THREE.Group();
    globeGroup.add(arcsGroup);

    // Начальный поворот лицом к Европе / РФ
    globeGroup.rotation.y = -0.65;
    globeGroup.rotation.x = 0.32;

    const canvasEl = renderer.domElement;
    canvasEl.style.touchAction = 'none';

    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        diagGlobe.isDragging = true;
        diagGlobe.prevX = e.clientX;
        diagGlobe.prevY = e.clientY;
        diagGlobe.velX = 0;
        diagGlobe.velY = 0;
        try { canvasEl.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch { }
    });

    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
        if (!diagGlobe.isDragging) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();

        const dx = e.clientX - diagGlobe.prevX;
        const dy = e.clientY - diagGlobe.prevY;
        diagGlobe.prevX = e.clientX;
        diagGlobe.prevY = e.clientY;

        diagGlobe.velX = dx * 0.0065;
        diagGlobe.velY = dy * 0.0065;

        applyGlobeQuaternionDelta(diagGlobe.velX, diagGlobe.velY);
    });

    const stopGlobeDrag = (e) => {
        if (!diagGlobe.isDragging) return;
        diagGlobe.isDragging = false;
        try { canvasEl.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch { }
    };

    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointerup', stopGlobeDrag);
    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointercancel', stopGlobeDrag);

    Object.assign(diagGlobe, {
        initialized: true,
        scene,
        camera,
        renderer,
        globeGroup,
        arcsGroup
    });

    rebuildGlobe3DArcs();
    animateDiagGlobe3D();
}

function rebuildGlobe3DArcs() {
    if (!diagGlobe.initialized) return;

    while (diagGlobe.arcsGroup.children.length > 0) {
        const ch = diagGlobe.arcsGroup.children[0];
        diagGlobe.arcsGroup.remove(ch);
    }
    diagGlobe.pulses = [];

    const radius = 1.62;
    const originPos = latLonToVec3(GLOBE_NODES[0].lat, GLOBE_NODES[0].lon, radius);

    // Маяк пользователя (синий)
    const userDot = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.06, 14, 14),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x60A5FA })
    );
    userDot.position.copy(originPos);
    diagGlobe.arcsGroup.add(userDot);

    const accentHex = typeof getThemeHexNumber === 'function' ? getThemeHexNumber() : 0x4CAF50;
    const legendHtml = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < GLOBE_NODES.length; i++) {
        const node = GLOBE_NODES[i];
        const targetPos = latLonToVec3(node.lat, node.lon, radius);
        const colorHex = node.ok ? accentHex : 0xEF4444;

        const dcDot = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.055, 14, 14),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colorHex })
        );
        dcDot.position.copy(targetPos);
        diagGlobe.arcsGroup.add(dcDot);

        const midPos = originPos.clone().add(targetPos).multiplyScalar(0.5).normalize().multiplyScalar(radius + 0.52);
        const curve = new THREE.QuadraticBezierCurve3(originPos, midPos, targetPos);
        const points = curve.getPoints(40);
        const lineGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
        const lineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: colorHex, transparent: true, opacity: 0.75 });
        diagGlobe.arcsGroup.add(new THREE.Line(lineGeo, lineMat));

        const pulseMesh = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.042, 10, 10),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xFFFFFF })
        );
        diagGlobe.arcsGroup.add(pulseMesh);
        diagGlobe.pulses.push({
            mesh: pulseMesh,
            curve,
            t: (i * 0.23) % 1.0,
            speed: node.ok ? 0.011 : 0.0035
        });

        const dotCls = node.ok ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#EF4444]';
        const statusStr = node.ok ? 'ONLINE' : 'BLOCK';
        legendHtml.push(`<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><span>${node.name}</span><span class="${dotCls} font-bold">${statusStr}</span></div>`);
    }

    const legEl = document.getElementById('globe-node-list');
    if (legEl) legEl.innerHTML = legendHtml.join('');
}

function syncGlobe3DWithDiagReport(report) {
    if (!report) return;
    const dcs = report.dcResults || [];
    const ds = report.discordPing || [];

    const dc13 = dcs.filter(x => x.dcId === 1 || x.dcId === 3);
    const dc24 = dcs.filter(x => x.dcId === 2 || x.dcId === 4);
    const dc5 = dcs.filter(x => x.dcId === 5);

    if (dc24.length) GLOBE_NODES[1].ok = dc24.some(x => x.ok);
    if (dc13.length) GLOBE_NODES[2].ok = dc13.some(x => x.ok);
    if (dc5.length)  GLOBE_NODES[3].ok = dc5.some(x => x.ok);
    if (ds.length)   GLOBE_NODES[4].ok = ds.some(x => x.ok);

    rebuildGlobe3DArcs();
}

function applyGlobeQuaternionDelta(deltaX, deltaY) {
    if (!diagGlobe.globeGroup) return;

    const qY = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), deltaX);
    const qX = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), deltaY);

    diagGlobe.globeGroup.quaternion.premultiply(qY);
    diagGlobe.globeGroup.quaternion.premultiply(qX);
}

function animateDiagGlobe3D() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animateDiagGlobe3D);
    if (!diagGlobe.initialized) return;

    const wrap = document.getElementById('diag-globe3d-wrap');
    if (!wrap || wrap.classList.contains('hidden')) return;

    const host = document.getElementById('diag-globe3d-canvas-host');
    if (host && host.clientWidth > 100) {
        const w = host.clientWidth;
        const h = host.clientHeight || 290;
        if (Math.abs(diagGlobe.renderer.domElement.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1) - w) > 5) {
            diagGlobe.renderer.setSize(w, h);
            diagGlobe.camera.aspect = w / h;
            diagGlobe.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        }
    }

    // Свободная инерция после отпускания мыши + легкое авто-вращение
    if (!diagGlobe.isDragging) {
        diagGlobe.velX = diagGlobe.velX * 0.94 + 0.0022 * 0.06;
        diagGlobe.velY = diagGlobe.velY * 0.92;
        applyGlobeQuaternionDelta(diagGlobe.velX, diagGlobe.velY);
    }

    diagGlobe.pulses.forEach(p => {
        p.t += p.speed;
        if (p.t > 1) p.t = 0;
        const pos = p.curve.getPoint(p.t);
        p.mesh.position.copy(pos);
    });

    diagGlobe.renderer.render(diagGlobe.scene, diagGlobe.camera);
}

// =========================================================================
// 1. ЛОГИКА ВКЛАДКИ 1: ГЛАВНАЯ (HOMEVIEW)

let sessionStartTime = null;
let sessionTimerId = null;
let watchdogFailCount = 0;
let lastProgressLogEl = null;
let currentLogFilter = 'all';

function updatePingDisplay(pingMs) {
    const pingEl = document.getElementById('stat-ping');
    const badgeEl = document.getElementById('ping-quality-badge');

    if (pingMs < 0) {
        pingEl.innerText = '—';
        pingEl.style.color = '#D13438';
        badgeEl.innerText = 'Недоступен';
        badgeEl.className = 'px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-mono font-semibold bg-[#2A1215] text-[#F44336]';
        return;
    }

    pingEl.innerText = pingMs;
    if (pingMs < 80) {
        pingEl.style.color = 'var(--accent-light)';
        badgeEl.innerText = 'Отлично';
        badgeEl.className = 'px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-mono font-semibold bg-[#0F240F] text-brand';
    } else if (pingMs < 150) {
        pingEl.style.color = '#FF8C00';
        badgeEl.innerText = 'Норма';
        badgeEl.className = 'px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-mono font-semibold bg-[#2E2210] text-[#F59E0B]';
    } else {
        pingEl.style.color = '#D13438';
        badgeEl.innerText = 'Высокий';
        badgeEl.className = 'px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-mono font-semibold bg-[#2A1215] text-[#F44336]';
    }
}

function formatUptime(ms) {
    const totalSec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
    const h = String(Math.floor(totalSec / 3600)).padStart(2, '0');
    const m = String(Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
    const s = String(totalSec % 60).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
}

function updateSessionBadgeTick() {
    const badge = document.getElementById('session-badge');
    const dot = document.getElementById('session-dot');
    const txt = document.getElementById('session-text');

    if (appState.isRunning && sessionStartTime) {
        const elapsed = Date.now() - sessionStartTime;
        badge.className = 'mt-2.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#0F240F] border border-[#107C10] flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono text-white transition-all';
        dot.className = 'w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full dot-online';
        txt.innerText = `АКТИВЕН • ${formatUptime(elapsed)}`;

        if (appState.settings.watchdogPingAutoRestart && Math.floor(elapsed / 1000) % 25 === 0) {
            sendCmd('ping');
        }
    } else {
        badge.className = 'mt-2.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#181818] border border-[#2A2A2A] flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono text-[#888888] transition-all';
        dot.className = 'w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#555555]';
        txt.innerText = 'ОЖИДАНИЕ ЗАПУСКА';
    }
}

function onMainToggleClick() {
    if (!appState.isRunning) {
        const useZapret = document.getElementById('chk-zapret').checked;
        const useProxy = document.getElementById('chk-proxy').checked;

        if (useZapret && !appState.firstTimeScanPrompted) {
            appState.firstTimeScanPrompted = true;
            sendCmd('markFirstScanPrompted');

            showConfirm(
                'Первый запуск Zapret',
                'Перед первым включением рекомендуется протестировать стратегии обхода, чтобы выбрать самую быструю и стабильную для вашего провайдера.\n\nЗапустить автоматическое тестирование конфигураций сейчас?',
                'Запустить тест',
                () => {
                    openConfigModal();
                    toggleSmartScan();
                },
                false,
                () => {
                    sendCmd('toggleBypass', {
                        profile: appState.selectedProfile,
                        useZapret,
                        useProxy
                    });
                }
            );
            return;
        }

        sendCmd('toggleBypass', {
            profile: appState.selectedProfile,
            useZapret,
            useProxy
        });
    } else {
        sendCmd('toggleBypass');
    }
}

function onModuleToggleChange() {
    sendCmd('updateModuleToggles', {
        useZapret: document.getElementById('chk-zapret').checked,
        useProxy: document.getElementById('chk-proxy').checked
    });
}

function refreshPing() {
    document.getElementById('stat-ping').innerText = '...';
    document.getElementById('ping-refresh-icon').classList.add('spin-anim');
    sendCmd('ping');
}

function updateRunningUI(zapretRunning, proxyRunning) {
    const wasRunning = appState.isRunning;
    appState.zapretRunning = zapretRunning;
    appState.proxyRunning = proxyRunning;
    appState.isRunning = zapretRunning || proxyRunning;

    if (appState.isRunning && !wasRunning) {
        sessionStartTime = Date.now();
        watchdogFailCount = 0;
        if (!sessionTimerId) sessionTimerId = setInterval(updateSessionBadgeTick, 1000);
    } else if (!appState.isRunning) {
        sessionStartTime = null;
        if (sessionTimerId) {
            clearInterval(sessionTimerId);
            sessionTimerId = null;
        }
    }
    updateSessionBadgeTick();

    const btn = document.getElementById('main-toggle-btn');
    const reactor = document.getElementById('reactor-container');
    const txt = document.getElementById('main-btn-text');
    const lbl = document.getElementById('zapret-status-label');
    const proxyLbl = document.getElementById('proxy-status-label');

    btn.classList.toggle('active', appState.isRunning);
    reactor.classList.toggle('active', appState.isRunning);
    txt.innerText = appState.isRunning ? 'Остановить' : 'Починить интернет';

    lbl.innerText = zapretRunning ? 'Работает' : 'Остановлен';
    lbl.className = `text-xs font-medium mb-3 ${zapretRunning ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#D13438]'}`;

    if (proxyLbl) {
        proxyLbl.innerText = proxyRunning ? 'Работает локально (:1080)' : 'Прокси для Telegram';
        proxyLbl.className = `text-xs mt-0.5 ${proxyRunning ? 'text-brand font-medium' : 'text-[#A0A0A0]'}`;
    }

    document.getElementById('dot-zapret').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${zapretRunning ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
    document.getElementById('dot-proxy').className = `w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${proxyRunning ? 'dot-online' : 'dot-offline'}`;
    if (typeof syncReactor3DThemeAndState === 'function') syncReactor3DThemeAndState();
}

// =========================================================================
// 2. ВЫБОР КОНФИГОВ ZAPRET (.BAT) И ИГРОВОГО ФИЛЬТРА

function initConfigsList(fileNames) {
    const existingMap = {};
    appState.configs.forEach(c => existingMap[c.fileName.toLowerCase()] = c);

    appState.configs = fileNames.map(name => existingMap[name.toLowerCase()] || ({
        fileName: name,
        topRank: 999999,
        pingMs: appState.currentPing > 0 ? appState.currentPing : 999999,
        testsOk: -1,
        testsTotal: 0
    }));

    document.getElementById('txt-main-profile').innerText = appState.selectedProfile;
    document.getElementById('modal-active-profile').innerText = appState.selectedProfile;
    document.getElementById('modal-configs-count').innerText = `${appState.configs.length} конфигов`;

    renderConfigCards();
}

function renderConfigCards() {
    const list = document.getElementById('profile-cards-list');
    if (!list) return;

    const sorted = [...appState.configs].sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.topRank !== b.topRank) return a.topRank - b.topRank;
        if (b.testsOk !== a.testsOk) return b.testsOk - a.testsOk;
        return a.fileName.localeCompare(b.fileName);
    });

    list.innerHTML = sorted.map(cfg => {
        const isSelected = cfg.fileName.toLowerCase() === appState.selectedProfile.toLowerCase();
        const isMod = cfg.fileName.toLowerCase().startsWith('mod_');

        let badgeHtml = '';
        if (cfg.topRank === 0) {
            badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#0F240F] border border-[#107C10] text-brand font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">★ ЛУЧШИЙ</span>`;
        } else if (cfg.testsOk >= 0) {
            const allOk = cfg.testsOk === cfg.testsTotal && cfg.testsTotal > 0;
            badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${allOk ? 'bg-[#0F240F] text-brand' : 'bg-[#2E2210] text-[#F59E0B]'} font-mono text-[10px] font-semibold">OK: ${cfg.testsOk}/${cfg.testsTotal}</span>`;
        } else if (isMod) {
            badgeHtml = `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#1E1E24] border border-[#333333] text-[#CCCCCC] font-mono text-[10px]">MOD</span>`;
        }

        return `
            <div onclick="selectProfile('${cfg.fileName.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')"
                 class="p-3 rounded-lg border transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between ${isSelected ? 'bg-[#0F240F] border-[#107C10]' : 'bg-[#181818] border-[#262626] hover:border-[#3E3E3E]'}">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 overflow-hidden">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-sm ${isSelected ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#777777]'}">&#xE756;</span>
                    <span class="font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-white truncate">${cfg.fileName}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                    ${badgeHtml}
                    ${isSelected ? '<span class="text-xs font-bold text-brand">✓ Выбран</span>' : '<span class="text-xs text-[#666666]">Выбрать</span>'}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    }).join('');
}

function selectProfile(profileName) {
    appState.selectedProfile = profileName;
    const mainProfEl = document.getElementById('txt-main-profile');
    const modalProfEl = document.getElementById('modal-active-profile');
    if (mainProfEl) mainProfEl.innerText = profileName;
    if (modalProfEl) modalProfEl.innerText = profileName;

    renderConfigCards();
    if (typeof renderModsView === 'function') renderModsView();
    sendCmd('selectProfile', { profile: profileName });
}

    document.getElementById('config-modal').classList.add('open');

    document.getElementById('config-modal').classList.remove('open');

// =========================================================================
// 3. ДВУХКАНАЛЬНЫЙ ГРАФИК ТРАФИКА
// =========================================================================
const CHART_POINTS = 40;
let rxHistory = new Array(CHART_POINTS).fill(0);
let txHistory = new Array(CHART_POINTS).fill(0);
let peakSpeedMbps = 0;
let totalRxMB = 0;
let totalTxMB = 0;
let chartHoverIndex = -1;

function updateChart(rxMbps, txMbps) {
    rxHistory.push(rxMbps);
    if (rxHistory.length > CHART_POINTS) rxHistory.shift();

    txHistory.push(txMbps);
    if (txHistory.length > CHART_POINTS) txHistory.shift();

    const maxNow = Math.max(rxMbps, txMbps);
    if (maxNow > peakSpeedMbps) {
        peakSpeedMbps = maxNow;
        const peakEl = document.getElementById('stat-peak');
        if (peakEl) peakEl.innerText = `Пик: ${peakSpeedMbps.toFixed(1)} Мбит/с`;
    }

    const intervalSec = Math.max(1, appState.settings.statsUpdateInterval || 1);
    totalRxMB += (rxMbps * intervalSec) / 8.0;
    totalTxMB += (txMbps * intervalSec) / 8.0;

    const totalEl = document.getElementById('stat-session-total');
    if (totalEl) {
        totalEl.innerText = `Сессия: ↓ ${totalRxMB.toFixed(1)} МБ • ↑ ${totalTxMB.toFixed(1)} МБ`;
    }

    drawChart();
    if (typeof feedReactor3DTraffic === 'function') feedReactor3DTraffic(rxMbps, txMbps);
}

function onChartMouseMove(e) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('traffic-chart');
    if (!canvas) return;
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const relX = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width));
    chartHoverIndex = Math.round(relX * (CHART_POINTS - 1));
    drawChart();
}

function onChartMouseLeave() {
    chartHoverIndex = -1;
    drawChart();
}

function drawChart() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('traffic-chart');
    if (!canvas || canvas.offsetParent === null) return;

    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const w = rect.width;
    const h = rect.height;
    if (w < 10 || h < 10) return;

    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const targetW = Math.round(w * dpr);
    const targetH = Math.round(h * dpr);
    if (canvas.width !== targetW || canvas.height !== targetH) {
        canvas.width = targetW;
        canvas.height = targetH;
    }

    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.save();
    ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    const accentLight = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--accent-light').trim() || '#4CAF50';
    const maxVal = Math.max(5, ...rxHistory, ...txHistory) * 1.25;

    function drawSeries(data, strokeColor, fillHex) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
            const x = (i / (CHART_POINTS - 1)) * w;
            const y = h - 4 - (data[i] / maxVal) * (h - 20);
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
            else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        }
        ctx.strokeStyle = strokeColor;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.lineTo(w, h);
        ctx.lineTo(0, h);
        ctx.closePath();

        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
        grad.addColorStop(0, fillHex + '40');
        grad.addColorStop(1, fillHex + '00');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fill();
    }

    drawSeries(txHistory, '#60A5FA', '#3B82F6');
    drawSeries(rxHistory, accentLight, accentLight.startsWith('#') ? accentLight : '#4CAF50');

    if (chartHoverIndex >= 0 && chartHoverIndex < CHART_POINTS) {
        const x = Math.floor((chartHoverIndex / (CHART_POINTS - 1)) * w) + 0.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(x, h);
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#555555';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.stroke();

        const rxVal = rxHistory[chartHoverIndex].toFixed(1);
        const txVal = txHistory[chartHoverIndex].toFixed(1);
        const label = `↓ ${rxVal}  ↑ ${txVal} Мбит/с`;

        ctx.font = '600 10px Consolas, monospace';
        const textW = ctx.measureText(label).width + 10;
        const boxX = Math.min(w - textW - 4, Math.max(4, x - textW / 2));

        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.92)';
        ctx.fillRect(boxX, 16, textW, 16);
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#333333';
        ctx.strokeRect(boxX, 16, textW, 16);

        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.fillText(label, boxX + 5, 27);
    }

    ctx.restore();
}

// =========================================================================
// 4. ЖУРНАЛ СОБЫТИЙ
// =========================================================================
const MAX_LOG_LINES = 300;

function appendLog(time, message, isProgress = false) {
    const box = document.getElementById('log-box');
    if (!box) return;

    const fullText = `[${time}] ${message}`;
    if (isProgress && lastProgressLogEl && lastProgressLogEl.parentNode === box) {
        lastProgressLogEl.innerText = fullText;
        box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        return;
    }

    const row = document.createElement('div');
    const isErr = message.includes('ОШИБКА') || message.includes('ERR') || message.includes('❌') || message.includes('⚠');
    row.setAttribute('data-err', isErr ? '1' : '0');

    if (isErr) row.className = 'text-[#F87171]';
    else if (message.includes('✅') || message.includes('🏆')) row.className = 'text-brand font-semibold';
    else row.className = 'text-[#CCCCCC]';

    row.innerText = fullText;
    box.appendChild(row);
    lastProgressLogEl = isProgress ? row : null;

    while (box.children.length > MAX_LOG_LINES) {
        box.removeChild(box.firstElementChild);
    }

    updateLogCounter();
    applyLogFilters();
    box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

function updateLogCounter() {
    const box = document.getElementById('log-box');
    const badge = document.getElementById('log-counter-badge');
    if (box && badge) {
        badge.innerText = box.children.length;
    }
}

function setLogFilter(mode) {
    currentLogFilter = mode;

    const btnAll = document.getElementById('log-filter-all');
    const btnErr = document.getElementById('log-filter-err');

    if (btnAll && btnErr) {
        btnAll.className = mode === 'all'
            ? 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-[#2A2A2A] text-white cursor-pointer transition'
            : 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium text-[#888888] hover:text-white cursor-pointer transition';

        btnErr.className = mode === 'err'
            ? 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-[#2A1215] text-[#F44336] cursor-pointer transition'
            : 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium text-[#888888] hover:text-white cursor-pointer transition';
    }

    applyLogFilters();
}

function applyLogFilters() {
    const box = document.getElementById('log-box');
    if (!box) return;
    const q = (document.getElementById('log-search-input')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();

    Array.from(box.children).forEach(el => {
        const isErr = el.getAttribute('data-err') === '1';
        const matchType = currentLogFilter === 'all' || isErr;
        const matchQuery = !q || el.innerText.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        el.classList.toggle('hidden', !(matchType && matchQuery));
    });
}

function clearLogs() {
    const box = document.getElementById('log-box');
    if (box) box.innerHTML = '';
    lastProgressLogEl = null;
    updateLogCounter();
}

function exportLogs() {
    const box = document.getElementById('log-box');
    if (!box) return;
    const text = Array.from(box.children).map(el => el.innerText).join('\r\n');
    sendCmd('exportLogs', { text });
}

let modsLayoutMode = 'grid'; // 'grid' (2 колонки) или 'list' (1 колонка)
let editorViewMode = 'code'; // 'code' или 'visual'

// Интерактивный словарь-переводчик флагов и переменных winws.exe
const WINWS_FLAG_DICT = {
    '--wf-tcp': {
        badge: 'Перехват WinDivert',
        desc: 'Указывает драйверу ядра WinDivert, какие TCP-порты нужно перехватывать для модификации трафика (обычно 80, 443 и игровые порты).'

    },
    '--filter-tcp': {
        badge: 'Фильтр профиля',
        desc: 'Применяет текущую стратегию обхода только к указанным TCP-портам внутри данного блока (до следующего флага --new).'
    },
    '--filter-udp': {
        badge: 'Фильтр профиля',
