function setModsLayout(mode) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    modsLayoutMode = mode;

    const btnGrid = document.getElementById('btn-layout-grid');
    const btnList = document.getElementById('btn-layout-list');

    if (btnGrid && btnList) {
        btnGrid.className = mode === 'grid'
            ? 'w-7 h-6 rounded flex items-center justify-center bg-[#262626] text-white transition cursor-pointer'
            : 'w-7 h-6 rounded flex items-center justify-center text-[#666666] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer';

        btnList.className = mode === 'list'
            ? 'w-7 h-6 rounded flex items-center justify-center bg-[#262626] text-white transition cursor-pointer'
            : 'w-7 h-6 rounded flex items-center justify-center text-[#666666] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer';
    }

    renderModsView();
}

function switchModsSubTab(subTab) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    appState.modsSubTab = subTab;

    ['bat', 'list', 'guide'].forEach(t => {
        const btn = document.getElementById('mod-tab-' + t);
        if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', t === subTab);
    });

    const desc = document.getElementById('mods-cat-desc');
    const actions = document.getElementById('mods-action-bar');
    const filterControls = document.getElementById('mods-filter-controls');
    const listsContainer = document.getElementById('mods-lists-container');
    const guideContainer = document.getElementById('mods-guide-container');

    if (subTab === 'guide') {
        desc.innerText = 'Подробная документация по созданию собственных стратегий и списков доменов.';
        actions.classList.add('hidden');
        filterControls.classList.add('hidden');
        listsContainer.classList.add('hidden');
        guideContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
        actions.classList.remove('hidden');
        filterControls.classList.remove('hidden');
        listsContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
        guideContainer.classList.add('hidden');

        if (subTab === 'bat') {
            desc.innerText = 'Пользовательские скрипты обхода (.bat) для специфичных игр, провайдеров и нестандартных блокировок.';
        } else {
            desc.innerText = 'Дополнительные списки доменов (list.txt), которые автоматически объединяются с основным списком обхода.';
        }
        renderModsView();
    }
    if (typeof updateModsSlidingPill === 'function') {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => updateModsSlidingPill());
    }
}

function updateModsCounters() {
    const batTotal = appState.batMods.length;
    const batActive = appState.batMods.filter(m => m.isActive).length;
    const listTotal = appState.listMods.length;
    const listActive = appState.listMods.filter(m => m.isActive).length;

    const badgeBat = document.getElementById('badge-count-bat');
    const badgeList = document.getElementById('badge-count-list');
    const summary = document.getElementById('mods-active-summary');

    if (badgeBat) badgeBat.innerText = batTotal;
    if (badgeList) badgeList.innerText = listTotal;

    const totalActive = batActive + listActive;
    if (summary) {
        summary.innerText = `Активно: ${totalActive} из ${batTotal + listTotal}`;
        summary.className = totalActive > 0
            ? 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#0F240F] border border-[#107C10] font-mono text-[11px] text-brand font-semibold'
            : 'px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#181818] border border-[#2A2A2A] font-mono text-[11px] text-[#888888]';
    }
}

function renderModsView() {
    updateModsCounters();
    if (appState.modsSubTab === 'guide') return;

    const isBat = appState.modsSubTab === 'bat';
    const rawSource = isBat ? appState.batMods : appState.listMods;
    const query = (document.getElementById('mods-search-input')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();

    const source = rawSource.filter(m => {
        if (!query) return true;
        const name = (m.meta?.name || '').toLowerCase();
        const author = (m.meta?.author || '').toLowerCase();
        const desc = (m.meta?.description || '').toLowerCase();
        const id = (m.id || '').toLowerCase();
        return name.includes(query) || author.includes(query) || desc.includes(query) || id.includes(query);
    });

    const activeMods = source.filter(m => m.isActive);
    const availableMods = source.filter(m => !m.isActive);

    const useGrid = modsLayoutMode === 'grid' && !appState.settings.compactMode;
    const gridClass = useGrid ? 'grid grid-cols-2 gap-3' : 'flex flex-col gap-2.5';
    const activeListEl = document.getElementById('active-mods-list');
    const availListEl = document.getElementById('available-mods-list');
    activeListEl.className = gridClass;
    availListEl.className = gridClass;

    document.getElementById('active-mods-section').classList.toggle('hidden', activeMods.length === 0);
    document.getElementById('available-mods-section').classList.toggle('hidden', availableMods.length === 0);
    document.getElementById('mods-divider').classList.toggle('hidden', !(activeMods.length > 0 && availableMods.length > 0));

    const emptyState = document.getElementById('mods-empty-state');
    const emptyTitle = document.getElementById('mods-empty-title');
    const emptySub = document.getElementById('mods-empty-sub');

    if (source.length === 0) {
        emptyState.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (query && rawSource.length > 0) {
            emptyTitle.innerText = `По запросу «${query}» ничего не найдено`;
            emptySub.innerText = 'Попробуйте изменить поисковый запрос или очистить поле фильтра.';
        } else {
            emptyTitle.innerText = isBat
                ? 'У вас пока нет пользовательских .bat стратегий'
                : 'У вас пока нет дополнительных списков доменов';
            emptySub.innerText = 'Создайте собственную модификацию в встроенном редакторе кода или импортируйте готовый архив (.zip / .netfix-mod).';
        }
    } else {
        emptyState.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    activeListEl.innerHTML = activeMods.map((m, idx) => buildModCardHtml(m, isBat, idx)).join('');
    availListEl.innerHTML = availableMods.map((m, idx) => buildModCardHtml(m, isBat, idx + activeMods.length)).join('');
}

function escapeStr(str) {
    return (str || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
}

function buildModCardHtml(mod, isBat, index = 0) {
    const safeName = escapeStr(mod.meta.name);
    const delayMs = Math.min(index * 40, 240);
    const typeGlyph = isBat ? '&#xE756;' : '&#xE774;';
    const fileBadge = isBat ? `mod_${mod.id}.bat` : `${mod.id}/list.txt`;
    const isCurrentSelectedProf = isBat && mod.isActive && (appState.selectedProfile || '').toLowerCase() === fileBadge.toLowerCase();
    const statusText = mod.isActive ? (isCurrentSelectedProf ? 'Выбран ★' : 'Активен') : 'Отключен';
    const statusCls = mod.isActive ? 'text-brand font-semibold' : 'text-[#777777]';
    const iconBoxCls = mod.isActive
        ? 'bg-[#107C10]/20 border-[#107C10] text-brand'
        : 'bg-[#202020] border-[#2E2E2E] text-[#888888]';

    const selectProfileBtn = isBat && mod.isActive
        ? `<button onclick="selectProfile('${fileBadge}')" class="mod-action-pill ${isCurrentSelectedProf ? '!border-[#107C10] !text-brand' : ''}" title="Сделать текущим профилем запуска Zapret">
               <span class="mdl-icon text-[11px]">${isCurrentSelectedProf ? '&#xE73E;' : '&#xE768;'}</span>
               <span>${isCurrentSelectedProf ? 'Выбран' : 'Выбрать'}</span>
           </button>`
        : '';

    return `
    <div style="animation-delay: ${delayMs}ms" class="mod-workshop-card ${mod.isActive ? 'is-active' : ''} flex flex-col justify-between gap-3">
        <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <div class="flex items-start gap-3 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border ${iconBoxCls} flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5 transition-colors">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-base">${typeGlyph}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="overflow-hidden">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span class="font-bold text-[15px] text-white truncate">${mod.meta.name}</span>
                        <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#222222] border border-[#333333] text-[#AAAAAA] font-mono text-[10px]">v${mod.meta.version || '1.0'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-[11px] font-mono text-[#777777] min-w-0 w-full">
                        <span class="shrink-0 max-w-[50%] truncate" title="${fileBadge}">${fileBadge}</span>
                        <span class="shrink-0">•</span>
                        <span class="text-[#999999] truncate flex-1 min-w-0" title="Автор: ${mod.meta.author || 'Аноним'}">Автор: ${mod.meta.author || 'Аноним'}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <span class="text-[11px] ${statusCls}">${statusText}</span>
                <input type="checkbox" ${mod.isActive ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleMod('${mod.id}', ${isBat}, '${safeName}')" class="cyber-toggle" title="Включить / отключить мод">
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="text-xs text-[#A8A8A8] leading-relaxed line-clamp-2 min-h-[32px]">
            ${mod.meta.description || 'Описание не указано.'}
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-2.5 border-t border-[#242424]">
            <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#555555]">ID: ${mod.id}</span>

            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                ${selectProfileBtn}
                <button onclick="openModEditor('${mod.id}', ${isBat})" class="mod-action-pill" title="Открыть в Code Studio">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-[11px]">&#xE70F;</span>
                    <span>Код</span>
                </button>
                <button onclick="exportMod('${mod.id}', ${isBat}, '${safeName}')" class="mod-action-pill" title="Упаковать мод в .zip архив">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-[11px]">&#xE78C;</span>
                    <span>Экспорт</span>
                </button>
                <button onclick="promptDeleteMod('${mod.id}', ${isBat}, '${safeName}')" class="mod-action-pill danger" title="Удалить мод с диска">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-[11px]">&#xE74D;</span>
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>`;
}

function toggleMod(id, isBat, name) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    sendCmd('toggleMod', { id, isBat, name });
}

function promptDisableAllMods() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const isBat = appState.modsSubTab === 'bat';
    const activeCount = (isBat ? appState.batMods : appState.listMods).filter(m => m.isActive).length;
    if (activeCount === 0) return;

    showConfirm(
        'Отключить всё',
        'Вы уверены, что хотите отключить все активные моды в этой категории?',
        'Отключить',
        () => sendCmd('disableAllMods', { isBat }),
        true
    );
}

function promptDeleteMod(id, isBat, name) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    showConfirm(
        'Удаление мода',
        `Удалить мод «${name}» навсегда?\nЭто удалит папку мода и все его файлы с диска.`,
        'Удалить',
        () => sendCmd('deleteMod', { id, isBat, name }),
        true
    );
}

function exportMod(id, isBat, name) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    sendCmd('exportMod', { id, isBat, name });
}

function openCreateModModal() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    if (appState.modsSubTab === 'guide') {
        switchModsSubTab('bat');
    }
    document.getElementById('new-mod-name').value = 'Мой новый мод';
    document.getElementById('new-mod-author').value = 'You';
    document.getElementById('new-mod-desc').value = 'Пользовательская модификация обхода';
    sendCmd('generateModId', { baseName: 'custom_mod' });
    document.getElementById('create-mod-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function closeCreateModModal() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    document.getElementById('create-mod-modal').classList.remove('open');
}

function regenerateModId() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const name = document.getElementById('new-mod-name').value || 'mod';
    sendCmd('generateModId', { baseName: name });
}

function onNewModNameInput() {
    const name = document.getElementById('new-mod-name').value || 'mod';
    sendCmd('generateModId', { baseName: name });
}

function onNewModIdInput() {
    const id = document.getElementById('new-mod-id').value;
    sendCmd('checkModId', { id });
}

function updateModIdValidationUI(cleanId, isTaken, isEmpty) {
    const status = document.getElementById('new-mod-id-status');
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-confirm-create-mod');
    if (isEmpty) {
        status.innerText = '⚠ Введите корректный ID (a-z, 0-9)';
        status.className = 'text-[11px] font-mono text-[#FF8C00]';
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.style.opacity = '0.5';
    } else if (isTaken) {
        status.innerText = '❌ Такой ID уже занят!';
        status.className = 'text-[11px] font-mono text-[#F44336]';
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.style.opacity = '0.5';
    } else {
        status.innerText = `✓ ID «${cleanId}» уникален`;
        status.className = 'text-[11px] font-mono text-brand';
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.style.opacity = '1';
    }
}

function confirmCreateMod() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    sendCmd('createMod', {
        isBat: appState.modsSubTab !== 'list',
        id: document.getElementById('new-mod-id').value,
        name: document.getElementById('new-mod-name').value,
        author: document.getElementById('new-mod-author').value,
        description: document.getElementById('new-mod-desc').value
    });
}

function openModEditor(id, isBat) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    sendCmd('getModContent', { id, isBat });
}

function escapeSyntaxHtml(str) {
    return str
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

function highlightSingleBatLine(rawLine) {
    const trimmed = rawLine.trimStart();
    if (trimmed.startsWith('::') || /^rem(\s|$)/i.test(trimmed)) {
        return `<span class="syn-comment">${escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine)}</span>`;
    }

    const tokenRegex = /("(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|%[a-zA-Z0-9_~]+%|--new\b|--[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|\bwinws(?:\.exe)?\b|\^(?=\s*$)|\b(?:@?echo|chcp|cd|pushd|popd|set|title|cls|color|mode|goto|call|if|for|pause|timeout)\b|\b\d+(?:-\d+)?(?:,\d+(?:-\d+)?)*\b)/gi;

    let result = '';
    let lastIndex = 0;
    let match;

    while ((match = tokenRegex.exec(rawLine)) !== null) {
        if (match.index > lastIndex) {
            result += escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine.slice(lastIndex, match.index));
        }
        const token = match[0];
        const lower = token.toLowerCase();
        let cls = '';

        if (token.startsWith('"')) {
            const inner = escapeSyntaxHtml(token).replace(/(%[a-zA-Z0-9_~]+%)/g, '<span class="syn-var" data-token="$1">$1</span>');
            result += `<span class="syn-string">${inner}</span>`;
            lastIndex = tokenRegex.lastIndex;
            continue;
        } else if (token.startsWith('%')) {
            cls = 'syn-var';
        } else if (lower === '--new') {
            cls = 'syn-new';
        } else if (token.startsWith('-')) {
            cls = 'syn-flag';
        } else if (lower.startsWith('winws')) {
            cls = 'syn-exe';
        } else if (token === '^') {
            cls = 'syn-caret';
        } else if (/^\d/.test(token)) {
            cls = 'syn-num';
        } else {
            cls = 'syn-cmd';
        }

        result += `<span class="${cls}" data-token="${escapeSyntaxHtml(lower)}">${escapeSyntaxHtml(token)}</span>`;
        lastIndex = tokenRegex.lastIndex;
    }

    if (lastIndex < rawLine.length) {
        result += escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine.slice(lastIndex));
    }
    return result;
}

function highlightSingleListLine(rawLine) {
    const trimmed = rawLine.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine);
    if (trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
        return `<span class="syn-comment">${escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine)}</span>`;
    }
    if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed) || trimmed.includes('/')) {
        if (/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}(\/\d{1,2})?$/.test(trimmed)) {
            return `<span class="syn-ip">${escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine)}</span>`;
        }
        return `<span class="syn-warn" title="Нажмите «Очистить дубли и URL», чтобы оставить чистый домен">${escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine)}</span>`;
    }
    if (/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$/.test(trimmed)) {
        return `<span class="syn-ip">${escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine)}</span>`;
    }
    return `<span class="syn-domain">${escapeSyntaxHtml(rawLine)}</span>`;
}

function updateEditorSyntaxHighlight() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const hl = document.getElementById('editor-highlight-layer');
    if (!ta || !hl) return;

    const text = ta.value || '';
    const lines = text.split('\n');
    const isBat = appState.editingMod.isBat;

    const htmlLines = lines.map(line => isBat ? highlightSingleBatLine(line) : highlightSingleListLine(line));
    hl.innerHTML = htmlLines.join('\n') + '\n ';
}

// =========================================================================
// ЖИВОЙ ПЕРЕВОДЧИК ФЛАГОВ WINWS ПО НАВЕДЕНИЮ МЫШИ В CODE STUDIO
// =========================================================================
function onEditorMouseMove(e) {
    const modal = document.getElementById('editor-modal');
    if (!appState.editingMod.isBat || !modal || !modal.classList.contains('open')) {
        hideWinwsFlagTooltip();
        return;
    }

    const hl = document.getElementById('editor-highlight-layer');
    const wrap = document.getElementById('editor-canvas-wrap');
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    if (!hl || !wrap || !ta) return;

    ta.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    const elemUnder = document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    ta.style.pointerEvents = '';

    if (elemUnder && elemUnder.hasAttribute('data-token')) {
        const rawToken = (elemUnder.getAttribute('data-token') || '').toLowerCase();
        const info = WINWS_FLAG_DICT[rawToken];
        if (info) {
            const wrapRect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
            const spanRect = elemUnder.getBoundingClientRect();
            showWinwsFlagTooltip(rawToken, info, spanRect.left - wrapRect.left, spanRect.top - wrapRect.top);
            return;
        }
    }

    hideWinwsFlagTooltip();
}

function showWinwsFlagTooltip(tokenName, info, relX, relY) {
    const tip = document.getElementById('winws-flag-tooltip');
    const wrap = document.getElementById('editor-canvas-wrap');
    if (!tip || !wrap) return;

    document.getElementById('wft-flag-name').innerText = tokenName;
    document.getElementById('wft-flag-badge').innerText = info.badge;
    document.getElementById('wft-flag-desc').innerText = info.desc;

    tip.classList.remove('hidden');

    const wrapW = wrap.clientWidth;
    const tipW = tip.offsetWidth || 340;
    const tipH = tip.offsetHeight || 85;

    const left = Math.max(10, Math.min(relX, wrapW - tipW - 12));
    const top = relY > tipH + 16 ? (relY - tipH - 8) : (relY + 24);

    tip.style.left = `${left}px`;
    tip.style.top = `${top}px`;
}

function hideWinwsFlagTooltip() {
    document.getElementById('winws-flag-tooltip')?.classList.add('hidden');
}

// =========================================================================
// ВИЗУАЛЬНЫЙ КОНСТРУКТОР СТРАТЕГИЙ («STRATEGY LEGO»)
// =========================================================================
function switchEditorViewMode(mode) {
    editorViewMode = mode;
    const codeContainer = document.getElementById('editor-code-view-container');
    const visualContainer = document.getElementById('editor-visual-builder');
    const btnCode = document.getElementById('btn-ed-mode-code');
    const btnVis = document.getElementById('btn-ed-mode-visual');

    if (mode === 'visual' && appState.editingMod.isBat) {
        codeContainer?.classList.add('hidden');
        visualContainer?.classList.remove('hidden');
        visualContainer?.classList.add('flex');
        if (btnCode) btnCode.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-[#888888] hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
        if (btnVis) btnVis.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-md bg-[#26262A] text-white text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
        updateLegoPreview();
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            if (typeof initPacketSim3D === 'function') initPacketSim3D();
            if (typeof updatePacketSimConfig === 'function') updatePacketSimConfig();
        });
    } else {
        visualContainer?.classList.add('hidden');
        visualContainer?.classList.remove('flex');
        codeContainer?.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (btnCode) btnCode.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-md bg-[#26262A] text-white text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
        if (btnVis) btnVis.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-[#888888] hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5';
    }
}

function updateLegoPreview() {
    const tcpDesync = document.getElementById('lego-tcp-desync')?.value || 'fake,split2';
    const fooling = document.getElementById('lego-fooling')?.value || 'md5sig';
    const quicEnabled = document.getElementById('lego-quic-enabled')?.checked ?? true;
    const repeats = document.getElementById('lego-repeats')?.value || '6';
    const discordUdp = document.getElementById('lego-discord-udp')?.checked ?? true;
    const useGf = document.getElementById('lego-game-filter')?.checked ?? true;

    const wfTcp = useGf ? '80,443,%GameFilterTCP%' : '80,443';
    let wfUdpParts = ['443'];
    if (discordUdp) wfUdpParts.push('50000-50100');
    if (useGf) wfUdpParts.push('%GameFilterUDP%');
    const wfUdp = wfUdpParts.join(',');

    const blocks = [];

    if (quicEnabled) {
        blocks.push(`--filter-udp=443 --hostlist="%LISTS%list-general.txt" --dpi-desync=fake --dpi-desync-repeats=${repeats}`);
    }

    if (discordUdp) {
        const udpPorts = useGf ? '50000-50100,%GameFilterUDP%' : '50000-50100';
        blocks.push(`--filter-udp=${udpPorts} --dpi-desync=fake --dpi-desync-any-protocol --dpi-desync-cutoff=d3 --dpi-desync-repeats=${repeats}`);
    }

    const foolPart = fooling === 'none'
        ? '--dpi-desync-autottl=2'
        : `--dpi-desync-fooling=${fooling}`;

    const tcpPorts = useGf ? '80,443,%GameFilterTCP%' : '80,443';
    blocks.push(`--filter-tcp=${tcpPorts} --hostlist="%LISTS%list-general.txt" --dpi-desync=${tcpDesync} --dpi-desync-repeats=${repeats} ${foolPart}`);

    const header = `:: Сгенерировано в Визуальном конструкторе Zapret for ADHD\r\n"%BIN%winws.exe" --wf-tcp=${wfTcp} --wf-udp=${wfUdp} ^\r\n`;
    const body = blocks.join(' ^\r\n--new ^\r\n');

    const previewEl = document.getElementById('lego-code-preview');
    if (previewEl) previewEl.textContent = header + body;
    if (typeof updatePacketSimConfig === 'function') updatePacketSimConfig();
}

function applyLegoToCodeEditor() {
    updateLegoPreview();
    const code = document.getElementById('lego-code-preview')?.textContent || '';
    if (!code) return;

    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    ta.value = code;
    onEditorTextInput();
    switchEditorViewMode('code');
    setEditorStatus('✓ Схема из конструктора перенесена в код!', 'text-brand');
}

function openEditorWithData(data) {
    appState.editingMod.id = data.id;
    appState.editingMod.isBat = data.isBat;

    document.getElementById('editor-title').innerText = `Редактор: ${data.meta.name}`;
    document.getElementById('editor-subtitle').innerText = `ID: ${data.id} • Файл: ${data.fileName}`;

    document.getElementById('edit-mod-name').value = data.meta.name || '';
    document.getElementById('edit-mod-author').value = data.meta.author || '';
    document.getElementById('edit-mod-version').value = data.meta.version || '1.0';
    document.getElementById('edit-mod-desc').value = data.meta.description || '';

    document.getElementById('editor-mode-tabs')?.classList.toggle('hidden', !data.isBat);
    document.getElementById('editor-bat-tools').className = data.isBat ? 'flex items-center gap-1' : 'hidden';
    document.getElementById('editor-snippets-bar').className = data.isBat ? 'flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto pb-1 mb-1.5 shrink-0' : 'hidden';
    document.getElementById('editor-list-tools').className = data.isBat ? 'hidden' : 'flex items-center gap-1';

    switchEditorViewMode('code');

    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    ta.value = data.content || '';
    onEditorTextInput();
    setEditorStatus('Наведите курсор на любой флаг для перевода', 'text-[#4ADE80]');
    document.getElementById('editor-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function closeEditorModal() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    hideWinwsFlagTooltip();
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    if (ta) {
        ta.style.pointerEvents = '';
        ta.blur();
    }
    document.getElementById('editor-modal').classList.remove('open');
}

function toggleEditorMetaPanel() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    document.getElementById('editor-meta-panel').classList.toggle('hidden');
}

function toggleEditorSearch() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const p = document.getElementById('editor-search-panel');
    const isHidden = p.classList.contains('hidden');
    if (isHidden) {
        p.classList.remove('hidden');
        p.classList.add('flex');
        document.getElementById('editor-find-input').focus();
    } else {
        p.classList.add('hidden');
        p.classList.remove('flex');
        document.getElementById('editor-textarea').focus();
    }
}

function toggleEditorWordWrap() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    appState.editingMod.wordWrap = !appState.editingMod.wordWrap;
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const hl = document.getElementById('editor-highlight-layer');
    const ws = appState.editingMod.wordWrap ? 'pre-wrap' : 'pre';
    ta.style.whiteSpace = ws;
    if (hl) hl.style.whiteSpace = ws;
    document.getElementById('btn-word-wrap').innerText = `Перенос: ${appState.editingMod.wordWrap ? 'Вкл' : 'Выкл'}`;
    syncEditorScroll();
}

function changeEditorFontSize(delta) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    appState.editingMod.fontSize = Math.min(22, Math.max(10, appState.editingMod.fontSize + delta));
    const sizePx = `${appState.editingMod.fontSize}px`;
    document.getElementById('editor-textarea').style.fontSize = sizePx;
    document.getElementById('editor-line-numbers').style.fontSize = sizePx;
    const hl = document.getElementById('editor-highlight-layer');
    if (hl) hl.style.fontSize = sizePx;
    syncEditorScroll();
}

function onEditorTextInput() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const lines = ta.value.split('\n');
    const lineNums = document.getElementById('editor-line-numbers');

    let nums = '';
    for (let i = 1; i <= Math.max(1, lines.length); i++) {
        nums += i + '\n';
    }
    lineNums.innerText = nums;

    updateEditorSyntaxHighlight();
    syncEditorScroll();

    if (appState.editingMod.isBat) {
        document.getElementById('editor-stats-info').innerText = `Строк: ${lines.length} | Символов: ${ta.value.length}`;
    } else {
        const validDomains = lines.filter(l => l.trim().length > 0 && !l.trim().startsWith('#')).length;
        document.getElementById('editor-stats-info').innerText = `Доменов: ${validDomains} | Строк: ${lines.length}`;
    }
    updateEditorCursorPos();
}

function syncEditorScroll() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const hl = document.getElementById('editor-highlight-layer');
    const ln = document.getElementById('editor-line-numbers');
    if (ln) ln.scrollTop = ta.scrollTop;
    if (hl) {
        hl.scrollTop = ta.scrollTop;
        hl.scrollLeft = ta.scrollLeft;
    }
}

function updateEditorCursorPos() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const upToCaret = ta.value.substring(0, ta.selectionStart);
    const lines = upToCaret.split('\n');
    const row = lines.length;
    const col = lines[lines.length - 1].length + 1;
    document.getElementById('editor-cursor-pos').innerText = `Стр: ${row}, Столб: ${col}`;
}

function onEditorKeyDown(e) {
    if (e.ctrlKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'f') {
        e.preventDefault();
        toggleEditorSearch();
    } else if (e.ctrlKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 's') {
        e.preventDefault();
        saveEditorChanges(false);
    }
}

function insertSnippet(snippet) {
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const start = ta.selectionStart;
    const end = ta.selectionEnd;
    ta.value = ta.value.substring(0, start) + snippet + ta.value.substring(end);
    ta.selectionStart = ta.selectionEnd = start + snippet.length;
    ta.focus();
    onEditorTextInput();
}

function commentCurrentLine() {
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const caret = ta.selectionStart;
    const lines = ta.value.split('\n');
    const lineIdx = ta.value.substring(0, caret).split('\n').length - 1;

    if (lineIdx >= 0 && lineIdx < lines.length) {
        const line = lines[lineIdx];
        if (line.trimStart().startsWith('::')) {
            const idx = line.indexOf('::');
            lines[lineIdx] = line.slice(0, idx) + line.slice(idx + 2).trimStart();
        } else {
            lines[lineIdx] = ':: ' + line;
        }
        ta.value = lines.join('\n');
        ta.focus();
        onEditorTextInput();
    }
}

function validateCurrentBat() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    sendCmd('validateBatMod', {
        name: document.getElementById('edit-mod-name').value || 'Текущий скрипт',
        content: document.getElementById('editor-textarea').value
    });
}

function cleanDomainsList() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const rawLines = ta.value.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim().length > 0);
    const cleaned = [];

    rawLines.forEach(raw => {
        let d = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!d || d.startsWith('#') || d.startsWith('//')) return;
        if (d.startsWith('http://')) d = d.substring(7);
        else if (d.startsWith('https://')) d = d.substring(8);
        if (d.startsWith('www.')) d = d.substring(4);
        const slashIdx = d.indexOf('/');
        if (slashIdx > 0) d = d.substring(0, slashIdx);
        const colonIdx = d.indexOf(':');
        if (colonIdx > 0) d = d.substring(0, colonIdx);
        if (d && d.includes('.')) cleaned.push(d);
    });

    const uniqueSorted = [...new Set(cleaned)].sort();
    const removed = Math.max(0, rawLines.length - uniqueSorted.length);
    ta.value = uniqueSorted.join('\n');
    onEditorTextInput();
    setEditorStatus(`Готово! Доменов: ${uniqueSorted.length} (удалено: ${removed})`, 'text-[#60A5FA]');
}

function editorFindNext() {
    const query = document.getElementById('editor-find-input').value;
    if (!query) return;
    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const text = ta.value.toLowerCase();
    const q = query.toLowerCase();

    let start = ta.selectionEnd;
    if (start >= text.length) start = 0;

    let idx = text.indexOf(q, start);
    if (idx === -1 && start > 0) idx = text.indexOf(q, 0);

    if (idx >= 0) {
        ta.focus();
        ta.setSelectionRange(idx, idx + query.length);
        const lineNum = text.substring(0, idx).split('\n').length;
        setEditorStatus(`Найдено на строке ${lineNum}`, 'text-[#60A5FA]');
    } else {
        setEditorStatus('Совпадений не найдено', 'text-[#F44336]');
    }
}

function editorReplaceAll() {
    const query = document.getElementById('editor-find-input').value;
    const replacement = document.getElementById('editor-replace-input').value || '';
    if (!query) return;

    const ta = document.getElementById('editor-textarea');
    const regex = new RegExp(query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'), 'gi');
    const matches = ta.value.match(regex);
    const count = matches ? matches.length : 0;

    if (count > 0) {
        ta.value = ta.value.replace(regex, replacement);
        onEditorTextInput();
        setEditorStatus(`Заменено совпадений: ${count}`, 'text-[#4ADE80]');
    } else {
        setEditorStatus('Совпадений для замены нет', 'text-[#F44336]');
    }
}

function setEditorStatus(msg, colorCls) {
    const el = document.getElementById('editor-status-msg');
    el.innerText = msg;
    el.title = msg; 
    el.className = `truncate flex-1 min-w-0 ${colorCls}`;
}

function saveEditorChanges(bypassWarning = false) {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    if (editorViewMode === 'visual' && appState.editingMod.isBat) {
        updateLegoPreview();
        const gen = document.getElementById('lego-code-preview')?.textContent || '';
        if (gen) document.getElementById('editor-textarea').value = gen;
    }

    sendCmd('saveModContent', {
        id: appState.editingMod.id,
        isBat: appState.editingMod.isBat,
        name: document.getElementById('edit-mod-name').value,
        author: document.getElementById('edit-mod-author').value,
        version: document.getElementById('edit-mod-version').value,
        description: document.getElementById('edit-mod-desc').value,
        content: document.getElementById('editor-textarea').value,
        bypassWarning
    });
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: wwwroot\js\packet-sim3d.js
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

// =========================================================================
// МОДУЛЬ: 3D ЛАБОРАТОРИЯ ПРОБИТИЯ ТСПУ 2.0 (СВЕТ, СЕТКА И ВЗРЫВЫ ФЕЙКОВ)
// Файл: wwwroot/js/packet-sim3d.js
// =========================================================================

let packetSim = {
    initialized: false,
    scene: null,
    camera: null,
    renderer: null,
    dpiScannerRing: null,
    dpiCoreLaser: null,
    serverNode: null,
    clientNode: null,
    packets: [],
    sparks: [],
    spawnTimer: 0,
    config: {
        mode: 'fake,split2',
        fooling: 'md5sig',
        quic: true,
        repeats: 6
    }
};

function initPacketSim3D() {
    if (packetSim.initialized) return;
    if (typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;
    if (appState?.settings?.hardwareAcceleration === false) return;

    const host = document.getElementById('lego-3d-sim-canvas-wrap');
    if (!host) return;

    try {
        const w = host.clientWidth || 780;
        const h = host.clientHeight || 148;

        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        // Изометрический кинематографичный ракурс сверху-сбоку
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(34, w / h, 0.1, 100);
        camera.position.set(0, 2.35, 7.2);
        camera.lookAt(0, -0.1, 0);

        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: true, antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
        renderer.setSize(w, h);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
        host.innerHTML = '';
        host.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        // Освещение для объемных 3D-граней пакетов
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.65);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        const dirLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.1);
        dirLight.position.set(3, 6, 5);
        scene.add(dirLight);

        // 1. Перспективная кибер-сетка пола
        const grid = new THREE.GridHelper(11, 22, 0x2A2A35, 0x181820);
        grid.position.y = -0.75;
        scene.add(grid);

        // 2. Светящаяся магистральная линия передачи данных
        const beamGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.025, 0.025, 8.2, 8);
        beamGeo.rotateZ(Math.PI / 2);
        const beamMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x334155, transparent: true, opacity: 0.6 });
        const beamMesh = new THREE.Mesh(beamGeo, beamMat);
        beamMesh.position.set(0, 0, 0);
        scene.add(beamMesh);

        // 3. Портал Клиента (Отправитель — слева, x = -4.0)
        const cliGroup = new THREE.Group();
        cliGroup.position.set(-4.0, 0, 0);
        const cliRing = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.52, 0.04, 12, 32),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x60A5FA })
        );
        cliRing.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
        cliGroup.add(cliRing);
        scene.add(cliGroup);

        // 4. Лазерный Щит ТСПУ / DPI (Посередине, x = 0)
        const dpiGroup = new THREE.Group();
        dpiGroup.position.set(0, 0, 0);

        // Внешнее зубчатое кольцо сканера РКН/ТСПУ
        const scannerRing = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.92, 0.035, 10, 6),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xEF4444, wireframe: false })
        );
        scannerRing.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
        dpiGroup.add(scannerRing);

        // Полупрозрачное красное силовое поле внутри кольца
        const laserDisc = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.88, 24),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xEF4444, transparent: true, opacity: 0.18, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
        );
        laserDisc.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
        dpiGroup.add(laserDisc);
        scene.add(dpiGroup);

        // 5. Ядро Целевого Сервера (YouTube / Discord — справа, x = 4.0)
        const srvGroup = new THREE.Group();
        srvGroup.position.set(4.0, 0, 0);
        const srvCore = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.55, 0),
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: 0x4CAF50,
                emissive: 0x107C10,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
                roughness: 0.2,
                metalness: 0.5
            })
        );
        const srvWire = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.72, 1),
            new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x4ADE80, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 })
        );
        srvGroup.add(srvCore);
        srvGroup.add(srvWire);
        scene.add(srvGroup);

        Object.assign(packetSim, {
            initialized: true,
            scene,
            camera,
            renderer,
            dpiScannerRing: scannerRing,
            dpiCoreLaser: laserDisc,
            serverNode: srvGroup,
            clientNode: cliGroup
        });

        updatePacketSimConfig();
        spawnSimPacketWave();
        animatePacketSim3D();
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('PacketSim3D error:', e);
    }
}

// Взрыв искр при сгорании фейк-пакета после обмана ТСПУ
function createFakeExplosionSparks(pos, hexColor) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const sparkGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.09, 0.09, 0.09);
        const sparkMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: hexColor, transparent: true, opacity: 1 });
        const spark = new THREE.Mesh(sparkGeo, sparkMat);
        spark.position.copy(pos);
        packetSim.scene.add(spark);

        packetSim.sparks.push({
            mesh: spark,
            vx: (Math.random() - 0.2) * 0.08,
            vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.12,
            vz: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.12
        });
    }
}

function updatePacketSimConfig() {
    const mode = document.getElementById('lego-tcp-desync')?.value || 'fake,split2';
    const fooling = document.getElementById('lego-fooling')?.value || 'md5sig';
    const quic = document.getElementById('lego-quic-enabled')?.checked ?? true;
    const repeats = parseInt(document.getElementById('lego-repeats')?.value || '6', 10);

    packetSim.config = { mode, fooling, quic, repeats };

    const lbl = document.getElementById('lego-sim-caption');
    if (lbl) {
        const parts = [];
        if (mode.includes('fake')) parts.push(`Фейк-приманка (${fooling === 'none' ? 'Auto-TTL' : fooling})`);
        if (mode.includes('multisplit')) parts.push('Шинковка на 4 части');
        else if (mode.includes('disorder')) parts.push('Обратный порядок (Disorder)');
        else if (mode.includes('split')) parts.push('Разрез на 2 сегмента (Split)');
        else if (mode.includes('syndata')) parts.push('SYN-Data пакет');
        lbl.innerText = parts.join(' • ');
    }
}

function spawnSimPacketWave() {
    if (!packetSim.initialized) return;

    const { mode } = packetSim.config;
    const accentHex = typeof getThemeHexNumber === 'function' ? getThemeHexNumber() : 0x4CAF50;

    // 1. Если включен fake — выпускаем яркую оранжевую капсулу-приманку впереди пакета
    if (mode.includes('fake')) {
        const fakeGeo = new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.28, 0);
        const fakeMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0xF59E0B,
            emissive: 0xD97706,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.7,
            roughness: 0.3
        });
        const fakeMesh = new THREE.Mesh(fakeGeo, fakeMat);
        fakeMesh.position.set(-3.4, 0, 0);
        packetSim.scene.add(fakeMesh);

        packetSim.packets.push({
            mesh: fakeMesh,
            isFake: true,
            speed: 0.062
        });
    }

    // 2. Выпускаем реальные сегменты пакета данных
    let segmentsCount = 1;
    if (mode.includes('multisplit')) segmentsCount = 4;
    else if (mode.includes('split') || mode.includes('disorder')) segmentsCount = 2;

    const isDisorder = mode.includes('disorder');

    for (let i = 0; i < segmentsCount; i++) {
        const w = Math.max(0.18, 0.56 / segmentsCount);
        const segGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, 0.36, 0.36);
        const segColor = i % 2 === 0 ? accentHex : 0x38BDF8;
        const segMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: segColor,
            emissive: segColor,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
            roughness: 0.25,
            metalness: 0.4
        });
        const segMesh = new THREE.Mesh(segGeo, segMat);
        segMesh.position.set(-3.85 - i * (w + 0.18), 0, 0);
        packetSim.scene.add(segMesh);

        packetSim.packets.push({
            mesh: segMesh,
            isFake: false,
            segIndex: i,
            totalSegs: segmentsCount,
            isDisorder,
            speed: 0.056
        });
    }
}

function animatePacketSim3D() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animatePacketSim3D);
    if (!packetSim.initialized) return;

    const modal = document.getElementById('editor-modal');
    const vis = document.getElementById('editor-visual-builder');
    if (!modal || !modal.classList.contains('open') || !vis || vis.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        return;
    }

    const host = document.getElementById('lego-3d-sim-canvas-wrap');
    if (host && host.clientWidth > 100) {
        const w = host.clientWidth;
        const h = host.clientHeight || 148;
        if (Math.abs(packetSim.renderer.domElement.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1) - w) > 5 ||
            Math.abs(packetSim.renderer.domElement.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1) - h) > 5) {
            packetSim.renderer.setSize(w, h);
            packetSim.camera.aspect = w / h;
            packetSim.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        }
    }

    packetSim.spawnTimer++;
    if (packetSim.spawnTimer > 62) {
        packetSim.spawnTimer = 0;
        spawnSimPacketWave();
    }

    packetSim.dpiScannerRing.rotation.z -= 0.025;
    packetSim.serverNode.rotation.y += 0.022;
    packetSim.clientNode.rotation.x += 0.02;

    // Движение пакетов
    for (let i = packetSim.packets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const p = packetSim.packets[i];
        p.mesh.position.x += p.speed;
        p.mesh.rotation.x += 0.035;
        p.mesh.rotation.y += 0.025;

        if (p.isFake) {
            // Когда фейк проходит щит ТСПУ (x > 0.25) — он отвлекает сканер и взрывается искрами
            if (p.mesh.position.x >= 0.45) {
                createFakeExplosionSparks(p.mesh.position, 0xF59E0B);
                packetSim.dpiCoreLaser.material.opacity = 0.42; // вспышка щита
                packetSim.scene.remove(p.mesh);
                p.mesh.geometry.dispose();
                p.mesh.material.dispose();
                packetSim.packets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }
        } else {
            // Реальные сегменты: расходятся по орбитам вокруг лазера ТСПУ
            if (p.totalSegs > 1 && p.mesh.position.x > -1.8 && p.mesh.position.x < 1.8) {
                const dir = (p.segIndex % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1);
                const angle = (p.mesh.position.x + 1.8) * 1.1;
                const amp = 0.42 * dir * (p.isDisorder ? -1 : 1);
                p.mesh.position.y = Math.sin(angle) * amp;
                p.mesh.position.z = Math.cos(angle) * (amp * 0.5);
            } else {
                p.mesh.position.y *= 0.85;
                p.mesh.position.z *= 0.85;
            }

            if (p.mesh.position.x >= 3.7) {
                packetSim.serverNode.scale.set(1.22, 1.22, 1.22);
                packetSim.scene.remove(p.mesh);
                p.mesh.geometry.dispose();
                p.mesh.material.dispose();
                packetSim.packets.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }
        }
    }

    // Анимация разлетающихся искр от сгоревшего фейка
    for (let i = packetSim.sparks.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const sp = packetSim.sparks[i];
        sp.mesh.position.x += sp.vx;
        sp.mesh.position.y += sp.vy;
        sp.mesh.position.z += sp.vz;
        sp.mesh.scale.multiplyScalar(0.89);
        sp.mesh.material.opacity *= 0.88;

        if (sp.mesh.material.opacity < 0.06) {
            packetSim.scene.remove(sp.mesh);
            sp.mesh.geometry.dispose();
            sp.mesh.material.dispose();
            packetSim.sparks.splice(i, 1);
        }
    }

    packetSim.dpiCoreLaser.material.opacity += (0.14 - packetSim.dpiCoreLaser.material.opacity) * 0.1;
    packetSim.serverNode.scale.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1), 0.12);
    packetSim.renderer.render(packetSim.scene, packetSim.camera);
}

// =========================================================================
// 1. УНИВЕРСАЛЬНЫЕ ДИАЛОГИ (ALERT, CONFIRM, SELECTION)
// =========================================================================
let confirmCallback = null;
let cancelConfirmCallback = null;
let isBackendConfirm = false;
let selectionCallback = null;

function showAlert(title, message, isError = false) {
    isBackendConfirm = false;
    confirmCallback = null;
    cancelConfirmCallback = null;

    document.getElementById('confirm-title').innerText = title || 'Уведомление';
    document.getElementById('confirm-title').className = `text-base font-bold mb-2 ${isError ? 'text-[#F44336]' : 'text-white'}`;
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').innerText = message || '';
    document.getElementById('confirm-cancel-btn').classList.add('hidden');

    const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
    okBtn.innerText = 'ОК';
    okBtn.className = 'px-4 py-1.5 rounded-md btn-brand text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer';

    document.getElementById('confirm-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function showConfirm(title, message, confirmText = 'Да', onAccept = null, isDanger = false, onCancel = null) {
    confirmCallback = onAccept;
    cancelConfirmCallback = onCancel;

    document.getElementById('confirm-title').innerText = title || 'Подтверждение';
    document.getElementById('confirm-title').className = 'text-base font-bold text-white mb-2';
    document.getElementById('confirm-msg').innerText = message || '';
    document.getElementById('confirm-cancel-btn').classList.remove('hidden');

    const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
    okBtn.innerText = confirmText;
    okBtn.className = isDanger
        ? 'px-4 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-[#D13438] hover:bg-[#B8282C] text-white text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer transition'
        : 'px-4 py-1.5 rounded-md btn-brand text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer';

    document.getElementById('confirm-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function closeConfirmModal(accepted) {
    document.getElementById('confirm-modal').classList.remove('open');

    if (isBackendConfirm) {
        isBackendConfirm = false;
        sendCmd('confirmResponse', { accepted });
        return;
    }

    if (accepted && typeof confirmCallback === 'function') {
        const cb = confirmCallback;
        confirmCallback = null;
        cb();
    } else if (!accepted && typeof cancelConfirmCallback === 'function') {
        const ccb = cancelConfirmCallback;
        cancelConfirmCallback = null;
        ccb();
    }
}

function openSelectionModal(subTitle, title, options, currentVal, onSelect) {
    selectionCallback = onSelect;
    document.getElementById('sel-modal-sub').innerText = subTitle;
    document.getElementById('sel-modal-title').innerText = title;

    const list = document.getElementById('sel-modal-list');
    list.innerHTML = options.map((opt, idx) => {
        const isSel = String(opt.value) === String(currentVal);
        return `
            <div onclick="chooseSelectionOption(${idx})"
                 class="p-3 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between transition ${isSel ? 'bg-[#0F240F] border-[#107C10]' : 'bg-[#181818] border-[#262626] hover:bg-[#222222]'}">
                <div class="pr-3">
                    <div class="text-xs font-bold ${isSel ? 'text-brand' : 'text-white'}">${opt.title}</div>
                    <div class="text-[11px] text-[#888888] mt-0.5">${opt.desc}</div>
                </div>
                <span class="text-sm font-bold ${isSel ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#555555]'}">${isSel ? '✓' : '→'}</span>
            </div>
        `;
    }).join('');

    list._options = options;
    document.getElementById('selection-modal').classList.add('open');
}

function chooseSelectionOption(idx) {
    const list = document.getElementById('sel-modal-list');
    const opt = list._options?.[idx];
    document.getElementById('selection-modal').classList.remove('open');
    if (opt && typeof selectionCallback === 'function') {
        selectionCallback(opt.value);
    }
}

function closeSelectionModal() {
    document.getElementById('selection-modal').classList.remove('open');
}

let cmdSelectedIndex = 0;
let filteredCommands = [];

function getCommandPaletteItems() {
    const base = [
        { title: appState.isRunning ? 'Остановить обход блокировок' : 'Включить обход блокировок', cat: 'Управление', icon: '&#xE7E8;', run: () => onMainToggleClick() },
        { title: 'Перезапустить службы Zapret и Proxy', cat: 'Управление', icon: '&#xE777;', run: () => sendCmd('restartBypass') },
        { title: 'Авто-подбор лучшего конфига (Smart DPI Scan)', cat: 'Сканер', icon: '&#xE721;', run: () => { openConfigModal(); toggleSmartScan(); } },
        { title: 'Запустить полную диагностику сети', cat: 'Диагностика', icon: '&#xE9D9;', run: () => { switchTab('diag'); runDiagnostics(); } },
        { title: 'Настроить Игровой фильтр (Game Filter)', cat: 'Игры', icon: '&#xE7FC;', run: () => openGameFilterModal() },
        { title: 'Создать новую модификацию (.bat / список)', cat: 'Мастерская', icon: '&#xE710;', run: () => { switchTab('mods'); openCreateModModal(); } },
        { title: 'Выгрузить драйвер WinDivert (для античитов)', cat: 'Система', icon: '&#xEA18;', run: () => sendCmd('unloadWinDivert') },
        { title: 'Подключить прокси в Telegram Desktop (1 клик)', cat: 'Telegram', icon: '&#xE709;', run: () => sendCmd('connectTelegramProxy') },
        { title: 'Обновить системный файл Hosts', cat: 'Маршруты', icon: '&#xE8B7;', run: () => updateSystemHosts() },
        { title: 'Экспортировать резервную копию настроек', cat: 'Бэкап', icon: '&#xE78C;', run: () => sendCmd('exportConfigBackup') },
        { title: 'Перейти: Главная панель', cat: 'Навигация', icon: '&#xE80F;', run: () => switchTab('home') },
        { title: 'Перейти: Мастерская модификаций', cat: 'Навигация', icon: '&#xE74C;', run: () => switchTab('mods') },
        { title: 'Перейти: Настройки системы', cat: 'Навигация', icon: '&#xE713;', run: () => switchTab('settings') }
    ];

    appState.configs.forEach(c => {
        base.push({
            title: `Профиль: ${c.fileName}`,
            cat: 'Конфиг .bat',
            icon: '&#xE756;',
            run: () => selectProfile(c.fileName)
        });
    });

    return base;
}

function toggleCommandPaletteModal() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal');
    if (modal && modal.classList.contains('open')) closeCommandPalette();
    else openCommandPalette();
}

function openCommandPalette() {
    const input = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-input');
    if (input) input.value = '';
    cmdSelectedIndex = 0;
    renderCommandPaletteList();
    document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal').classList.add('open');
    setTimeout(() => input?.focus(), 40);
}

function closeCommandPalette() {
    document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal').classList.remove('open');
}

function renderCommandPaletteList() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('cmd-palette-input')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const all = getCommandPaletteItems();
    filteredCommands = all.filter(item => !q || item.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || item.cat.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    cmdSelectedIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(cmdSelectedIndex, filteredCommands.length - 1));

    const list = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-list');
    if (!list) return;

    if (filteredCommands.length === 0) {
        list.innerHTML = `<div class="p-4 text-center text-xs text-[#666666]">Ничего не найдено</div>`;
        return;
    }

    list.innerHTML = filteredCommands.map((item, idx) => {
        const active = idx === cmdSelectedIndex;
        return `
            <div onclick="executeCommandPaletteItem(${idx})"
                 onmouseenter="cmdSelectedIndex=${idx}; renderCommandPaletteList()"
                 class="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer transition ${active ? 'bg-[#0F240F] border border-[#107C10]' : 'border border-transparent hover:bg-[#1C1C1C]'}">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 overflow-hidden">
                    <span class="mdl-icon text-xs ${active ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#888888]'}">${item.icon}</span>
                    <span class="text-xs font-medium text-white truncate">${item.title}</span>
                </div>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#181818] text-[#888888] shrink-0">${item.cat}</span>
            </div>
        `;
    }).join('');
}

function onCommandPaletteKeyDown(e) {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (filteredCommands.length > 0) {
            cmdSelectedIndex = (cmdSelectedIndex + 1) % filteredCommands.length;
            renderCommandPaletteList();
        }
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (filteredCommands.length > 0) {
            cmdSelectedIndex = (cmdSelectedIndex - 1 + filteredCommands.length) % filteredCommands.length;
            renderCommandPaletteList();
        }
    } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        executeCommandPaletteItem(cmdSelectedIndex);
    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        closeCommandPalette();
    }
}

function executeCommandPaletteItem(idx) {
    const item = filteredCommands[idx];
    closeCommandPalette();
    if (item && typeof item.run === 'function') {
        item.run();
    }
}

let diagViewMode = 'radar';
let diagRadarAnimId = null;
let diagRadarAngle = 0;
let diagRadarNodes = [];
let diagHoveredNode = null;
let diagRadarLastTime = 0;

function initDiagnosticsRadarView() {
    const dcGrid = document.getElementById('diag-dc-grid');
    if (!dcGrid) return;
    const matrixCard = dcGrid.closest('.glass-card');
    if (!matrixCard || document.getElementById('diag-radar-wrap')) return;

    const headerBar = document.createElement('div');
    headerBar.className = 'flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-[#242424]';
    headerBar.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full dot-online"></span>
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider">Топология маршрутов: Telegram DC & Discord</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div class="flex items-center bg-[#101010] border border-[#2A2A2A] rounded-lg p-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold">
                <button id="btn-diag-view-radar" onclick="setDiagViewMode('radar')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-[#262626] text-white transition cursor-pointer">🎯 Радар узлов</button>
                <button id="btn-diag-view-table" onclick="setDiagViewMode('table')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[#888888] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer">📋 Таблица</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;

    const radarWrap = document.createElement('div');
    radarWrap.id = 'diag-radar-wrap';
    radarWrap.className = 'relative w-full h-[300px] rounded-xl bg-[#08080A] border border-[#222226] overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center';
    radarWrap.innerHTML = `
        <canvas id="diag-radar-canvas" width="760" height="300" class="w-full h-full cursor-crosshair"></canvas>
        <div class="absolute top-2.5 left-3 pointer-events-none flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] font-mono text-[#888888]">
            <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5"><i class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#4CAF50] inline-block"></i>Маршрут пробит</span>
            <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5"><i class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#F59E0B] inline-block"></i>Частично / Пинг</span>
            <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5"><i class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#D13438] inline-block"></i>Потеря пакетов</span>
        </div>
        <div id="diag-radar-tooltip" class="hidden absolute pointer-events-none bg-[#121216]/95 border border-[#33333A] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono text-white shadow-2xl z-20"></div>
    `;

    const tablesContainer = document.createElement('div');
    tablesContainer.id = 'diag-tables-wrap';
    tablesContainer.className = 'space-y-4 hidden';
    while (matrixCard.firstChild) {
        tablesContainer.appendChild(matrixCard.firstChild);
    }

    matrixCard.appendChild(headerBar);
    matrixCard.appendChild(radarWrap);
    matrixCard.appendChild(tablesContainer);

    const canvas = document.getElementById('diag-radar-canvas');
    if (canvas) {
        canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', onDiagRadarMouseMove);
        canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            diagHoveredNode = null;
            document.getElementById('diag-radar-tooltip')?.classList.add('hidden');
        });
    }

    buildDefaultRadarNodes();
    startDiagRadarLoop();
}

function setDiagViewMode(mode) {
    diagViewMode = mode;
    const radarWrap = document.getElementById('diag-radar-wrap');
    const tablesWrap = document.getElementById('diag-tables-wrap');
    const btnRadar = document.getElementById('btn-diag-view-radar');
    const btnTable = document.getElementById('btn-diag-view-table');

    if (mode === 'radar') {
        radarWrap?.classList.remove('hidden');
        tablesWrap?.classList.add('hidden');
        if (btnRadar) btnRadar.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-[#262626] text-white transition cursor-pointer';
        if (btnTable) btnTable.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[#888888] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer';
    } else {
        radarWrap?.classList.add('hidden');
        tablesWrap?.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (btnRadar) btnRadar.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-[#888888] hover:text-white transition cursor-pointer';
        if (btnTable) btnTable.className = 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-[#262626] text-white transition cursor-pointer';
    }
}

function buildDefaultRadarNodes() {
    diagRadarNodes = [
        { id: 'dc1', label: 'TG DC 1', sub: '149.154.175.50 (US)', angle: -2.45, radius: 0.78, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Ожидание сканирования...' },
        { id: 'dc2', label: 'TG DC 2', sub: '149.154.167.51 (NL)', angle: -1.80, radius: 0.62, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Ожидание сканирования...' },
        { id: 'dc3', label: 'TG DC 3', sub: '149.154.175.100 (US)', angle: -1.15, radius: 0.80, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Ожидание сканирования...' },
        { id: 'dc4', label: 'TG DC 4', sub: '149.154.167.91 (NL)', angle: -0.50, radius: 0.64, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Ожидание сканирования...' },
        { id: 'dc5', label: 'TG DC 5', sub: '91.108.56.130 (SG)', angle: 0.25, radius: 0.82, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Ожидание сканирования...' },
        { id: 'ds_gw', label: 'Discord GW', sub: '162.159.130.234 :443', angle: 1.05, radius: 0.66, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Gateway TCP :443' },
        { id: 'ds_med', label: 'Discord Media', sub: '162.159.129.235 :443', angle: 1.75, radius: 0.76, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Media TCP :443' },
        { id: 'ds_api', label: 'Discord API', sub: '162.159.128.233 :443', angle: 2.40, radius: 0.62, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'API TCP :443' },
        { id: 'ds_udp', label: 'Voice UDP', sub: 'Ports 50000–50010', angle: 3.05, radius: 0.82, status: 'idle', ping: null, details: 'Голосовой шлюз UDP' }
    ];
}

function updateRadarNodesFromReport(report) {
    if (!report) return;
    if (!diagRadarNodes.length) buildDefaultRadarNodes();

    for (let dcId = 1; dcId <= 5; dcId++) {
        const node = diagRadarNodes.find(n => n.id === 'dc' + dcId);
        if (!node) continue;

        const ports = (report.dcResults || []).filter(r => r.dcId === dcId);
        const okPorts = ports.filter(p => p.ok);

        if (ports.length === 0) node.status = 'idle';
        else if (okPorts.length === ports.length) node.status = 'ok';
        else if (okPorts.length > 0) node.status = 'warn';
        else node.status = 'fail';

        const lats = okPorts.filter(p => p.latencyMs != null).map(p => p.latencyMs);
        node.ping = lats.length > 0 ? Math.round(Math.min(...lats)) : null;
        node.details = ports.map(p => `:${p.port} → ${p.ok ? Math.round(p.latencyMs) + ' мс ✓' : 'Блок ✕'}`).join('\n');
    }

    const dsMap = { 'Gateway': 'ds_gw', 'Media': 'ds_med', 'API': 'ds_api' };
    (report.discordPing || []).forEach(d => {
        const node = diagRadarNodes.find(n => n.id === dsMap[d.label]);
        if (node) {
            node.status = d.ok ? (d.latencyMs > 180 ? 'warn' : 'ok') : 'fail';
            node.ping = d.ok && d.latencyMs != null ? Math.round(d.latencyMs) : null;
            node.details = `${d.ip}:443\nСтатус: ${d.ok ? node.ping + ' мс ✓' : 'Недоступен ✕'}`;
        }
    });

    const udpNode = diagRadarNodes.find(n => n.id === 'ds_udp');
    if (udpNode && report.udpResult) {
        const openCount = (report.udpResult.openPorts || []).length;
        const totalCount = (report.udpResult.testedPorts || []).length || 5;
        udpNode.status = !report.udpResult.blocked ? 'ok' : (report.appStatus?.zapretRunning ? 'warn' : 'fail');
        udpNode.ping = null;
        udpNode.details = `Открыто UDP: ${openCount}/${totalCount}\n${!report.udpResult.blocked ? 'Голос доступен ✓' : (report.appStatus?.zapretRunning ? 'Обход через Zapret ✓' : 'UDP заблокирован ✕')}`;
    }
}

function onDiagRadarMouseMove(e) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('diag-radar-canvas');
    const tooltip = document.getElementById('diag-radar-tooltip');
    if (!canvas || !tooltip) return;

    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mx = e.clientX - rect.left;
    const my = e.clientY - rect.top;

    let found = null;
    for (const n of diagRadarNodes) {
        if (n._px != null && Math.hypot(mx - n._px, my - n._py) < 22) {
            found = n;
            break;
        }
    }

    diagHoveredNode = found;
    if (found) {
        tooltip.classList.remove('hidden');
        tooltip.innerHTML = `
            <div class="font-bold text-brand mb-0.5">${found.label}</div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-[#888888] mb-1">${found.sub}</div>
            <div class="whitespace-pre-line text-[11px] text-[#E0E0E0]">${found.details}</div>
        `;
        const leftPx = Math.min(rect.width - 185, Math.max(8, mx + 14));
        const topPx = Math.min(rect.height - 95, Math.max(8, my - 10));
        tooltip.style.left = `${leftPx}px`;
        tooltip.style.top = `${topPx}px`;
    } else {
        tooltip.classList.add('hidden');
    }
}

function startDiagRadarLoop() {
    if (diagRadarAnimId) return;
    diagRadarLastTime = performance.now();

    function frame(now) {
        const dt = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0.001, (now - diagRadarLastTime) / 1000));
        diagRadarLastTime = now;
        try { drawDiagRadarFrame(dt, now); } catch (e) { }
        diagRadarAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }
    diagRadarAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}

function drawDiagRadarFrame(dt = 0.016, now = performance.now()) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('diag-radar-canvas');
    if (!canvas || canvas.offsetParent === null) return;

    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const w = rect.width;
    const h = rect.height;
    if (w < 20 || h < 20) return;

    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const targetW = Math.round(w * dpr);
    const targetH = Math.round(h * dpr);
    if (canvas.width !== targetW || canvas.height !== targetH) {
        canvas.width = targetW;
        canvas.height = targetH;
    }

    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.save();
    ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);

    const cx = Math.floor(w / 2) + 0.5;
    const cy = Math.floor(h / 2 + 6) + 0.5;
    const maxR = Math.min(w * 0.42, h * 0.43);

    const accentHex = getComputedStyle(document.body).getPropertyValue('--accent-light').trim() || '#4CAF50';
    const isScanningNow = !document.getElementById('diag-progress-wrap')?.classList.contains('hidden');

    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.055)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    [0.35, 0.65, 0.95].forEach(rMult => {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, Math.round(maxR * rMult), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
    });

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx - maxR * 1.15, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(cx + maxR * 1.15, cy);
    ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - maxR * 0.98);
    ctx.lineTo(cx, cy + maxR * 0.98);
    ctx.stroke();

    const angularSpeed = isScanningNow ? 1.35 : 0.42;
    diagRadarAngle = (diagRadarAngle + angularSpeed * dt) % (Math.PI * 2);

    const sweepArc = 0.52;
    const slices = 6;
    ctx.save();
    for (let s = 0; s < slices; s++) {
        const a0 = diagRadarAngle - sweepArc + (s / slices) * sweepArc;
        const a1 = diagRadarAngle - sweepArc + ((s + 1) / slices) * sweepArc + 0.005;
        const alphaHex = isScanningNow
            ? Math.round(((s + 1) / slices) * 36).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')
            : Math.round(((s + 1) / slices) * 16).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, maxR * 0.98, a0, a1);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fillStyle = accentHex.startsWith('#') ? (accentHex + alphaHex) : `rgba(76, 175, 80, ${isScanningNow ? 0.12 : 0.05})`;
        ctx.fill();
    }

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(diagRadarAngle) * maxR * 0.98, cy + Math.sin(diagRadarAngle) * maxR * 0.98);
    ctx.strokeStyle = accentHex.startsWith('#') ? (accentHex + '66') : 'rgba(76, 175, 80, 0.4)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();

    const pulseTime = now * 0.00028;

    diagRadarNodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
        const nx = cx + Math.cos(node.angle) * (maxR * 1.75 * node.radius);
        const ny = cy + Math.sin(node.angle) * (maxR * 0.92 * node.radius);
        node._px = nx;
        node._py = ny;

        let col = '#44444A';
        if (node.status === 'ok') col = accentHex;
        else if (node.status === 'warn') col = '#F59E0B';
        else if (node.status === 'fail') col = '#D13438';
        else if (isScanningNow) col = '#3B82F6';

        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        if (node.status === 'fail') {
            ctx.setLineDash([4, 5]);
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(209, 52, 56, 0.35)';
        } else if (node.status === 'ok' || node.status === 'warn') {
            ctx.strokeStyle = col + '55';
        } else {
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)';
        }
        ctx.lineWidth = diagHoveredNode === node ? 2.0 : 1.2;
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
        ctx.lineTo(nx, ny);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();

        if (node.status === 'ok' || node.status === 'warn' || isScanningNow) {
            const p = (pulseTime + idx * 0.19) % 1;
            const px = cx + (nx - cx) * p;
            const py = cy + (ny - cy) * p;
            ctx.fillStyle = col;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(px, py, 2.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        }
    });

    ctx.fillStyle = '#141418';
    ctx.strokeStyle = accentHex;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, 18, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();

    ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
    ctx.font = '700 10px "Segoe UI", Consolas, monospace';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('YOU', cx, cy + 0.5);

    diagRadarNodes.forEach(node => {
        const nx = node._px;
        const ny = node._py;
        const isHov = diagHoveredNode === node;

        let col = '#52525B';
        let glow = 'transparent';
        if (node.status === 'ok') { col = accentHex; glow = accentHex + '44'; }
        else if (node.status === 'warn') { col = '#F59E0B'; glow = 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.30)'; }
        else if (node.status === 'fail') { col = '#D13438'; glow = 'rgba(209, 52, 56, 0.25)'; }
        else if (isScanningNow) { col = '#60A5FA'; glow = 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.28)'; }

        if (node.status === 'ok' || isHov) {
            ctx.fillStyle = glow;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(nx, ny, isHov ? 18 : 14, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
        }

        ctx.fillStyle = '#121216';
        ctx.strokeStyle = col;
        ctx.lineWidth = isHov ? 2.2 : 1.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(nx, ny, isHov ? 10.5 : 8.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(nx, ny, node.status === 'fail' ? 2.5 : 3.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.font = '600 11px "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillStyle = node.status === 'fail' ? '#999999' : '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillText(node.label, nx, ny - 16);

        if (node.ping != null) {
            ctx.font = '600 10px Consolas, monospace';
            ctx.fillStyle = col;
            ctx.fillText(`${node.ping} мс`, nx, ny + 18);
        } else if (node.status === 'fail') {
            ctx.font = '600 9.5px Consolas, monospace';
            ctx.fillStyle = '#F87171';
            ctx.fillText('LOSS', nx, ny + 18);
        }
    });

    ctx.restore();
}
